# stickreel: the whole thing in one file

Generated by `stick/tools/bundle.py`. Restore with `python3 tools/bundle.py --extract THIS_FILE OUTDIR`. Needs numpy, Pillow, ffmpeg and the font `fonts/Poppins-Bold.ttf` from the LegoReel repo (not bundled; any bold TTF works if you edit `engine.py`).

## Live reference (`python3 -m stickreel docs`)

``````text
STICKREEL: 2D stick-figure animation from a JSON scene. Everything is in seconds and pixels of the canvas
(default 576 x 1024, y points DOWN). You write keyframes; the engine does IK, grounding, easing, loops, effects.

SCENE = {"canvas":{...}, "poses":{}, "loops":{}, "camera":{...}, "actors":{...}, "objects":{...}, "plugins":[]}

KEYFRAMES (same for actors, objects and the camera)
  "keys": [ {"t":0, "x":100}, {"t":1.5, "x":400, "ease":"out"} ]
  A key sets values that ARRIVE at time t. Anything a key does not mention keeps its last value. "ease" shapes the
  segment that ENDS at that key. Eases: linear, in, out, inout, in2, out2, back, backin, elastic, bounce, step.
  Numbers, [x,y] lists and "#rrggbb" colours blend; text/bool/null switch when the key time is reached.

ACTOR (the stick figure, a small 3D rig). Angles in degrees:
  yaw: 0 = profile facing right, 90 = facing the camera, 180 = profile facing left, 270/-90 = back to camera.
       yaw tweens: 0 -> 180 turns through the FRONT, 0 -> -180 through the BACK. "face":"left"/"right" = yaw 180/0
       (add "ease":"step" to flip instantly).
  limb angle 0 = hanging down, 90 = pointing forward, 180 = straight up, -90 = backward.
  x (hip x), ground (floor y; the hip height is solved so the lowest foot touches it), lift (jump height)
  lean (torso, + forward), head (extra tilt), rot (whole body pitch about the hip), shadow (true/false)
  arm_r/arm_l = [swing, elbow_bend, out], leg_r/leg_l = [swing, knee_bend, out]  (r = the figure's right, near at yaw 0)
  "pose": "stand | relax | hands_up | hands_up_wide | cheer | wave | point | reach_forward | crouch | sit | slump | shrug | hold_overhead | fall | tired"  -> sets all limb angles at once; list any param next to it to override.
  FACE: "expr": "calm | happy | laugh | sad | angry | scared | surprised | smirk | cool | sleepy | wink | dead | cry | blank | love | smug" (doodled, wraps around the head, hidden from behind); "talk": true flaps the mouth.
        Add your own: top-level "faces": {"name": {"parts":[["dot",u,v,rx,ry],["line",[[u,v],..],w],...], "mouth":[...]}}
        (head units: u -1..1 across, v down, radius 1; see stickreel/faces.py).
  Reach (IK, screen space, works from any yaw): hand_r_at / hand_l_at / foot_r_at / foot_l_at = [x,y] | "@name" | "@actor.anchor";
  ..._rel = [dx,dy] from the hip (dx forward); null releases. grip_rel = [dx,dy] puts BOTH hands on one point (tool grip).
  anchors: head neck hip shoulder_r/l hand_r hand_l grip foot_r foot_l elbow_* knee_* tip (item tip)
  ITEMS: any object can be held. "prop": "objname", "prop_hand": "auto"|"r"|"l"|"grip", "prop_angle" (0 = forward,
  -90 = up, in facing space; the item's top/tip points that way). "prop_l"/"prop_l_angle" = a second item in the left hand.
  Item kinds made for this: "pick", "phone" (video: path, video_start: scene time it starts, video_speed, loop,
  screen_color; key "video" to swap what plays), plus rect, circle, sprite (PNG), text. Held objects are not drawn on their own.
  "anim": a loop name. Built in: walk, run, idle, wave, bounce, dance, chop. Loops play on top of (add) or instead of (replace) the keys, blend in over
  anim_blend seconds, and anim:null stops them. walk/run are distance-locked: feet do not slide whatever the x speed.
  "hits": [t,t,...] next to "anim":"chop" times each tool contact exactly; the loop is retimed to land on them.
  Hit times become events "actor.hit" that effects can subscribe to.
  tint: [r,g,b] multipliers or "#rrggbb" colours the figure (e.g. gold).

OBJECTS  {"kind": ..., props..., "keys": [...]}   kinds and defaults:
    rect: x=288, y=512, w=100, h=100, rot=0, fill='#ffffff', outline=None, ow=2, anchor='center'
    circle: x=288, y=512, r=40, fill='#ffffff', outline=None, ow=2
    ellipse: x=288, y=512, rx=50, ry=30, rot=0, fill='#ffffff', outline=None, ow=2
    line: x=0, y=0, x2=100, y2=100, color='#ffffff', width=3
    poly: points=[[0, 0], [50, 0], [25, 40]], x=0, y=0, rot=0, scale=1.0, fill='#ffffff', outline=None, ow=2
    text: text='TEXT', x=288, y=512, size=24, fill='#ffffff', outline='#000000', ow=2, track=0.0, width=None, weight=800, grad=None, shadow=None, align='center', reveal=None
    glow: x=288, y=512, r=60, color='#ffd27a', strength=0.8, power=1.5
    ring: x=288, y=512, r=50, w=3, color='#ffffff'
    rays: x=288, y=512, r0=20, r1=200, n=16, w=3, color='#ffffff', rot=0
    star: x=288, y=512, r=8, color='#ffffff', thin=0.18
    sprite: file=None, x=288, y=512, scale=1.0, rot=0
    pick: x=288, y=512, angle=-45, len=64
    phone: x=288, y=512, rot=-90, w=26, h=48, scale=1.0, color='#2a2a30', grip_at=0.35, video=None, video_start=0.0, video_speed=1.0, loop=True, screen_color='#101018', len=48
    burst: times=[], at=[288, 512], n=10, speed=[40, 140], angle=[-150, -30], gravity=420, life=0.5, size=[1.5, 3.5], color='#8a5a2a', seed=1
    pulse: times=[], at=[288, 512], r=[46, 16], color=['#ffd278', '#fffadc'], strength=[0.8, 0.95], dur=0.18
    art: name=None
  common: z=0, show=None, alpha=1.0, space='world', follow=None, offset=[0, 0], blend='normal', grow=None, blur=None
  follow:"actor.anchor" + offset:[dx,dy] pins an object to a point (labels over heads).
  space:"screen" ignores the camera (HUD, text, fades). blend:"add" for light. show:[t0,t1] visible window.
  grow: {"prop":"w","times":"you.hit","by":6,"dur":0.1} makes a prop step up on every hit.
  burst/pulse take "times": [..] or "actor.hit" and "at": [x,y] or "actor.tip" (position is read at each event time).
  held objects (named in an actor "prop") are drawn in the hand, not on their own.
CAMERA {"zoom":1,"cx":288,"cy":512, "keys":[...]}: zoom about the world point (cx,cy), which sits at screen centre.
CANVAS defaults {'w': 576, 'h': 1024, 'fps': 30, 'duration': 5.0, 'scale': 2.0, 'bg': '#000000', 'blur': 1, 'out': None, 'post': None}; blur=4 gives motion blur (4 sub-frames) for fast action.
PLUGINS: "plugins":["module"] imports python that registers artwork with @render.art("name"); use {"kind":"art","name":...}.

SOUND: scene "audio": {"master":0.9,"music":{"file":..,"gain":.25,"fade_out":1},"cues":[{"sfx":"thunk","times":"you.hit","gain":.8,"pitch_jitter":.06},
  {"sfx":"boom","t":8.0},{"sfx":"tick","times":{"from":9.4,"to":9.8,"every":0.034},"gain":.3},{"file":"clap.mp3","t":3}]}. Built-in sounds are synthesised
  (thunk whoosh dirt clink coins ding sparkle riser boom shimmer tick pop step thump), no files or licences. times: [s..] | "actor.hit" | "actor.step"
  (footfalls of walk/run) | {from,to,every}. `render` muxes the mix in automatically (--no-audio to skip).

COMMANDS
  python -m stickreel validate scene.json        errors come with did-you-mean hints
  python -m stickreel sheet scene.json --times 0,0.5,1 [--out sheet.png]      LOOK at frames before rendering video
  python -m stickreel compare scene.json --ref REFDIR --frames 1,60,120 [--crop x0,y0,x1,y1] [--zoom .5]   reference | mine | blend
  python -m stickreel poses [--out poses.png]    all named poses, then a turnaround and a face sheet next to it
  python -m stickreel sfx [name] [--pitch 1.2] [--out x.wav]    list or write the built-in synthesised sounds
  python -m stickreel audio scene.json --out mix.wav            mix just the sound (no video)
  python -m stickreel render scene.json --out out.mp4 [--start 0 --end 3] [--workers 4] [--png dir]
WORKFLOW: write the scene with generous gaps, `sheet` it, fix what looks wrong, then render. Author a hit loop once and time
it with "hits"; do not key every frame.
``````

<!-- FILE stick/README.md -->
``````markdown
# stickreel

A 2D stick-figure animation engine built for an AI agent to drive. You write a JSON scene (keyframes, named poses,
loops, items, faces, camera, effects); it renders an MP4. No Python needed to make a video.

    python3 -m stickreel docs                      the full, always-current reference (print this first)
    python3 -m stickreel validate scene.json       errors come with did-you-mean hints
    python3 -m stickreel sheet scene.json --times 0,1,2     contact sheet: LOOK before you render video
    python3 -m stickreel poses                     sheets: every named pose, a turnaround (poses_turn.png), every face (poses_faces.png)
    python3 -m stickreel compare scene.json --ref REFDIR --frames 1,60,120    reference | mine | blend
    python3 -m stickreel render scene.json --out out.mp4 --workers 4
    python3 -m stickreel sfx                       list the synthesised sounds;  audio scene.json --out mix.wav mixes only the sound
    python3 -m unittest discover tests             27 tests
    python3 tools/bundle.py                        the whole project in ONE file: stickreel_bundle.md (--extract FILE OUT to unpack)

Needs numpy, Pillow, ffmpeg. (No scipy/OpenCV: the old `stickreel/rotofit.py` and `tools/` rotoscope code needs them and is kept only for reference.)

## The idea
One template figure with a handful of named numbers (limb angles, lean, yaw, expression). You key those numbers over
time. The engine does the rest: 2-bone IK so hands can grab things, automatic grounding so feet never float or sink,
easing, motion blur, distance-locked walk cycles, hit-timed tool loops, particles, light flashes.

- **3D turnaround**: `yaw` 0 = side, 90 = facing camera, 180 = other side, 270 = back. It tweens, so a turn is two keys.
- **Faces**: doodled expressions (`calm happy laugh sad angry scared surprised smirk cool sleepy wink dead cry blank love smug`)
  that wrap around the head, vanish from behind, and flap the mouth with `"talk": true`. Add your own in JSON.
- **Items**: any object can be held. Built in: `pick`, `phone` (screen plays a video; key `video` to swap it), plus
  rect/circle/sprite(PNG)/text. Two-handed grips and a second item in the other hand are supported.
- **Loops**: `walk`, `run`, `idle`, `wave`, `bounce`, `dance`, `chop`. Give a loop `"hits": [times]` and it retimes so
  each contact lands exactly on those times; `actor.hit` events then drive flashes, dust, and `grow` (e.g. a tunnel
  that gets longer on every strike).

## Sound
Scenes can carry an `"audio"` section: cues on fixed times or on events (`"you.hit"`, `"you.step"` for footfalls, a `{from,to,every}`
range for typing ticks), pitch jitter, pan, a music bed with fade-out. Built-in sounds are synthesised in `stickreel/sfx.py`
(thunk whoosh dirt clink coins ding sparkle riser boom shimmer tick pop step thump): no files, no downloads, no licences.
`python3 -m stickreel sfx` lists them, `sfx NAME --out x.wav` writes one, `audio scene.json --out mix.wav` mixes only the sound,
and `render` muxes it in automatically. Your own files work too (`{"file": "x.mp3", "t": 3}`). The synthesised sounds were
checked by spectrum and level only, nobody has listened yet: audition `sfx NAME` before trusting one.

## Polish switches
`canvas.out: [1080, 1920]` renders the 576x1024 authoring space at phone resolution; `canvas.post: {"vignette": 0.15, "grain": 0.02}`;
`canvas.blur: 3` motion blur; `camera.shake: {"times": "you.hit", "amp": 2, "dur": 0.15}`; faces blink by themselves
(`"blink": false` to stop); `"expr": null` hides the face (the dig-skit figures have none); actor `shadow`.

## Rules an agent must know (each one cost real time)
1. A key sets values that ARRIVE at its time; anything unmentioned holds. A prop first mentioned in a later key starts
   from its default at the previous key (it does not creep from t=0 or hold its final value backwards).
2. Reach targets (`hand_r_at`...) switch their own weight on when set and off with `null`. A hand target releases a
   two-handed `grip_rel` automatically.
3. Look at `sheet`/`compare` images, never trust numbers. Bad IK, wrong depth order and off-screen art only show up in pictures.
4. Artwork drawn for the unzoomed frame must extend past the frame edges if the camera will zoom or pan (the dig
   skit's wall and ground needed extending: black bars otherwise).
5. Time loops from events, do not key every frame: list the contact times in `hits`.

## Recreating a reference video (what worked)
Extract frames (`ffmpeg -i ref.mp4 ref/f%03d.png`), read contact sheets to find beats, write the generator script
(`scenes/dig_skit.py` is the worked example), `compare` against the reference at the beats, fix the biggest gap first.
`scenes/dig_skit.py` took the full 11.3 s reference (digging, shaft, drop, joint chop, flash, gold zoom, typed end text).

## Layout
`stickreel/`: `scene.py` (load/validate/evaluate), `skeleton.py` (3D rig + IK), `figure3d.py` (draw + faces),
`faces.py`, `poses.py` (poses and loops), `track.py` + `ease.py` (keyframes), `items.py` (phone/video), `render.py`,
`cli.py`, `art_dig.py` + `dig_bg.py` (artwork for the dig skit). `scenes/` worked example. `tests/` unit tests.

## Claude Code skill and Notion directory
`/stickmotion` (`.claude/skills/stickmotion/SKILL.md`, about 15 lines; `/stickmation` is an alias) only points at the Notion Brain: hub row
"Spec - Stickmation hub (start here)" with child pages (reference, code map, SFX catalog, scene recipes, gotchas, skill text).
Every session follows `docs/BRAIN_SYSTEM_PROMPT.md` (also loaded by the root `CLAUDE.md`).

## Media in this folder
`media/dig_skit.mp4` is the full 11.3 s recreation with sound (the render output folder `out/` is gitignored, so the keepers live here).
`media/all_frames.png` is a master image of all 339 frames of that video (26 per row, frame number every 10).
``````


<!-- FILE stick/NOTES.md -->
``````markdown
# stick: 2D stick-figure animation (side project of LegoReel)

Date: 2026-11-21. Goal from the owner: stop using the 3D Lego renderer, learn a 2D stick-figure rendering method by
recreating reference stick-figure videos 1:1. First reference: the "dig skit" by @cubemonsters (two stick figures,
YOU and FRIEND, dig tunnels toward a gold wall, a token, a zoomed gold finale). The reference video and its frames are
NOT in this repo (someone else's work). Do not post a recreation as is: it copies their format and share-bait text.

## What exists (all pure Python, Pillow + numpy + OpenCV + scipy)
- `stickreel/engine.py`: camera (zoom/pan), drawing helpers (poly, ellipse, glow, star, vertical gradient, text with
  tracking/outline/gradient). Authoring space is 576x1024; canvas is 2x, final downscale to 576x1024.
- `stickreel/figure.py`: the template figure. Points (head, neck, hip, 2 hands, 2 feet), two-bone IK for arms and
  legs with cartoon stretch and a bend side (+1/-1), capsule limbs with outline and edge shading, pickaxe prop.
- `stickreel/chop.py`: **the hand-keyed chop cycle**. Both hands on ONE grip point, the pick is a rigid stick that
  swings around the grip. 11 keys (wind-up, snap, contact, hold, pull, recoil, raise, high, overhead, back), period
  22 frames, periodic PCHIP interpolation. This is the method that finally looked right.
- `stickreel/dig_bg.py`: sky, sun rays, dirt, tunnels (jagged edge), gold wall, sign, token, labels, hook text.
- `stickreel/rotofit.py`: rotoscope fitter (silhouette IoU + chamfer, coordinate descent). Kept for reference.
- `scene_dig.py`: renders frames 1-152 (first ~5 s) with motion blur (`BLUR=4` env var = 4 sub-frames).
  Needs the reference frames in `A_all/fNNN.png` (not included) for tunnel growth and the strike-flash brightness.
  Contact frames (strike timing) are hard-coded: YOU 9, 30, 54, 84, 106, 127; FRIEND 13 + 18k.
- `tools/`: the rotoscope pipeline and helpers. Run from a folder that has `A_all/` (frames extracted with ffmpeg).
- `data/`: tunnel growth measurements, last fit (used for hip x drift only), last pick angles (no longer used).

## What I did (in order) and what happened
1. Built the engine, background and figure rig. First frame matched closely.
2. Tried to rotoscope the whole video: per-frame optimisation of 12 rig parameters against the white silhouette.
   - IoU-only loss drifted; chamfer + IoU with restarts worked on early frames.
   - Coordinate descent could not move the arms far; added polar enumeration of hand/foot targets (`refit.py`).
   - The optimiser CHEATED: it folded arms to zero length at the shoulder, because that looked fine in a skeleton
     overlay. Only rendering the real figure exposed it. Fixed with a minimum reach penalty in `Fitter.loss`.
   - The TikTok watermark is a don't-care box that hides the friend's torso and legs, so his fit was junk.
     Frozen planted feet and an upright torso as a workaround (`refit_friend.py`).
   - Pick angle: template scoring + Viterbi flattened the swing; metal-blob centroid near the grip (`pickfit3.py`)
     was better but still unreliable after the first cycle.
   - Result: silhouette IoU about 0.36-0.40. Not 1:1. Arms and pick jittered; the owner said so.
3. Owner's ideas that fixed it: (a) 10 fps keys then interpolate with motion blur, (b) one template figure, both
   hands on a single point on the pickaxe, code the key frames by hand. Done in `chop.py`.
   Looking at a large grid-annotated sheet of one real cycle (frames 26-50) was what made the keys right.
4. Timing comes from the original's strike flashes (bright pixels at the tunnel end): clean for FRIEND (every 18
   frames), irregular for YOU (21, 24, 30 ... frames apart; the last few are guesses).

## What I did NOT do
- Only frames 1-152 (0-5 s). NOT built: dig-down shaft growth with the figures, the drop-in and meeting, "ONE MORE
  GAME." beat (text drawn but figures wrong), the zoom/gold finale (rings, rays, tinted figures), text, fade.
- Not a measured 1:1: after the hand-keyed version I only compared by eye. The 0.36 IoU was for the old fit version.
- Strike flashes and tunnel growth are read from the reference frames, not authored. Stone chips/dust not built.
- Proportions differ a little (my legs look longer, head smaller). The friend's forward steps (about 4.2-4.9 s) are
  not reproduced (his hip x comes from the old noisy fit, smoothed).
- Font: Montserrat stand-in, not the original techno font.
- No second stick-figure reference recreated yet. TikTok cannot be fetched from the shell; the owner must attach
  videos. Only the cubemonsters video of the three attachments was a stick figure.

## Lessons
- Look at the actual rendered figure, not a skeleton overlay, before trusting a fit.
- For loops, author a template with a few keys and time it from events in the reference; do not fit every frame.
- Keep hands on one grip and make the prop rigid; that alone removes most arm/pick jitter.
- Render at 10 fps keys and interpolate; add motion blur by averaging sub-frames of the figure layer.
- Next: key the dig-down (pick pointing down, shaft growing) and the finale the same way, then a second reference.


## Update 2026-11-21 (later): general engine
The dig-skit-specific scripts above were superseded by a general JSON-driven engine, see README.md. Highlights: 3D turnaround
(`yaw`), doodled faces, items incl. a phone with swappable video, loops with timed `hits`, effects tied to hits, `grow`,
typewriter text, `sheet` / `compare` commands, unit tests. `scenes/dig_skit.py` rebuilds the whole 11.3 s reference
(not only the first 5 s) from those features; it still reads `data/fit_all11.json` for the hip x drift of the first 5 s.
Open gaps vs the reference: font (Poppins stand-in), sky/cloud art, figure limb thickness and pick style, finale light beams,
coin pile and the reference's audio (not added). The reference watermark is intentionally not reproduced.
``````

<!-- FILE stick/scenes/dig_skit.py -->
``````python
"""Builds scenes/dig_skit.json: a recreation study of a stick-figure dig skit, made only from engine features.
Frame numbers are the reference's 1-based frames; T(n) turns them into seconds. Run: python3 scenes/dig_skit.py"""
import json
import os

import numpy as np

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
FIT = json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, "..", "data", "fit_all11.json")))


def T(n):
    return round((n - 1) / 30.0, 4)


def smooth(vals, k=9):
    v = np.array(vals, float)
    pad = np.pad(v, (k // 2, k // 2), mode="edge")
    return np.convolve(pad, np.ones(k) / k, mode="valid")


def hip_keys(key, step=6, last=152):
    xs = smooth([FIT[str(n)][key][0] for n in range(1, last + 1)], 9)
    return [{"t": T(n), "x": round(float(xs[n - 1]), 1), "ease": "linear"} for n in range(1, last + 1, step)]


YOU_HITS = [9, 30, 54, 84, 106, 127, 148]
FRIEND_HITS = [13 + 18 * k for k in range(8)]
DIG_HITS = [154, 166, 178, 189]
TOGETHER = [207, 222, 237]
GOLD = [0.95, 0.83, 0.62]

    ]),

def label(text, follow, off, keys=None, show=None, **kw):
    d = dict(kind="text", text=text, follow=follow, offset=off, size=14, fill="#fff6e2", outline="#462c18", ow=1.6, weight=800, z=30)
    d.update(kw)
    if keys:
        d["keys"] = keys
    if show:
        d["show"] = show
    return d


def ring(t0, dur=0.55, r0=20, r1=240, x=COIN[0], y=COIN[1], w=4.5):
    return dict(kind="ring", x=x, y=y, r=r0, w=w, color="#fff6cc", alpha=0, z=40, show=[t0, t0 + dur + 0.05],
                keys=[{"t": t0, "r": r0, "alpha": 0.95, "ease": "out"}, {"t": t0 + dur, "r": r1, "alpha": 0.0, "ease": "out"}])
``````

<!-- FILE stick/tools/bundle.py -->
``````python
"""Pack the whole stickreel project into ONE text file, or unpack it again.

  python3 tools/bundle.py                      writes stickreel_bundle.md (README, live docs, every source file, tests, scene, skill)
  python3 tools/bundle.py --extract FILE OUT   rebuilds the folder tree under OUT (then: cd OUT/stick && python3 -m stickreel docs)

Format: <!-- FILE path --> then a code fence of 6 backticks around the file. Plain text, so an agent can read it directly.
"""
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
STICK = os.path.dirname(HERE)
ROOT = os.path.dirname(STICK)
FENCE = "`" * 6

INCLUDE = [
    "CLAUDE.md", "docs/BRAIN_SYSTEM_PROMPT.md", ".claude/skills/stickmotion/SKILL.md", ".claude/skills/stickmation/SKILL.md",
    "stick/README.md", "stick/NOTES.md", "stick/data/fit_all11.json", "stick/scenes/dig_skit.py",
    "stick/tools/bundle.py",
]
SKIP_DIRS = {"__pycache__", "out"}


def collect():
    files = [f for f in INCLUDE if os.path.exists(os.path.join(ROOT, f))]
    for sub in ("stick/stickreel", "stick/tests"):
        for n in sorted(os.listdir(os.path.join(ROOT, sub))):
            if n.endswith(".py"):
                files.append(f"{sub}/{n}")
    return files


def make(out):
    docs = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "stickreel", "docs"], cwd=STICK, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    parts = ["# stickreel: the whole thing in one file\n",
             "Generated by `stick/tools/bundle.py`. Restore with `python3 tools/bundle.py --extract THIS_FILE OUTDIR`. "
             "Needs numpy, Pillow, ffmpeg and the font `fonts/Poppins-Bold.ttf` from the LegoReel repo (not bundled; any bold TTF works if you edit `engine.py`).\n",
             "## Live reference (`python3 -m stickreel docs`)\n", FENCE + "text\n" + docs.rstrip() + "\n" + FENCE + "\n"]
    for f in collect():
        text = open(os.path.join(ROOT, f), encoding="utf-8").read()
        assert FENCE not in text, f"{f} contains the fence"
        lang = {"py": "python", "md": "markdown", "json": "json"}.get(f.rsplit(".", 1)[-1], "")
        parts.append(f"\n<!-- FILE {f} -->\n{FENCE}{lang}\n{text.rstrip(chr(10))}\n{FENCE}\n")
    open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("\n".join(parts))
    print(f"wrote {out}: {os.path.getsize(out) / 1024:.0f} KB, {len(collect())} files")


def extract(src, dst):
    text = open(src, encoding="utf-8").read()
    n = 0
    for m in re.finditer(r"<!-- FILE (.+?) -->\n(`{6})\w*\n(.*?)\n\2\n", text, re.S):
        path = os.path.join(dst, m.group(1))
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
        open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(m.group(3) + "\n")
        n += 1
    print(f"extracted {n} files under {dst}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) >= 4 and sys.argv[1] == "--extract":
        extract(sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3])
    else:
        make(os.path.join(STICK, "stickreel_bundle.md"))
``````

<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/art_dig.py -->
``````python
"""Artwork for the dig-skit recreation (sky, dirt, tunnels, shaft, gold wall, token). Static art; animated by props.
Use: "plugins": ["stickreel.art_dig"] and {"kind":"art","name":"dig_tunnel","right":300,"keys":[...]}."""
from PIL import Image
from . import dig_bg as B
from .render import art
from .engine import poly, glow, circle


@art("dig_sky")
def _sky(img, cam, p, t):
    B.draw_sky(img, cam)


@art("dig_dirt")
def _dirt(img, cam, p, t):
    B.draw_dirt(img, cam)


@art("dig_tunnel")
def _tunnel(img, cam, p, t):
    B.draw_tunnel(img, dict(top=p["top"], bot=p["bot"], right=p["right"]), cam, p["orb_y"])


@art("dig_shaft")
def _shaft(img, cam, p, t):
    d = p["depth"]
    if d <= 1:
        return
    x0, x1, top = p["x0"], p["x1"], p["top"]
    pts = [(x0, top - 3), (x1, top - 3)]
    steps = max(2, int(d / 12))
    for i in range(steps + 1):
        pts.append((x1 + (4 if i % 2 else -3), top + d * i / steps))
    for i in range(steps, -1, -1):
        pts.append((x0 + (-4 if i % 2 else 3), top + d * i / steps))
    poly(img, pts, cam, fill=(112, 64, 28, 255), outline=(70, 38, 16, 255), width=2.4)


@art("dig_wall")
def _wall(img, cam, p, t):
    B.draw_wall(img, cam)


@art("dig_sign")
def _sign(img, cam, p, t):
    B.draw_sign(img, cam)


@art("dig_token")
def _token(img, cam, p, t):
    B.draw_token(img, (p["x"], p["y"]), p["r"], cam, p.get("glow", 1.0))


@art("dig_pile")
def _pile(img, cam, p, t):
    import random
    rnd = random.Random(4)
    for i in range(34):
        x = p["x"] + rnd.uniform(-52, 52)
        y = p["y"] - abs(rnd.uniform(0, 1)) ** 1.5 * 12 - (1 - abs(x - p["x"]) / 52) * 9
        circle(img, (x, y), rnd.uniform(5, 8), cam, fill=(246, 196, 40, 255), outline=(120, 74, 12, 255), width=1.2)
``````

``````python
"""Sound: cues from a scene's "audio" section are mixed into one stereo track and muxed into the MP4.

"audio": {"master": 0.9, "music": {"file": "bed.mp3", "gain": 0.25, "start": 0, "fade_out": 1.0},
          "cues": [ {"sfx": "thunk", "times": "you.hit", "gain": 0.8, "pitch_jitter": 0.06},
                    {"sfx": "boom", "t": 8.0, "gain": 1.0},
                    {"sfx": "tick", "times": {"from": 9.4, "to": 9.8, "every": 0.034}, "gain": 0.3},
                    {"file": "sfx/clap.mp3", "t": 3.0} ] }
cue keys: sfx | file, t | times, gain (1.0), pitch (1.0), pitch_jitter (0), pan (-1 left .. 1 right), delay (s, shifts every time), seed.
times: [seconds...] | "actor.hit" | "actor.step" (footfalls of walk/run loops) | {"from","to","every"}.
Built-in sounds are synthesised (stickreel/sfx.py, no files, no licences): python3 -m stickreel sfx
"""
import subprocess

import numpy as np

from .sfx import SR, SFX, render_sfx
from .track import SceneError

CUE_KEYS = {"sfx", "file", "t", "times", "gain", "pitch", "pitch_jitter", "pan", "delay", "seed"}
AUDIO_KEYS = {"master", "music", "cues"}


def validate(spec, suggest):
    bad = [k for k in spec if k not in AUDIO_KEYS]
    if bad:
        raise SceneError(f"audio: unknown key '{bad[0]}'.{suggest(bad[0], AUDIO_KEYS)} Allowed: {', '.join(sorted(AUDIO_KEYS))}")
    for i, c in enumerate(spec.get("cues", [])):
        w = f"audio cue {i}"
        bad = [k for k in c if k not in CUE_KEYS]
        if bad:
            raise SceneError(f"{w}: unknown key '{bad[0]}'.{suggest(bad[0], CUE_KEYS)} Allowed: {', '.join(sorted(CUE_KEYS))}")
        if ("sfx" in c) == ("file" in c):
            raise SceneError(f"{w}: give exactly one of 'sfx' (built-in sound) or 'file'")
        if "sfx" in c and c["sfx"] not in SFX:
            raise SceneError(f"{w}: unknown sfx '{c['sfx']}'.{suggest(c['sfx'], SFX)} Built in: {', '.join(SFX)}")
        if ("t" in c) == ("times" in c):
            raise SceneError(f"{w}: give exactly one of 't' (seconds) or 'times'")


_FILES = {}


def load_file(path):
    """decode any audio file to stereo float32 at SR via ffmpeg (cached)."""
    if path not in _FILES:
        p = subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-i", path, "-f", "f32le", "-ac", "2", "-ar", str(SR), "-"],
                           capture_output=True)
        if p.returncode != 0:
            raise SceneError(f"audio: cannot read '{path}': {p.stderr.decode()[:200]}")
        _FILES[path] = np.frombuffer(p.stdout, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1, 2).copy()
    return _FILES[path]


def _resolve_path(scene, p):
    import os
    return p if os.path.isabs(p) else os.path.join(scene.base_dir, p)


def _stereo(mono, pan):
    pan = max(-1.0, min(1.0, pan))
    ang = (pan + 1) * np.pi / 4
    return np.stack([mono * np.cos(ang), mono * np.sin(ang)], axis=1)


def mix(scene, start=0.0, end=None):
    """-> float32 (n, 2) array covering [start, end) seconds."""
    spec = scene.audio or {}
    end = scene.duration if end is None else end
    n = int(round((end - start) * SR))
    out = np.zeros((n, 2), dtype=np.float32)

    def add(samples, t, gain):
        i0 = int(round((t - start) * SR))
        if i0 >= n or i0 + len(samples) <= 0:
            return
        a0 = max(0, -i0)
        a1 = min(len(samples), n - i0)
        out[i0 + a0:i0 + a1] += samples[a0:a1] * gain

    for ci, c in enumerate(spec.get("cues", [])):
        times = [c["t"]] if "t" in c else scene.times(c["times"])
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(c.get("seed", 0)) * 1000 + ci)
        for k, t in enumerate(times):
            t = t + c.get("delay", 0.0)
            pitch = c.get("pitch", 1.0) * (1.0 + rng.uniform(-1, 1) * c.get("pitch_jitter", 0.0))
            gain = c.get("gain", 1.0) * (1.0 + rng.uniform(-0.08, 0.08))
            if "sfx" in c:
                mono = render_sfx(c["sfx"], pitch, seed=ci * 101 + k)
                st = _stereo(mono, c.get("pan", 0.0))
            else:
                st = load_file(_resolve_path(scene, c["file"]))
            add(st, t, gain)
    m = spec.get("music")
    if m:
        st = load_file(_resolve_path(scene, m["file"]))
        t0 = m.get("start", 0.0)
        seg = st[int(max(0.0, start - t0) * SR):]
        seg = seg[:n]
        g = np.full(len(seg), m.get("gain", 0.3), dtype=np.float32)
        fo = m.get("fade_out", 0.0)
        if fo > 0:
            nf = min(int(fo * SR), len(g))
            g[len(g) - nf:] *= np.linspace(1, 0, nf)
        out[:len(seg)] += seg * g[:, None]
    out *= spec.get("master", 0.9)
    peak = float(np.max(np.abs(out))) if n else 0.0
    if peak > 0.98:                                    # keep it out of clipping
        out *= 0.98 / peak
    return out


def write_wav(path, stereo):
    import wave
    pcm = (np.clip(stereo, -1, 1) * 32767).astype("<i2")
    with wave.open(path, "wb") as w:
        w.setnchannels(2)
        w.setsampwidth(2)
        w.setframerate(SR)
        w.writeframes(pcm.tobytes())


def mux(video_path, wav_path, out_path):
    r = subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-i", video_path, "-i", wav_path, "-map", "0:v", "-map", "1:a",
                        "-c:v", "copy", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "160k", "-shortest", "-movflags", "+faststart", out_path])
    if r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError("ffmpeg mux failed")
``````

``````python
"""Hand-keyed chop cycle for the stick-figure rig.

One template figure, both hands on a single grip point on the pick handle, the pick is a rigid stick that swings
about that grip. Every key is hand-authored: (time in frames, grip relative to hip, pick angle in degrees (0 = right,
-90 = straight up), torso lean in radians (+ = forward), head tilt, hip bob, feet).
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.interpolate import PchipInterpolator
from .figure import ik2

TL, HL = 58.0, 19.0
PERIOD = 22.0

#       t    grip_rel_hip   pick   lean  tilt  hip_dy  footA     footB
YOU_KEYS = [
    (0.0,  (-45, -86), -148, 0.06, 0.00,  0, (16, 82), (-20, 81)),   # BACK: pick behind the head, body upright
    (2.0,  (-40, -90), -132, 0.04, 0.00, -1, (16, 82), (-20, 81)),   # wind-up settle
    (3.0,  (  8, -80),  -70, 0.28, -0.10, 1, (20, 82), (-24, 81)),   # snap forward (fast)
    (4.0,  ( 76, -38),    8, 0.52, -0.22, 4, (26, 82), (-30, 80)),   # CONTACT
    (7.0,  ( 80, -36),    6, 0.54, -0.24, 5, (26, 82), (-30, 80)),   # hold, flash
    (9.5,  ( 78, -38),    0, 0.52, -0.24, 4, (26, 82), (-30, 80)),   # pull
    (11.5, ( 68, -52),  -14, 0.46, -0.30, 3, (24, 82), (-27, 81)),   # recoil
    (14.0, ( 46, -72),  -30, 0.36, -0.30, 1, (22, 82), (-22, 81)),   # raise
    (17.0, ( 44, -86),  -56, 0.24, -0.20, -1, (20, 82), (-18, 81)),  # high
    (19.5, ( 12, -98),  -92, 0.12, -0.05, -2, (18, 82), (-17, 81)),  # overhead
    (22.0, (-45, -86), -148, 0.06, 0.00,  0, (16, 82), (-20, 81)),   # BACK again
]


def _interp(keys, t, period):
    """periodic PCHIP over the key table; returns dict of values at time t (frames)."""
    ts = np.array([k[0] for k in keys])
    cols = {
        "gx": [k[1][0] for k in keys], "gy": [k[1][1] for k in keys], "pick": [k[2] for k in keys],
        "lean": [k[3] for k in keys], "tilt": [k[4] for k in keys], "dy": [k[5] for k in keys],
        "ax": [k[6][0] for k in keys], "ay": [k[6][1] for k in keys], "bx": [k[7][0] for k in keys], "by": [k[7][1] for k in keys],
    }
    t = t % period
    out = {}
    for name, vals in cols.items():
        v = np.array(vals, float)
        T = np.concatenate([ts[:-1] - period, ts, ts[1:] + period])
        V = np.concatenate([v[:-1], v, v[1:]])
        out[name] = float(PchipInterpolator(T, V)(t))
    return out


def pose_from(v, hip_x, hip_y, speed_scale=1.0):
    hip = (hip_x, hip_y + v["dy"])
    a = v["lean"]
    neck = (hip[0] + TL * math.sin(a), hip[1] - TL * math.cos(a))
    hd = a + v["tilt"]
    head = (neck[0] + HL * math.sin(hd), neck[1] - HL * math.cos(hd))
    grip = (hip[0] + v["gx"], hip[1] + v["gy"])
    feet = [(hip[0] + v["ax"], hip[1] + v["ay"]), (hip[0] + v["bx"], hip[1] + v["by"])]
    return dict(head=head, neck=neck, hip=hip, hands=[grip, grip], feet=feet, eb=[1, 1], kb=[-1, -1],
                pick=v["pick"], pick_len=64)


def chop_pose(keys, t, hip_x, hip_y, period=PERIOD):
    return pose_from(_interp(keys, t, period), hip_x, hip_y)
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/cli.py -->
``````python
import argparse
import json
import sys

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

from .scene import Scene, KINDS, COMMON, CANVAS_DEFAULT
from .skeleton import DEFAULTS
from .poses import POSES, LOOPS
from .track import SceneError
from .ease import EASES

DOCS = """\
STICKREEL: 2D stick-figure animation from a JSON scene. Everything is in seconds and pixels of the canvas
(default 576 x 1024, y points DOWN). You write keyframes; the engine does IK, grounding, easing, loops, effects.

SCENE = {"canvas":{...}, "poses":{}, "loops":{}, "camera":{...}, "actors":{...}, "objects":{...}, "plugins":[]}

KEYFRAMES (same for actors, objects and the camera)
  "keys": [ {"t":0, "x":100}, {"t":1.5, "x":400, "ease":"out"} ]
  A key sets values that ARRIVE at time t. Anything a key does not mention keeps its last value. "ease" shapes the
  segment that ENDS at that key. Eases: {eases}.
  Numbers, [x,y] lists and "#rrggbb" colours blend; text/bool/null switch when the key time is reached.

ACTOR (the stick figure, a small 3D rig). Angles in degrees:
  yaw: 0 = profile facing right, 90 = facing the camera, 180 = profile facing left, 270/-90 = back to camera.
       yaw tweens: 0 -> 180 turns through the FRONT, 0 -> -180 through the BACK. "face":"left"/"right" = yaw 180/0
       (add "ease":"step" to flip instantly).
  limb angle 0 = hanging down, 90 = pointing forward, 180 = straight up, -90 = backward.
  x (hip x), ground (floor y; the hip height is solved so the lowest foot touches it), lift (jump height)
  lean (torso, + forward), head (extra tilt), rot (whole body pitch about the hip), shadow (true/false)
  arm_r/arm_l = [swing, elbow_bend, out], leg_r/leg_l = [swing, knee_bend, out]  (r = the figure's right, near at yaw 0)
  "pose": "{poses}"  -> sets all limb angles at once; list any param next to it to override.
  FACE: "expr": "{faces}" (doodled, wraps around the head, hidden from behind); "talk": true flaps the mouth.
        Add your own: top-level "faces": {{"name": {{"parts":[["dot",u,v,rx,ry],["line",[[u,v],..],w],...], "mouth":[...]}}}}
        (head units: u -1..1 across, v down, radius 1; see stickreel/faces.py).
  Reach (IK, screen space, works from any yaw): hand_r_at / hand_l_at / foot_r_at / foot_l_at = [x,y] | "@name" | "@actor.anchor";
  ..._rel = [dx,dy] from the hip (dx forward); null releases. grip_rel = [dx,dy] puts BOTH hands on one point (tool grip).
  anchors: head neck hip shoulder_r/l hand_r hand_l grip foot_r foot_l elbow_* knee_* tip (item tip)
  ITEMS: any object can be held. "prop": "objname", "prop_hand": "auto"|"r"|"l"|"grip", "prop_angle" (0 = forward,
  -90 = up, in facing space; the item's top/tip points that way). "prop_l"/"prop_l_angle" = a second item in the left hand.
  Item kinds made for this: "pick", "phone" (video: path, video_start: scene time it starts, video_speed, loop,
  screen_color; key "video" to swap what plays), plus rect, circle, sprite (PNG), text. Held objects are not drawn on their own.
  "anim": a loop name. Built in: {loops}. Loops play on top of (add) or instead of (replace) the keys, blend in over
  anim_blend seconds, and anim:null stops them. walk/run are distance-locked: feet do not slide whatever the x speed.
  "hits": [t,t,...] next to "anim":"chop" times each tool contact exactly; the loop is retimed to land on them.
  Hit times become events "actor.hit" that effects can subscribe to.
  tint: [r,g,b] multipliers or "#rrggbb" colours the figure (e.g. gold).

OBJECTS  {{"kind": ..., props..., "keys": [...]}}   kinds and defaults:
{kinds}
  common: {common}
  follow:"actor.anchor" + offset:[dx,dy] pins an object to a point (labels over heads).
  space:"screen" ignores the camera (HUD, text, fades). blend:"add" for light. show:[t0,t1] visible window.
  grow: {{"prop":"w","times":"you.hit","by":6,"dur":0.1}} makes a prop step up on every hit.
  burst/pulse take "times": [..] or "actor.hit" and "at": [x,y] or "actor.tip" (position is read at each event time).
  held objects (named in an actor "prop") are drawn in the hand, not on their own.
CAMERA {{"zoom":1,"cx":288,"cy":512, "keys":[...]}}: zoom about the world point (cx,cy), which sits at screen centre.
CANVAS defaults {canvas}; blur=4 gives motion blur (4 sub-frames) for fast action.
PLUGINS: "plugins":["module"] imports python that registers artwork with @render.art("name"); use {{"kind":"art","name":...}}.

SOUND: scene "audio": {"master":0.9,"music":{"file":..,"gain":.25,"fade_out":1},"cues":[{"sfx":"thunk","times":"you.hit","gain":.8,"pitch_jitter":.06},
  {"sfx":"boom","t":8.0},{"sfx":"tick","times":{"from":9.4,"to":9.8,"every":0.034},"gain":.3},{"file":"clap.mp3","t":3}]}. Built-in sounds are synthesised
  (thunk whoosh dirt clink coins ding sparkle riser boom shimmer tick pop step thump), no files or licences. times: [s..] | "actor.hit" | "actor.step"
  (footfalls of walk/run) | {from,to,every}. `render` muxes the mix in automatically (--no-audio to skip).

COMMANDS
  python -m stickreel validate scene.json        errors come with did-you-mean hints
  python -m stickreel sheet scene.json --times 0,0.5,1 [--out sheet.png]      LOOK at frames before rendering video
  python -m stickreel compare scene.json --ref REFDIR --frames 1,60,120 [--crop x0,y0,x1,y1] [--zoom .5]   reference | mine | blend
  python -m stickreel poses [--out poses.png]    all named poses, then a turnaround and a face sheet next to it
  python -m stickreel sfx [name] [--pitch 1.2] [--out x.wav]    list or write the built-in synthesised sounds
  python -m stickreel audio scene.json --out mix.wav            mix just the sound (no video)
  python -m stickreel render scene.json --out out.mp4 [--start 0 --end 3] [--workers 4] [--png dir]
WORKFLOW: write the scene with generous gaps, `sheet` it, fix what looks wrong, then render. Author a hit loop once and time
it with "hits"; do not key every frame.
"""


def docs_text():
    from .faces import FACES
    kinds = "\n".join(f"    {k}: " + ", ".join(f"{p}={v!r}" for p, v in d.items()) for k, d in KINDS.items())
    vals = dict(eases=", ".join(EASES), poses=" | ".join(POSES), faces=" | ".join(FACES), loops=", ".join(LOOPS),
                kinds=kinds, common=", ".join(f"{k}={v!r}" for k, v in COMMON.items()), canvas=str(CANVAS_DEFAULT))
    out = DOCS
    for k, v in vals.items():
        out = out.replace("{" + k + "}", v)
    return out.replace("{{", "{").replace("}}", "}")


def sheet(scene, times, out, cols=None):
    from .render import frame_rgb
    c = scene.canvas
    cols = cols or min(len(times), 6)
    rows = (len(times) + cols - 1) // cols
    W = 288
    H = int(W * c["h"] / c["w"])
    sh = Image.new("RGB", (W * cols, H * rows), "black")
    for i, t in enumerate(times):
        im = frame_rgb(scene, int(round(t * scene.fps))).resize((W, H), Image.LANCZOS)
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
        d.rectangle([0, 0, 52, 14], fill="black")
        d.text((3, 2), f"{t:.2f}s", fill="yellow")
        sh.paste(im, ((i % cols) * W, (i // cols) * H))
    sh.save(out)
    print("wrote", out)


def compare(scene, ref_dir, frames, out, crop=None, zoom=0.5, scale=1.0):
    """reference frame | my render | 50% blend. ref_dir holds f001.png... (ffmpeg -i ref.mp4 f%03d.png); frames are 1-based."""
    import os
    from .render import frame_rgb
    scene.canvas["scale"] = scale
    scene.canvas["blur"] = 1
    W, H = scene.canvas["w"], scene.canvas["h"]
    box = tuple(int(v) for v in crop.split(",")) if crop else (0, 0, W, H)
    cw, ch = box[2] - box[0], box[3] - box[1]
    rows = []
    for n in frames:
        ref = Image.open(os.path.join(ref_dir, f"f{n:03d}.png")).convert("RGB").resize((W, H))
        mine = frame_rgb(scene, n - 1)
        row = Image.new("RGB", (cw * 3, ch))
        for j, im in enumerate((ref, mine, Image.blend(ref, mine, 0.5))):
            row.paste(im.crop(box), (j * cw, 0))
        row = row.resize((int(cw * 3 * zoom), int(ch * zoom)))
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(row)
        d.rectangle([0, 0, 28, 12], fill="black")
        d.text((2, 1), str(n), fill="yellow")
        rows.append(row)
    sh = Image.new("RGB", (rows[0].width, rows[0].height * len(rows)))
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        sh.paste(r, (0, i * r.height))
    sh.save(out)
    print("wrote", out)


def _tile_sheet(tiles, out, cols=6, W=200, H=260):
    """tiles = [(label, actor_spec)] rendered one per cell."""
    from .render import frame_rgb
    rows = (len(tiles) + cols - 1) // cols
    sh = Image.new("RGB", (W * cols, H * rows), "white")
    base = {"canvas": {"w": W, "h": H, "scale": 1.5, "bg": "#e8e2d0"}}
    for i, (label, a) in enumerate(tiles):
        im = frame_rgb(Scene(dict(base, actors={"a": a})), 0)
        ImageDraw.Draw(im).text((4, 4), label, fill="black")
        sh.paste(im, ((i % cols) * W, (i // cols) * H))
    sh.save(out)
    print("wrote", out)


def poses_sheet(out):
    """poses.png (every named pose), poses_turn.png (one pose at 8 yaw angles), poses_faces.png (every expression, front view)."""
    from .faces import FACES
    _tile_sheet([(n, {"x": 100, "ground": 200, "keys": [{"t": 0, "pose": n}]}) for n in POSES], out)
    stem = out[:-4] if out.endswith(".png") else out
    _tile_sheet([(f"yaw {y}", {"x": 100, "ground": 200, "keys": [{"t": 0, "pose": "relax", "yaw": y, "expr": "happy"}]})
                 for y in (0, 45, 90, 135, 180, 225, 270, 315)], stem + "_turn.png", cols=8)
    _tile_sheet([(n, {"x": 100, "ground": 200, "keys": [{"t": 0, "pose": "relax", "yaw": 90, "expr": n}]}) for n in FACES], stem + "_faces.png", cols=8)
    print("poses:", ", ".join(POSES))


def main(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="stickreel")
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    sub.add_parser("docs")
    v = sub.add_parser("validate"); v.add_argument("scene")
    s = sub.add_parser("sheet"); s.add_argument("scene"); s.add_argument("--times", required=True); s.add_argument("--out", default="sheet.png"); s.add_argument("--cols", type=int)
    p = sub.add_parser("poses"); p.add_argument("--out", default="poses.png")
    c = sub.add_parser("compare"); c.add_argument("scene"); c.add_argument("--ref", required=True); c.add_argument("--frames", required=True)
    c.add_argument("--out", default="compare.png"); c.add_argument("--crop"); c.add_argument("--zoom", type=float, default=0.5)
    au = sub.add_parser("audio"); au.add_argument("scene"); au.add_argument("--out", default="mix.wav")
    sf = sub.add_parser("sfx"); sf.add_argument("name", nargs="?"); sf.add_argument("--pitch", type=float, default=1.0); sf.add_argument("--out")
    r = sub.add_parser("render"); r.add_argument("scene"); r.add_argument("--out"); r.add_argument("--start", type=float); r.add_argument("--end", type=float)
    r.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=2); r.add_argument("--no-audio", action="store_true"); r.add_argument("--png"); r.add_argument("--crf", type=int, default=18)
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        if a.cmd == "docs":
            print(docs_text())
        elif a.cmd == "validate":
            sc = Scene.load(a.scene)
            print(f"ok: {len(sc.actors)} actors, {len(sc.objects)} objects, {sc.duration}s @ {sc.fps} fps")
            for t in (0.0, sc.duration / 2, sc.duration - 1 / sc.fps):
                for ac in sc.actors.values():
                    ac.solve(t)
        elif a.cmd == "sheet":
            sc = Scene.load(a.scene)
            sheet(sc, [float(x) for x in a.times.split(",")], a.out, a.cols)
        elif a.cmd == "audio":
            from . import audio as A
            sc = Scene.load(a.scene)
            m = A.mix(sc)
            A.write_wav(a.out, m)
            print(f"wrote {a.out}: {len(m) / 44100:.2f}s, peak {abs(m).max():.2f}, {len(sc.audio.get('cues', [])) if sc.audio else 0} cue lines")
        elif a.cmd == "sfx":
            from .sfx import SFX, describe, render_sfx, write_wav
            if not a.name:
                for k, v in describe().items():
                    print(f"  {k:9s} {v}")
            else:
                write_wav(a.out or f"{a.name}.wav", render_sfx(a.name, a.pitch))
                print("wrote", a.out or f"{a.name}.wav")
        elif a.cmd == "compare":
            compare(Scene.load(a.scene), a.ref, [int(x) for x in a.frames.split(",")], a.out, a.crop, a.zoom)
        elif a.cmd == "poses":
            poses_sheet(a.out)
        elif a.cmd == "render":
            from .render import render_video
            render_video(a.scene, a.out, a.start, a.end, a.workers, a.png, a.crf, audio=not a.no_audio)
    except SceneError as e:
        print("scene error:", e, file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(2)
``````

``````python
"""Static artwork for the dig skit: sky, sun, dirt, tunnels, gold wall, sign. Everything in reference space."""
import math
import random
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFilter
from .engine import *

UP_TUNNEL = dict(top=390, bot=572, right=252)      # x grows to the right edge, left edge is off screen
LO_TUNNEL = dict(top=678, bot=862, right=342)
WALL_X0 = 458                                       # left edge of the gold wall (jagged)
WALL_Y0, WALL_Y1 = 368, 885
TOKEN_C = (510, 757)


def zigzag_x(base, y, y0, y1, amp=6.0, n=9):
    """jagged right-hand edge of a tunnel: x as a function of y."""
    u = (y - y0) / (y1 - y0)
    return base + (amp if int(u * n * 2) % 2 == 0 else -amp * 0.6)


def tunnel_poly(t, n=9):
    pts = [(-10, t["top"]), (t["right"] + 6, t["top"])]
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        y = t["top"] + (t["bot"] - t["top"]) * i / n
        pts.append((t["right"] + (6 if i % 2 == 0 else -3), y - (t["bot"] - t["top"]) / n * 0.5))
        pts.append((t["right"] + (-3 if i % 2 == 0 else 6), y))
    pts.append((-10, t["bot"]))
    return pts


def draw_sky(img, cam, sun_pulse=0.0):
    vgrad(img, 0, 290, [(0, "#443d6f"), (0.21, "#634a79"), (0.345, "#98648a"), (0.59, "#c5777e"), (0.71, "#dd9074"), (1.0, "#f5ac6c")], cam, x0=-400, x1=1000)
    # rays from the sun
    sun = (395, 178)
    rays = Image.new("RGBA", img.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(rays)
    sx, sy = cam.p(*sun)
    for i in range(14):
        a = math.radians(-180 + i * (180 / 13))
        w = math.radians(2.6 if i % 2 else 4.2)
        L = cam.k(460)
        P = [(sx, sy), (sx + math.cos(a - w) * L, sy + math.sin(a - w) * L), (sx + math.cos(a + w) * L, sy + math.sin(a + w) * L)]
        d.polygon(P, fill=(255, 235, 190, 34))
    hz = cam.p(0, 285)[1]
    rays.paste((0, 0, 0, 0), (0, int(hz), img.size[0], img.size[1]))
    img.alpha_composite(rays)
    glow(img, sun, 150, cam, (255, 214, 140), 0.55 + sun_pulse, 1.6)
    glow(img, sun, 62, cam, (255, 244, 205), 0.9, 1.2)
    circle(img, sun, 36, cam, fill=(255, 246, 214, 255))
    # clouds
    for cx, cy, w in ((92, 20, 62), (447, 38, 48), (240, 78, 36)):
        for dx, dy, rx, ry in ((0, 0, w, w * 0.26), (-w * 0.35, w * 0.08, w * 0.55, w * 0.2), (w * 0.4, w * 0.06, w * 0.5, w * 0.19)):
            ellipse(img, (cx + dx, cy + dy), rx, ry, cam, fill=(236, 140, 176, 255))
        ellipse(img, (cx, cy - w * 0.08), w * 0.7, w * 0.14, cam, fill=(246, 172, 196, 255))
    # hills
    pts = [(-400, 262), (0, 262), (40, 250), (90, 246), (150, 258), (220, 268), (300, 262), (380, 252), (450, 258), (520, 246), (576, 252), (1000, 256),
           (1000, 290), (-400, 290)]
    poly(img, pts, cam, fill=(176, 104, 158, 255))
    poly(img, [(-400, 276), (80, 268), (180, 280), (300, 276), (420, 280), (576, 270), (1000, 272), (1000, 290), (-400, 290)], cam,
         fill=(160, 90, 152, 255))
    # grass line
    poly(img, [(-400, 285), (1000, 285), (1000, 294), (-400, 294)], cam, fill=(98, 184, 62, 255))
    line(img, (-400, 285.5), (1000, 285.5), cam, (58, 110, 40, 255), 1.4, cap=False)
    line(img, (-400, 294), (1000, 294), cam, (92, 56, 20, 255), 1.4, cap=False)


def draw_dirt(img, cam):
    vgrad(img, 293, 1400, [(0, "#dcac5e"), (0.16, "#d2a053"), (0.5, "#bc8840"), (1.0, "#b3813a")], cam, x0=-400, x1=1000)
    rnd = random.Random(11)
    spots = [(35, 355, 11, 8), (272, 340, 26, 17), (330, 390, 13, 20), (433, 375, 13, 15), (24, 592, 10, 9),
             (305, 548, 25, 16), (130, 612, 12, 9), (410, 690, 9, 20), (432, 640, 18, 22), (52, 930, 20, 13),
             (130, 915, 14, 10), (250, 960, 18, 12), (350, 935, 16, 10), (475, 985, 20, 14), (360, 990, 12, 9),
             (115, 985, 15, 11), (205, 900, 11, 7), (540, 930, 12, 8), (60, 640, 11, 8), (180, 625, 10, 6)]
    for x, y, rx, ry in spots:
        ellipse(img, (x, y), rx, ry, cam, fill=(206, 138, 60, 255), outline=(190, 120, 46, 255), width=1.5, rot=0.35)
        ellipse(img, (x - rx * 0.1, y - ry * 0.15), rx * 0.62, ry * 0.5, cam, fill=(224, 158, 74, 255), rot=0.35)


def draw_tunnel(img, t, cam, orb_y):
    pts = tunnel_poly(t)
    # shadowed body, lighter toward the bottom
    mask = Image.new("L", img.size, 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(mask).polygon([cam.p(*p) for p in pts], fill=255)
    top_y, bot_y = cam.p(0, t["top"])[1], cam.p(0, t["bot"])[1]
    H = img.size[1]
    ts = np.clip((np.arange(H) - top_y) / max(1.0, bot_y - top_y), 0, 1)
    c0, c1 = np.array([118, 68, 28]), np.array([170, 104, 50])
    col = (c0[None, :] * (1 - ts[:, None]) + c1[None, :] * ts[:, None]).astype(np.uint8)
    arr = np.repeat(col[:, None, :], img.size[0], axis=1)
    img.paste(Image.fromarray(arr, "RGB").convert("RGBA"), (0, 0), mask)
    poly(img, pts, cam, outline=(70, 38, 16, 255), width=2.6)
    # dark top lip
    poly(img, [(-10, t["top"]), (t["right"] + 5, t["top"]), (t["right"] + 5, t["top"] + 9), (-10, t["top"] + 9)], cam,
         fill=(86, 46, 18, 120))
    glow(img, (53, orb_y), 38, cam, (255, 196, 110), 0.7, 1.4)
    glow(img, (53, orb_y), 15, cam, (255, 244, 206), 0.9, 1.0)


def coin_positions(seed=5):
    rnd = random.Random(seed)
    out = []
    for _ in range(150):
        x = rnd.uniform(WALL_X0 + 8, 700)
        y = rnd.uniform(WALL_Y0 + 6, WALL_Y1 - 6)
        # leave the token clear
        if math.hypot(x - TOKEN_C[0], y - TOKEN_C[1]) < 40:
            continue
        # keep inside the jagged left edge
        edge = WALL_X0 + 10 + 8 * math.sin(y * 0.21)
        if x < edge:
            continue
        out.append((x, y, rnd.choice([7.5, 8.5, 9.5, 10.5])))
    return out


COINS = coin_positions()
SPARKS = [(600, 450, 8), (640, 560, 7), (610, 700, 8), (650, 820, 7), (558, 408, 8), (488, 455, 7), (527, 543, 9), (515, 572, 7), (505, 672, 9), (470, 742, 8), (528, 790, 9),
          (466, 590, 6), (557, 705, 6), (500, 405, 6), (545, 860, 7)]


def draw_wall(img, cam):
    pts = [(WALL_X0 + 8 * math.sin(y * 0.21) + 6, y) for y in range(WALL_Y0, WALL_Y1 + 1, 9)]
    full = pts + [(720, WALL_Y1), (720, WALL_Y0)]
    poly(img, full, cam, fill=(66, 38, 16, 255), outline=(40, 22, 8, 255), width=2.2)
    glow(img, (500, 640), 160, cam, (90, 50, 14), 0.25, 1.0)
    for x, y, r in COINS:
        circle(img, (x, y), r, cam, fill=(246, 196, 40, 255), outline=(120, 74, 12, 255), width=1.4)
        circle(img, (x - r * 0.12, y - r * 0.12), r * 0.62, cam, fill=(255, 226, 112, 255))
        circle(img, (x, y), r * 0.3, cam, fill=(226, 160, 28, 255))
    for x, y, r in SPARKS:
        star(img, (x, y), r, cam)


def draw_token(img, c, r, cam, glow_k=1.0):
    glow(img, c, r * 2.6, cam, (255, 214, 110), 0.7 * glow_k, 1.4)
    circle(img, c, r, cam, fill=(250, 200, 48, 255), outline=(150, 96, 14, 255), width=2.6)
    circle(img, c, r * 0.84, cam, fill=(255, 224, 104, 255), outline=(206, 140, 24, 255), width=1.6)
    circle(img, c, r * 0.68, cam, fill=(222, 156, 30, 255))
    # skull and crossbones
    x, y = c
    line(img, (x - r * 0.4, y + r * 0.34), (x + r * 0.4, y - r * 0.05), cam, (255, 238, 170, 255), r * 0.1)
    line(img, (x + r * 0.4, y + r * 0.34), (x - r * 0.4, y - r * 0.05), cam, (255, 238, 170, 255), r * 0.1)
    circle(img, (x, y - r * 0.12), r * 0.3, cam, fill=(255, 238, 170, 255))
    circle(img, (x - r * 0.1, y - r * 0.13), r * 0.07, cam, fill=(150, 96, 14, 255))
    circle(img, (x + r * 0.1, y - r * 0.13), r * 0.07, cam, fill=(150, 96, 14, 255))


def draw_sign(img, cam, text_alpha=255):
    line(img, (472, 285), (472, 304), cam, (60, 36, 18, 255), 2.0, cap=False)
    line(img, (543, 285), (543, 304), cam, (60, 36, 18, 255), 2.0, cap=False)
    poly(img, [(446, 303), (572, 303), (572, 356), (446, 356)], cam, fill=(190, 122, 52, 255), outline=(60, 32, 12, 255), width=3.0)
    poly(img, [(452, 309), (566, 309), (566, 350), (452, 350)], cam, outline=(224, 160, 84, 255), width=1.6)
    text(img, "PIRATE", (508, 317), 19, cam, fill=(255, 244, 226, 255), outline=(60, 32, 12, 255), ow=1.6, weight=800, width=88)
    text(img, "TOKEN", (509, 337), 19, cam, fill=(255, 244, 226, 255), outline=(60, 32, 12, 255), ow=1.6, weight=800, width=82)


def draw_background(img, cam, token=True, labels_alpha=1.0):
    draw_sky(img, cam)
    draw_dirt(img, cam)
    draw_tunnel(img, UP_TUNNEL, cam, 428)
    draw_tunnel(img, LO_TUNNEL, cam, 715)
    draw_wall(img, cam)
    draw_sign(img, cam)
    if token:
        draw_token(img, TOKEN_C, 33, cam)


def draw_hook(img, cam):
    text(img, "THIS TIME IT ENDS", (288, 101), 24, cam, fill=(255, 250, 238, 255), outline=(76, 50, 40, 255), ow=2.2,
         weight=800, width=300)
    text(img, "DIFFERENTLY.", (289, 151), 40, cam, fill=(255, 255, 255, 255), outline=(78, 52, 38, 255), ow=3.0,
         weight=900, grad=("#fff6d8", "#e8c98a"), width=352)


def draw_label(img, name, c, cam, alpha=1.0):
    text(img, name, c, 14, cam, fill=(255, 246, 226, 255), outline=(70, 44, 24, 255), ow=1.6, weight=800,
         width={"FRIEND": 84, "YOU": 44}.get(name, 70), alpha=alpha)
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/ease.py -->
``````python
"""Easing functions (0..1 -> 0..1) and a small monotone cubic (PCHIP) for periodic loops. No scipy needed."""
import math
import numpy as np


def _c(u):
    return 0.0 if u < 0 else 1.0 if u > 1 else u


EASES = {
    "linear": lambda u: _c(u),
    "in": lambda u: _c(u) ** 3,
    "out": lambda u: 1 - (1 - _c(u)) ** 3,
    "inout": lambda u: (lambda u: u * u * (3 - 2 * u))(_c(u)),
    "in2": lambda u: _c(u) ** 2,
    "out2": lambda u: 1 - (1 - _c(u)) ** 2,
    "back": lambda u: (lambda u: 1 + 2.70158 * (u - 1) ** 3 + 1.70158 * (u - 1) ** 2)(_c(u)),     # overshoot, then settle
    "backin": lambda u: (lambda u: 2.70158 * u ** 3 - 1.70158 * u ** 2)(_c(u)),                    # pull back first
    "elastic": lambda u: 0.0 if u <= 0 else 1.0 if u >= 1 else 2 ** (-10 * u) * math.sin((u * 10 - 0.75) * (2 * math.pi / 3)) + 1,
    "bounce": lambda u: _bounce(_c(u)),
    "step": lambda u: 1.0 if u >= 1 else 0.0,      # value jumps when the key time is reached
}


def _bounce(u):
    n, d = 7.5625, 2.75
    if u < 1 / d:
        return n * u * u
    if u < 2 / d:
        u -= 1.5 / d
        return n * u * u + 0.75
    if u < 2.5 / d:
        u -= 2.25 / d
        return n * u * u + 0.9375
    u -= 2.625 / d
    return n * u * u + 0.984375


def ease(name, u):
    return EASES[name](u)


def _pchip_slopes(x, y):
    h = np.diff(x)
    d = np.diff(y) / h
    m = np.zeros_like(y)
    m[1:-1] = np.where(d[:-1] * d[1:] > 0, 2 * d[:-1] * d[1:] / (d[:-1] + d[1:] + 1e-12), 0.0)
    m[0] = d[0]
    m[-1] = d[-1]
    return m


def periodic_pchip(xs, ys, period, t):
    """monotone cubic through (xs, ys) repeating every `period`; xs in [0, period), ys same length."""
    xs = np.asarray(xs, float)
    ys = np.asarray(ys, float)
    X = np.concatenate([xs - period, xs, xs + period])
    Y = np.concatenate([ys, ys, ys])
    m = _pchip_slopes(X, Y)
    t = t % period
    i = int(np.searchsorted(X, t, side="right") - 1)
    i = min(max(i, 0), len(X) - 2)
    h = X[i + 1] - X[i]
    s = (t - X[i]) / h
    h00, h10, h01, h11 = 2 * s ** 3 - 3 * s ** 2 + 1, s ** 3 - 2 * s ** 2 + s, -2 * s ** 3 + 3 * s ** 2, s ** 3 - s ** 2
    return float(h00 * Y[i] + h10 * h * m[i] + h01 * Y[i + 1] + h11 * h * m[i + 1])
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/engine.py -->
``````python
"""stickreel engine: small 2D vector animation helpers on top of Pillow + numpy.

Everything is authored in "reference space" (576 x 1024, the size of the TikTok reference) and drawn through a
Cam that maps reference space to the render canvas. Render canvas = REF * S pixels (S=2 -> 1152x2048), and the
final frame is resized to 1080x1920 (or 576x1024 for side-by-side checks).
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFilter, ImageChops, ImageFont

REF_W, REF_H = 576, 1024
S = 2.0


class Cam:
    """zoom z about the reference point (cx, cy), which lands at (tx, ty) in reference space of the output."""

    def __init__(self, z=1.0, cx=288.0, cy=512.0, tx=288.0, ty=512.0):
        self.z, self.cx, self.cy, self.tx, self.ty = z, cx, cy, tx, ty

    def p(self, x, y):
        return ((self.tx + (x - self.cx) * self.z) * S, (self.ty + (y - self.cy) * self.z) * S)

    def k(self, w):
        return w * self.z * S


def lerp(a, b, u):
    return a + (b - a) * u


def smooth(u):
    u = min(1.0, max(0.0, u))
    return u * u * (3 - 2 * u)


def ease_out(u):
    u = min(1.0, max(0.0, u))
    return 1 - (1 - u) ** 3


def ease_in(u):
    u = min(1.0, max(0.0, u))
    return u ** 3


def hexc(h, a=255):
    h = h.lstrip("#")
    return (int(h[0:2], 16), int(h[2:4], 16), int(h[4:6], 16), a)


def new_canvas():
    return Image.new("RGBA", (int(REF_W * S), int(REF_H * S)), (0, 0, 0, 255))


def vgrad(img, y0, y1, stops, cam, x0=0, x1=REF_W):
    """vertical gradient over reference y0..y1 using stops [(pos 0..1, '#hex'), ...]."""
    ax, ay = cam.p(x0, y0)
    bx, by = cam.p(x1, y1)
    ay, by = int(round(ay)), int(round(by))
    ax, bx = int(round(ax)), int(round(bx))
    H = max(1, by - ay)
    W = max(1, bx - ax)
    pos = np.array([s[0] for s in stops])
    cols = np.array([hexc(s[1])[:3] for s in stops], dtype=np.float32)
    t = np.linspace(0, 1, H)
    col = np.stack([np.interp(t, pos, cols[:, i]) for i in range(3)], axis=1)
    arr = np.repeat(col[:, None, :], W, axis=1).astype(np.uint8)
    layer = Image.fromarray(arr, "RGB")
    # clip to canvas
    full = img.size
    px, py = ax, ay
    img.paste(layer, (px, py))


def _translucent(*cols):
    return any(c is not None and len(c) == 4 and c[3] < 255 for c in cols)


def _over(img, draw_fn):
    """run draw_fn(ImageDraw) on a transparent layer and alpha-composite it, so translucent colours blend
    (ImageDraw on an RGBA image replaces pixels instead of blending)."""
    layer = Image.new("RGBA", img.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw_fn(ImageDraw.Draw(layer, "RGBA"))
    img.alpha_composite(layer)


def poly(img, pts, cam, fill=None, outline=None, width=1.0, join=True):
    if _translucent(fill, outline):
        return _over(img, lambda d: _poly_d(d, pts, cam, fill, outline, width, join))
    return _poly_d(ImageDraw.Draw(img, "RGBA"), pts, cam, fill, outline, width, join)


def _poly_d(d, pts, cam, fill, outline, width, join):
    P = [cam.p(*q) for q in pts]
    if fill is not None:
        d.polygon(P, fill=fill)
    if outline is not None:
        w = max(1, int(round(cam.k(width))))
        d.line(P + ([P[0]] if join else []), fill=outline, width=w, joint="curve")


def circle(img, c, r, cam, fill=None, outline=None, width=1.0):
    if _translucent(fill, outline):
        return _over(img, lambda d: _circle_d(d, c, r, cam, fill, outline, width))
    return _circle_d(ImageDraw.Draw(img, "RGBA"), c, r, cam, fill, outline, width)


def _circle_d(d, c, r, cam, fill, outline, width):
    x, y = cam.p(*c)
    R = cam.k(r)
    d.ellipse([x - R, y - R, x + R, y + R], fill=fill, outline=outline, width=max(1, int(round(cam.k(width)))) if outline else 0)


def ellipse(img, c, rx, ry, cam, fill=None, outline=None, width=1.0, rot=0.0):
    if _translucent(fill, outline) and abs(rot) < 1e-6:
        return _over(img, lambda d: _ellipse_d(d, c, rx, ry, cam, fill, outline, width))
    if abs(rot) < 1e-6:
        return _ellipse_d(ImageDraw.Draw(img, "RGBA"), c, rx, ry, cam, fill, outline, width)
    if False:
        d = None
        pass
    else:
        pts = []
        for i in range(24):
            th = i / 24 * 2 * math.pi
            ex, ey = rx * math.cos(th), ry * math.sin(th)
            pts.append((c[0] + ex * math.cos(rot) - ey * math.sin(rot), c[1] + ex * math.sin(rot) + ey * math.cos(rot)))
        poly(img, pts, cam, fill, outline, width)


def _ellipse_d(d, c, rx, ry, cam, fill, outline, width):
    x, y = cam.p(*c)
    a, b = cam.k(rx), cam.k(ry)
    d.ellipse([x - a, y - b, x + a, y + b], fill=fill, outline=outline,
              width=max(1, int(round(cam.k(width)))) if outline else 0)


def line(img, a, b, cam, color, width, cap=True):
    if _translucent(color):
        return _over(img, lambda d: _line_d(d, a, b, cam, color, width, cap))
    return _line_d(ImageDraw.Draw(img, "RGBA"), a, b, cam, color, width, cap)


def _line_d(d, a, b, cam, color, width, cap):
    pa, pb = cam.p(*a), cam.p(*b)
    w = max(1, int(round(cam.k(width))))
    d.line([pa, pb], fill=color, width=w)
    if cap:
        r = w / 2
        for p in (pa, pb):
            d.ellipse([p[0] - r, p[1] - r, p[0] + r, p[1] + r], fill=color)


def glow(img, c, r, cam, color=(255, 240, 200), strength=1.0, power=2.0):
    """additive radial glow, radius r (ref px)."""
    x, y = cam.p(*c)
    R = cam.k(r)
    x0, y0 = int(max(0, x - R)), int(max(0, y - R))
    x1, y1 = int(min(img.size[0], x + R + 1)), int(min(img.size[1], y + R + 1))
    if x1 <= x0 or y1 <= y0:
        return
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[y0:y1, x0:x1]
    d = np.sqrt((xx - x) ** 2 + (yy - y) ** 2) / max(R, 1e-6)
    a = np.clip(1 - d, 0, 1) ** power * strength
    reg = np.asarray(img.crop((x0, y0, x1, y1)).convert("RGB")).astype(np.float32)
    col = np.array(color, dtype=np.float32)
    reg = reg + a[..., None] * col[None, None, :]
    out = Image.fromarray(np.clip(reg, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8), "RGB").convert("RGBA")
    img.paste(out, (x0, y0))


def star(img, c, r, cam, color=(255, 255, 255, 230), thin=0.18):
    x, y = c
    pts = []
    for i in range(8):
        a = i * math.pi / 4
        rr = r if i % 2 == 0 else r * thin
        pts.append((x + rr * math.cos(a - math.pi / 2), y + rr * math.sin(a - math.pi / 2)))
    poly(img, pts, cam, fill=color)


def tint(img, color, alpha):
    """overlay a flat colour on the whole frame (alpha 0..1)."""
    ov = Image.new("RGBA", img.size, (*color[:3], int(255 * alpha)))
    img.alpha_composite(ov)


import os as _os
FONT_BOLD = _os.path.join(_os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..", "fonts", "Poppins-Bold.ttf")


def font(size, weight=800):
    f = ImageFont.truetype(FONT_BOLD, int(max(6, size)))
    try:
        f.set_variation_by_axes([weight])
    except Exception:
        pass
    return f


def text(img, s, c, size, cam, fill=(255, 255, 255, 255), outline=(40, 25, 10, 255), ow=2.0, track=0.0,
         weight=800, grad=None, shadow=None, width=None, alpha=1.0, align="center"):
    """centered text at ref point c with letter spacing `track` (ref px). grad = (top_hex, bottom_hex).
    width = target total width in ref px (solves the letter spacing). alpha fades the whole text."""
    f = font(cam.k(size), weight)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(img, "RGBA")
    widths = [d.textlength(ch, font=f) for ch in s]
    if width is not None:
        track = (cam.k(width) - sum(widths)) / max(1, len(s) - 1) / cam.k(1.0)
    tr = cam.k(track)
    total = sum(widths) + tr * (len(s) - 1)
    x, y = cam.p(*c)
    if align != "left":
        x -= total / 2
    layer = Image.new("RGBA", img.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    ld = ImageDraw.Draw(layer)
    mask = Image.new("L", img.size, 0)
    md = ImageDraw.Draw(mask)
    cx = x
    for ch, w in zip(s, widths):
        md.text((cx, y), ch, font=f, anchor="lm", fill=255)
        cx += w + tr
    if alpha < 1.0:
        mask = mask.point(lambda v: int(v * alpha))
    if shadow:
        sh = mask.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(cam.k(2)))
        img.paste(Image.new("RGBA", img.size, shadow), (int(cam.k(1)), int(cam.k(2))), sh)
    if outline and ow > 0:
        o = mask.filter(ImageFilter.MaxFilter(max(3, int(cam.k(ow)) * 2 + 1)))
        img.paste(Image.new("RGBA", img.size, outline), (0, 0), o)
    if grad:
        top, bot = hexc(grad[0]), hexc(grad[1])
        H = img.size[1]
        t = np.clip((np.arange(H) - (y - cam.k(size) * 0.5)) / max(1, cam.k(size)), 0, 1)
        col = np.stack([np.interp(t, [0, 1], [top[i], bot[i]]) for i in range(3)], axis=1).astype(np.uint8)
        arr = np.repeat(col[:, None, :], img.size[0], axis=1)
        gl = Image.fromarray(arr, "RGB").convert("RGBA")
        img.paste(gl, (0, 0), mask)
    else:
        img.paste(Image.new("RGBA", img.size, fill), (0, 0), mask)
``````
