``````python
"""Doodled faces. Coordinates are in head units: u across (-1 left .. 1 right as seen from the front), v down,
the head circle has radius 1. Primitives (JSON friendly, so scenes can add their own under "faces"):
  ["dot", u, v, rx, ry]                 filled dark ellipse
  ["ellipse", u, v, rx, ry, fill, stroke]   fill/stroke are "#rrggbb[aa]" or null
  ["line", [[u,v],...], width]          dark stroked line (width in head units)
  ["fill", [[u,v],...], "#color"]       filled polygon (colour optional = dark)
A face is a dict: {"parts": [...], "mouth": [...]}. `mouth` is replaced by an animated mouth while `talk` is on.
Seen from the side the features wrap around the head; from behind the face is hidden.
"""
import math

INK = "#34303a"


def arc(u0, u1, v, depth, n=9):
    """curve from u0..u1 at height v bulging by depth (positive = smile)."""
    return [[u0 + (u1 - u0) * i / n, v + depth * (1 - ((2 * i / n - 1) ** 2))] for i in range(n + 1)]


def _eyes(v=0.0, rx=0.085, ry=0.13, gap=0.36):
    return [["dot", -gap, v, rx, ry], ["dot", gap, v, rx, ry]]


def _arch(u, v, w=0.17, up=True):
    h = -0.12 if up else 0.12
    return ["line", arc(u - w, u + w, v + (0.05 if up else -0.05), h, 6), 0.07]


FACES = {
    "calm": dict(parts=_eyes(), mouth=[["line", arc(-0.2, 0.2, 0.42, 0.05), 0.065]]),
    "happy": dict(parts=[_arch(-0.36, 0.02), _arch(0.36, 0.02), ["ellipse", -0.62, 0.3, 0.14, 0.08, "#ff9aa855", None],
                         ["ellipse", 0.62, 0.3, 0.14, 0.08, "#ff9aa855", None]],
                  mouth=[["line", arc(-0.32, 0.32, 0.34, 0.2), 0.07]]),
    "laugh": dict(parts=[_arch(-0.36, 0.0), _arch(0.36, 0.0)],
                  mouth=[["fill", arc(-0.34, 0.34, 0.3, 0.0, 2) + arc(0.34, -0.34, 0.3, 0.42, 8)[1:], INK],
                         ["fill", arc(-0.18, 0.18, 0.58, 0.1, 6), "#e8708a"]]),
    "sad": dict(parts=_eyes(0.05) + [["line", [[-0.55, -0.2], [-0.18, -0.34]], 0.06], ["line", [[0.55, -0.2], [0.18, -0.34]], 0.06],
                                     ["ellipse", -0.4, 0.34, 0.05, 0.1, "#7ec8ff", None]],
                mouth=[["line", arc(-0.22, 0.22, 0.56, -0.12), 0.065]]),
    "angry": dict(parts=_eyes(0.06, 0.08, 0.1) + [["line", [[-0.62, -0.32], [-0.14, -0.12]], 0.09], ["line", [[0.62, -0.32], [0.14, -0.12]], 0.09]],
                  mouth=[["line", [[-0.28, 0.5], [-0.1, 0.42], [0.1, 0.42], [0.28, 0.5]], 0.07]]),
    "scared": dict(parts=[["ellipse", -0.34, 0.0, 0.16, 0.2, "#ffffff", INK], ["ellipse", 0.34, 0.0, 0.16, 0.2, "#ffffff", INK],
                          ["dot", -0.34, 0.02, 0.04, 0.05], ["dot", 0.34, 0.02, 0.04, 0.05],
                          ["line", [[-0.6, -0.34], [-0.2, -0.44]], 0.05], ["line", [[0.6, -0.34], [0.2, -0.44]], 0.05],
                          ["ellipse", 0.78, -0.2, 0.06, 0.12, "#7ec8ff", None]],
                  mouth=[["line", [[-0.26, 0.5], [-0.13, 0.42], [0, 0.5], [0.13, 0.42], [0.26, 0.5]], 0.06]]),
    "surprised": dict(parts=[["ellipse", -0.34, 0.0, 0.14, 0.18, "#ffffff", INK], ["ellipse", 0.34, 0.0, 0.14, 0.18, "#ffffff", INK],
                             ["dot", -0.34, 0.0, 0.05, 0.07], ["dot", 0.34, 0.0, 0.05, 0.07],
                             ["line", arc(-0.55, -0.15, -0.4, -0.1, 6), 0.05], ["line", arc(0.15, 0.55, -0.4, -0.1, 6), 0.05]],
                     mouth=[["ellipse", 0, 0.5, 0.1, 0.15, INK, None]]),
    "smirk": dict(parts=_eyes(0.02) + [["line", [[0.14, -0.3], [0.56, -0.38]], 0.06], ["line", [[-0.56, -0.24], [-0.14, -0.24]], 0.06]],
                  mouth=[["line", [[-0.2, 0.48], [0.05, 0.48], [0.3, 0.36]], 0.065]]),
    "cool": dict(parts=[["fill", [[-0.7, -0.14], [0.7, -0.14], [0.62, 0.16], [0.08, 0.16], [0.0, 0.0], [-0.08, 0.16], [-0.62, 0.16]], "#1d1b22"]],
                 mouth=[["line", arc(-0.18, 0.2, 0.46, 0.06), 0.065]]),
    "sleepy": dict(parts=[["line", [[-0.5, 0.04], [-0.2, 0.04]], 0.07], ["line", [[0.2, 0.04], [0.5, 0.04]], 0.07]],
                   mouth=[["ellipse", 0, 0.5, 0.07, 0.07, INK, None]]),
    "wink": dict(parts=[["dot", -0.36, 0, 0.085, 0.13], _arch(0.36, 0.02, 0.16)],
                 mouth=[["line", arc(-0.3, 0.3, 0.36, 0.17), 0.07]]),
    "dead": dict(parts=[["line", [[-0.5, -0.12], [-0.22, 0.14]], 0.07], ["line", [[-0.5, 0.14], [-0.22, -0.12]], 0.07],
                        ["line", [[0.22, -0.12], [0.5, 0.14]], 0.07], ["line", [[0.22, 0.14], [0.5, -0.12]], 0.07]],
                 mouth=[["line", [[-0.2, 0.5], [0.2, 0.5]], 0.065]]),
    "cry": dict(parts=[["line", arc(-0.52, -0.2, 0.0, -0.1, 6), 0.07], ["line", arc(0.2, 0.52, 0.0, -0.1, 6), 0.07],
                       ["ellipse", -0.38, 0.3, 0.06, 0.2, "#7ec8ff", None], ["ellipse", 0.38, 0.3, 0.06, 0.2, "#7ec8ff", None]],
               mouth=[["fill", arc(-0.26, 0.26, 0.5, -0.2, 8) + arc(0.26, -0.26, 0.5, -0.02, 4)[1:], INK]]),
    "blank": dict(parts=_eyes(0.0, 0.07, 0.07), mouth=[]),
    "love": dict(parts=[["fill", [[-0.36, 0.2], [-0.62, -0.02], [-0.5, -0.16], [-0.36, -0.06], [-0.22, -0.16], [-0.1, -0.02]], "#e8446a"],
                        ["fill", [[0.36, 0.2], [0.1, -0.02], [0.22, -0.16], [0.36, -0.06], [0.5, -0.16], [0.62, -0.02]], "#e8446a"]],
                 mouth=[["line", arc(-0.3, 0.3, 0.38, 0.18), 0.07]]),
    "smug": dict(parts=[["line", [[-0.52, 0.06], [-0.2, 0.06]], 0.07], ["line", [[0.2, 0.06], [0.52, 0.06]], 0.07],
                        ["line", [[-0.56, -0.2], [-0.16, -0.28]], 0.055]],
                 mouth=[["line", arc(-0.22, 0.34, 0.42, 0.1), 0.065]]),
}


def talk_mouth(openness):
    """mouth primitives for talking, openness 0..1."""
    if openness < 0.12:
        return [["line", arc(-0.2, 0.2, 0.44, 0.04), 0.065]]
    ry = 0.06 + 0.2 * openness
    return [["ellipse", 0, 0.46 + ry * 0.3, 0.07 + 0.1 * openness, ry, INK, None]]


def talk_amount(t, seed=0):
    """deterministic flappy mouth: new random target every 1/9 s, smoothed."""
    def r(i):
        x = math.sin(i * 12.9898 + seed * 78.233) * 43758.5453
        return x - math.floor(x)
    s = t * 9.0
    i = math.floor(s)
    u = s - i
    u = u * u * (3 - 2 * u)
    a, b = r(i), r(i + 1)
    v = a + (b - a) * u
    return 0.0 if v < 0.25 else min(1.0, (v - 0.25) / 0.6)
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/figure.py -->
``````python
"""Stick figure rig: 2-bone IK arms and legs, thick outlined limbs with edge shading, a pickaxe prop.

A pose is a dict of reference-space points:
  head (x, y)      centre of the head circle
  neck (x, y)      base of the head / top of the torso
  hip  (x, y)
  hands [(x, y), (x, y)]      first = near hand (drawn on top)
  feet  [(x, y), (x, y)]      first = near foot
  eb [sign, sign]  elbow bend side (+1/-1); kb [sign, sign] knee bend side
  pick None or angle in degrees (handle direction from the grip, 0 = right, -90 = up)
  pick_len handle length beyond the grip
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageChops, ImageFilter
from .engine import S, lerp, hexc

OUTLINE = (52, 46, 52, 255)
FILL = (247, 247, 252, 255)
SHADE = (176, 182, 208, 255)

UPPER_ARM, FORE_ARM = 33.0, 37.0
THIGH, SHIN = 43.0, 43.0
LIMB_ARM, LIMB_LEG, LIMB_TORSO, HEAD_R = 15.0, 19.0, 21.0, 17.0


def ik2(root, target, l1, l2, side):
    """elbow/knee position for a 2-bone chain root->target; side = +1/-1 picks the bend direction."""
    rx, ry = root
    tx, ty = target
    dx, dy = tx - rx, ty - ry
    d = max(math.hypot(dx, dy), 1e-3)
    if d > (l1 + l2) * 0.999:                      # cartoon stretch: lengthen both bones to reach
        f = d / ((l1 + l2) * 0.999)
        l1, l2 = l1 * f, l2 * f
    a = (l1 * l1 - l2 * l2 + d * d) / (2 * d)
    h2 = max(l1 * l1 - a * a, 0.0)
    h = math.sqrt(h2)
    ux, uy = dx / math.hypot(dx, dy), dy / math.hypot(dx, dy)
    px, py = rx + ux * a, ry + uy * a
    return (px - uy * h * side, py + ux * h * side)


def lerp_pose(a, b, u):
    out = {}
    for k in ("head", "neck", "hip"):
        out[k] = (lerp(a[k][0], b[k][0], u), lerp(a[k][1], b[k][1], u))
    out["hands"] = [(lerp(a["hands"][i][0], b["hands"][i][0], u), lerp(a["hands"][i][1], b["hands"][i][1], u)) for i in range(2)]
    out["feet"] = [(lerp(a["feet"][i][0], b["feet"][i][0], u), lerp(a["feet"][i][1], b["feet"][i][1], u)) for i in range(2)]
    out["eb"] = a.get("eb", [1, 1])
    out["kb"] = a.get("kb", [1, -1])
    pa, pb = a.get("pick"), b.get("pick")
    if pa is None or pb is None:
        out["pick"] = pa if u < 0.5 else pb
    else:
        d = (pb - pa + 180) % 360 - 180          # shortest way round
        out["pick"] = pa + d * u
    out["pick_len"] = lerp(a.get("pick_len", 58), b.get("pick_len", 58), u)
    out["tint"] = a.get("tint")
    return out


def shift(pose, dx, dy):
    q = dict(pose)
    for k in ("head", "neck", "hip"):
        q[k] = (pose[k][0] + dx, pose[k][1] + dy)
    q["hands"] = [(h[0] + dx, h[1] + dy) for h in pose["hands"]]
    q["feet"] = [(h[0] + dx, h[1] + dy) for h in pose["feet"]]
    return q


def _capsule_mask(size, pts, width, S_, off):
    m = Image.new("L", size, 0)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(m)
    P = [((x - off[0]) * S_, (y - off[1]) * S_) for x, y in pts]
    w = max(1, int(round(width * S_)))
    d.line(P, fill=255, width=w, joint="curve")
    r = w / 2
    for p in (P[0], P[-1]):
        d.ellipse([p[0] - r, p[1] - r, p[0] + r, p[1] + r], fill=255)
    return m


def _circle_mask(size, c, r, S_, off):
    m = Image.new("L", size, 0)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(m)
    x, y = (c[0] - off[0]) * S_, (c[1] - off[1]) * S_
    d.ellipse([x - r * S_, y - r * S_, x + r * S_, y + r * S_], fill=255)
    return m


def _paint_part(patch, mask, ow, S_, fill=FILL, shade=SHADE, tint=None):
    """outline + fill + lower-right edge shading for one body part."""
    k = max(3, int(round(ow * S_)) * 2 + 1)
    om = mask.filter(ImageFilter.MaxFilter(k))
    patch.paste(Image.new("RGBA", patch.size, OUTLINE), (0, 0), om)
    f = fill
    if tint:
        f = tuple(int(c * t) for c, t in zip(fill[:3], tint)) + (255,)
    patch.paste(Image.new("RGBA", patch.size, f), (0, 0), mask)
    # shading = mask minus mask moved up-left; then soften
    sh = ImageChops.subtract(mask, ImageChops.offset(mask, -int(3 * S_), -int(3 * S_)))
    sh = sh.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(1.2 * S_))
    sh = ImageChops.multiply(sh, mask)
    sc = shade
    if tint:
        sc = tuple(int(c * t) for c, t in zip(shade[:3], tint)) + (255,)
    patch.paste(Image.new("RGBA", patch.size, sc), (0, 0), sh.point(lambda v: int(v * 0.85)))


def pick_geometry(grip, ang, plen):
    dx, dy = math.cos(math.radians(ang)), math.sin(math.radians(ang))
    nx, ny = -dy, dx
    end = (grip[0] + dx * plen, grip[1] + dy * plen)
    back = (grip[0] - dx * 14, grip[1] - dy * 14)

    def L(u, v):
        return (end[0] + dx * u + nx * v, end[1] + dy * u + ny * v)

    return back, end, L


def draw_pick(img, grip, ang, plen, cam, hot=0.0):
    from .engine import line, poly
    back, end, L = pick_geometry(grip, ang, plen)
    line(img, back, end, cam, (60, 36, 20, 255), 6.5)
    line(img, back, end, cam, (206, 128, 66, 255), 3.6)
    line(img, (back[0] - 0.5, back[1] - 0.5), (end[0] - 0.5, end[1] - 0.5), cam, (240, 176, 110, 160), 1.1)

    def bez(p0, c, p1, n=14):
        out = []
        for i in range(n + 1):
            t = i / n
            out.append(((1 - t) ** 2 * p0[0] + 2 * (1 - t) * t * c[0] + t * t * p1[0],
                        (1 - t) ** 2 * p0[1] + 2 * (1 - t) * t * c[1] + t * t * p1[1]))
        return out

    t1, t2 = L(-16, -46), L(-16, 46)
    outer = bez(t1, L(40, 0), t2)
    inner = bez(t2, L(12, 0), t1)
    shape = outer + inner
    poly(img, shape, cam, fill=(206, 212, 224, 255), outline=(48, 50, 60, 255), width=2.0)
    # lighter highlight along the outer edge
    hi = bez(L(-8, -38), L(30, -2), L(-2, 22), 10)
    for a, b in zip(hi[:-1], hi[1:]):
        line(img, a, b, cam, (250, 252, 255, 210), 1.8, cap=False)
    # metal block where the handle enters
    blk = [L(-6, -7), L(7, -7), L(7, 7), L(-6, 7)]
    poly(img, blk, cam, fill=(108, 112, 122, 255), outline=(40, 40, 48, 255), width=1.6)
    return L(20, 0)


def draw_figure(img, pose, cam, tint=None):
    """draw one stick figure (and its pick) onto img."""
    pts = [pose["head"], pose["neck"], pose["hip"]] + list(pose["hands"]) + list(pose["feet"])
    pts += list(pose.get("elbows") or []) + list(pose.get("knees") or [])
    if pose.get("pick") is not None:
        g = ((pose["hands"][0][0] + pose["hands"][1][0]) / 2, (pose["hands"][0][1] + pose["hands"][1][1]) / 2)
        _, e, _ = pick_geometry(g, pose["pick"], pose.get("pick_len", 58))
        pts.append(e)
    xs, ys = [p[0] for p in pts], [p[1] for p in pts]
    pad = 70
    # work in canvas pixel space of the camera
    cpts = [cam.p(x, y) for x, y in pts]
    cx0 = int(max(0, min(p[0] for p in cpts) - cam.k(pad)))
    cy0 = int(max(0, min(p[1] for p in cpts) - cam.k(pad)))
    cx1 = int(min(img.size[0], max(p[0] for p in cpts) + cam.k(pad)))
    cy1 = int(min(img.size[1], max(p[1] for p in cpts) + cam.k(pad)))
    if cx1 <= cx0 or cy1 <= cy0:
        return
    size = (cx1 - cx0, cy1 - cy0)
    patch = Image.new("RGBA", size, (0, 0, 0, 0))

    k = cam.z * S

    def T(p):
        q = cam.p(*p)
        return (q[0] - cx0, q[1] - cy0)

    def cap(pts_, width):
        m = Image.new("L", size, 0)
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(m)
        P = [T(p) for p in pts_]
        w = max(1, int(round(width * k)))
        d.line(P, fill=255, width=w, joint="curve")
        r = w / 2
        for p in (P[0], P[-1]):
            d.ellipse([p[0] - r, p[1] - r, p[0] + r, p[1] + r], fill=255)
        return m

    def circ(c, r):
        m = Image.new("L", size, 0)
        x, y = T(c)
        ImageDraw.Draw(m).ellipse([x - r * k, y - r * k, x + r * k, y + r * k], fill=255)
        return m

    def paint(mask, ow=2.4):
        # the helper uses S as pixel scale; here pixel scale is k
        kk = max(3, int(round(ow * k)) * 2 + 1)
        om = mask.filter(ImageFilter.MaxFilter(kk))
        patch.paste(Image.new("RGBA", size, OUTLINE), (0, 0), om)
        f = FILL if tint is None else tuple(int(c * t) for c, t in zip(FILL[:3], tint)) + (255,)
        patch.paste(Image.new("RGBA", size, f), (0, 0), mask)
        off = max(1, int(3 * k))
        sh = ImageChops.subtract(mask, ImageChops.offset(mask, -off, -off)).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(1.2 * k))
        sh = ImageChops.multiply(sh, mask)
        sc = SHADE if tint is None else tuple(int(c * t) for c, t in zip(SHADE[:3], tint)) + (255,)
        patch.paste(Image.new("RGBA", size, sc), (0, 0), sh.point(lambda v: int(v * 0.85)))

    neck, hip, head = pose["neck"], pose["hip"], pose["head"]
    eb = pose.get("eb", [1, 1])
    kb = pose.get("kb", [1, -1])
    sh_pt = (neck[0], neck[1] + 8)
    legs = []
    for i in (1, 0):                       # far leg first
        f = pose["feet"][i]
        knee = pose["knees"][i] if pose.get("knees") else ik2(hip, f, THIGH, SHIN, kb[i])
        legs.append([hip, knee, f])
    paint(cap(legs[0], LIMB_LEG))
    paint(cap([neck, hip], LIMB_TORSO))
    paint(cap(legs[1], LIMB_LEG))
    paint(circ(head, HEAD_R))
    arms = []
    for i in (1, 0):
        h = pose["hands"][i]
        sp = pose.get("shoulder", sh_pt)
        el = pose["elbows"][i] if pose.get("elbows") else ik2(sp, h, UPPER_ARM, FORE_ARM, eb[i])
        arms.append([sp, el, h])
    img.alpha_composite(patch, (cx0, cy0)) if False else None
    # arms are drawn after the pick handle so the hands wrap over it; pick drawn between body and arms
    base = img
    base.alpha_composite(patch, (cx0, cy0))
    patch.paste((0, 0, 0, 0), (0, 0, size[0], size[1]))
    if pose.get("prop_draw"):
        pose["prop_draw"](base)
    if pose.get("pick") is not None:
        g = ((pose["hands"][0][0] + pose["hands"][1][0]) / 2, (pose["hands"][0][1] + pose["hands"][1][1]) / 2)
        draw_pick(base, g, pose["pick"], pose.get("pick_len", 58), cam)
    for a in arms:
        paint(cap(a, LIMB_ARM))
    base.alpha_composite(patch, (cx0, cy0))
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/figure3d.py -->
``````python
"""Draws the solved figure: thick outlined capsules sorted by depth (so it works from the front, side and back),
a doodled face that wraps around the head, and a hook where held items are drawn between body parts."""
import math
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageChops, ImageFilter

from .engine import S
from .faces import FACES, INK, talk_mouth
from .track import parse_color

OUTLINE = (52, 46, 52, 255)
FILL = (247, 247, 252, 255)
SHADE = (176, 182, 208, 255)
LIMB_ARM, LIMB_LEG, LIMB_TORSO, HEAD_R = 14.0, 17.5, 19.5, 17.0
OUTLINE_W = 1.9


def _mul(c, tint):
    return tuple(int(v * t) for v, t in zip(c[:3], tint)) + (255,)


def _hex(c, a=None):
    r = parse_color(c)
    return r if a is None else r[:3] + (a,)


def draw_figure(img, pose, cam, face=None, talk_open=None, faces=None, item_hooks=None, t=0.0, blink=True, depth_shade=True):
    """pose from skeleton.solve. item_hooks = {"arm_r": fn(img), ...} drawn just before that arm (so the hand wraps it)."""
    from .engine import S as _S
    tint = pose.get("tint")
    tint = tuple(tint) if tint is not None else None
    pts = [pose["head"], pose["neck"], pose["hip"]] + pose["hands"] + pose["feet"] + pose["elbows"] + pose["knees"]
    cp = [cam.p(*q) for q in pts]
    pad = cam.k(60)
    x0, y0 = int(max(0, min(q[0] for q in cp) - pad)), int(max(0, min(q[1] for q in cp) - pad))
    x1, y1 = int(min(img.size[0], max(q[0] for q in cp) + pad)), int(min(img.size[1], max(q[1] for q in cp) + pad))
    if x1 <= x0 or y1 <= y0:
        return
    size = (x1 - x0, y1 - y0)
    patch = Image.new("RGBA", size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    k = cam.z * _S

    def T(p):
        q = cam.p(*p)
        return (q[0] - x0, q[1] - y0)

    def cap(points, width):
        m = Image.new("L", size, 0)
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(m)
        P = [T(p) for p in points]
        w = max(1, int(round(width * k)))
        d.line(P, fill=255, width=w, joint="curve")
        r = w / 2
        for p in (P[0], P[-1]):
            d.ellipse([p[0] - r, p[1] - r, p[0] + r, p[1] + r], fill=255)
        return m

    def circ(c, r):
        m = Image.new("L", size, 0)
        x, y = T(c)
        ImageDraw.Draw(m).ellipse([x - r * k, y - r * k, x + r * k, y + r * k], fill=255)
        return m

    def paint(mask, ow=OUTLINE_W, shade=0.0):
        kk = max(3, int(round(ow * k)) * 2 + 1)
        patch.paste(Image.new("RGBA", size, OUTLINE), (0, 0), mask.filter(ImageFilter.MaxFilter(kk)))
        base = FILL if not shade else tuple(int(a + (b - a) * shade) for a, b in zip(FILL[:3], SHADE[:3])) + (255,)
        patch.paste(Image.new("RGBA", size, _mul(base, tint) if tint else base), (0, 0), mask)
        off = max(1, int(3 * k))
        sh = ImageChops.subtract(mask, ImageChops.offset(mask, -off, -off)).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(1.2 * k))
        sh = ImageChops.multiply(sh, mask)
        patch.paste(Image.new("RGBA", size, _mul(SHADE, tint) if tint else SHADE), (0, 0), sh.point(lambda v: int(v * 0.85)))

    dep = pose["depth"]
    far = lambda name: (0.65 if dep[name] > dep["torso"] + 3.0 else 0.0) if depth_shade else 0.0      # far limbs read grey, like a drawn figure
    parts = [
        (dep["leg_r"], 0, "leg_r", lambda: paint(cap([pose["hip"], pose["knees"][0], pose["feet"][0]], LIMB_LEG), shade=far("leg_r"))),
        (dep["leg_l"], 1, "leg_l", lambda: paint(cap([pose["hip"], pose["knees"][1], pose["feet"][1]], LIMB_LEG), shade=far("leg_l"))),
        (dep["torso"], 2, "torso", lambda: paint(cap([pose["neck"], pose["hip"]], LIMB_TORSO))),
        (dep["head"] - 0.05, 3, "head", None),
        (dep["arm_r"], 4, "arm_r", lambda: paint(cap([pose["shoulders"][0], pose["elbows"][0], pose["hands"][0]], LIMB_ARM), shade=far("arm_r") * 0.5)),
        (dep["arm_l"], 5, "arm_l", lambda: paint(cap([pose["shoulders"][1], pose["elbows"][1], pose["hands"][1]], LIMB_ARM), shade=far("arm_l") * 0.5)),
    ]
    parts.sort(key=lambda a: (-a[0], a[1]))             # far first
    hooks = item_hooks or {}
    for _, _, name, fn in parts:
        if name in hooks:                                # items go behind the hand that holds them
            img.alpha_composite(patch, (x0, y0))
            patch.paste((0, 0, 0, 0), (0, 0, size[0], size[1]))
            hooks[name](img)
        if name == "head":
            paint(circ(pose["head"], HEAD_R))
            if face is not None:
                _draw_face(patch, pose, face, talk_open, T, k, faces or FACES, (t if blink else None))
        else:
            fn()
    img.alpha_composite(patch, (x0, y0))


def blink_amount(t):
    """1 = eyes open, ~0.1 = shut: one quick blink about every 3 s, at slightly irregular times."""
    period = 3.1
    i = math.floor(t / period)
    off = 0.5 + ((math.sin(i * 12.9898) * 43758.5453) % 1.0) * 2.0
    u = (t - i * period - off) / 0.13
    return 1.0 if not (0 <= u <= 1) else 0.12 + 0.88 * abs(2 * u - 1)


def _draw_face(patch, pose, face, talk_open, T, k, faces, t=None):
    spec = faces.get(face) or FACES.get(face)
    if spec is None:
        return
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(patch, "RGBA")
    R = HEAD_R * k * 0.94
    cx, cy = T(pose["head"])
    yaw = pose["yaw"]
    tilt = math.radians(pose["head_tilt"]) * (1 if math.cos(math.radians(yaw)) >= 0 else -1)
    ct, st = math.cos(tilt), math.sin(tilt)
    off = math.radians(90 - yaw) * 0.8              # <1: the face sits a little toward the camera so profiles still show it

    def proj(u, v):
        phi = math.asin(max(-1.0, min(1.0, u * 0.95)))
        lam = phi + off
        lam = (lam + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi
        vis = math.cos(lam)
        x, y = R * math.sin(lam), v * R
        return (cx + x * ct - y * st, cy + x * st + y * ct), vis

    mouth = spec.get("mouth", [])
    if talk_open is not None:
        mouth = talk_mouth(talk_open)
    for prim in list(spec.get("parts", [])) + list(mouth):
        kind = prim[0]
        if kind == "dot":
            _, u, v, rx, ry = prim
            if t is not None and abs(u) > 0.2 and -0.2 < v < 0.25:      # an eye
                ry = ry * blink_amount(t)
            _poly(d, proj, [(u + rx * 1.25 * math.cos(a), v + ry * 1.2 * math.sin(a)) for a in _ang(12)], (u, v), INK_C, None, 0)
        elif kind == "ellipse":
            _, u, v, rx, ry, fill, stroke = prim
            _poly(d, proj, [(u + rx * math.cos(a), v + ry * math.sin(a)) for a in _ang(14)], (u, v),
                  _hex(fill) if fill else None, _hex(stroke) if stroke else None, max(1, int(0.05 * R)))
        elif kind == "fill":
            pts = prim[1]
            col = _hex(prim[2]) if len(prim) > 2 and prim[2] else INK_C
            c = (sum(p[0] for p in pts) / len(pts), sum(p[1] for p in pts) / len(pts))
            _poly(d, proj, pts, c, col, None, 0)
        elif kind == "line":
            pts, w = prim[1], prim[2]
            col = _hex(prim[3]) if len(prim) > 3 and prim[3] else INK_C
            P = []
            for u, v in pts:
                q, vis = proj(u, v)
                if vis > 0.02:
                    P.append(q)
            if len(P) >= 2:
                wd = max(1, int(w * R * 1.4))
                d.line(P, fill=col, width=wd, joint="curve")
                for q in (P[0], P[-1]):
                    d.ellipse([q[0] - wd / 2, q[1] - wd / 2, q[0] + wd / 2, q[1] + wd / 2], fill=col)


INK_C = _hex(INK)


def _ang(n):
    return [2 * math.pi * i / n for i in range(n)]


def _poly(d, proj, pts, centre, fill, stroke, sw):
    _, vis = proj(*centre)
    if vis <= 0.02:
        return
    P = [proj(u, v)[0] for u, v in pts]
    d.polygon(P, fill=fill, outline=stroke)
    if stroke and sw > 1:
        d.line(P + [P[0]], fill=stroke, width=sw)
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/items.py -->
``````python
"""Phone items for stickreel: decoded video/still frames for the phone screen, and draw_phone().

Working units follow engine.py: world/reference units through a Cam, canvas pixels = units * cam.z * S.
Run the self-test from /home/user/LegoReel/stick with: python3 -m stickreel.items
"""
import math
import os
import subprocess
import sys

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageOps

from . import engine as E
from .track import parse_color

MAX_FRAMES = 600
SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H = 120, 240
_IMAGE_EXT = (".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".webp", ".bmp")
_VIDEO_CACHE = {}


def load_video_frames(path, w, h, fps):
    """Decode a video (or load a still image) to a list of RGB PIL images of size w x h, cropped to fill.

    Video is decoded once per (path, w, h, fps) with ffmpeg and cached. Missing or None paths give [].
    At most MAX_FRAMES frames are kept.
    """
    if not path or not os.path.isfile(path):
        return []
    key = (path, w, h, fps)
    if key in _VIDEO_CACHE:
        return _VIDEO_CACHE[key]
    frames = []
    try:
        if path.lower().endswith(_IMAGE_EXT):
            with Image.open(path) as im:
                frames = [ImageOps.fit(im.convert("RGB"), (w, h), Image.LANCZOS)]
        else:
            vf = f"fps={fps},scale={w}:{h}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={w}:{h}"
            cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-i", path, "-vf", vf, "-frames:v", str(MAX_FRAMES),
                   "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgb24", "-"]
            res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, check=False)
            if res.returncode != 0:
                print(f"items: ffmpeg failed on {path}: {res.stderr.decode(errors='replace').strip()}", file=sys.stderr)
            else:
                fsize = w * h * 3
                n = len(res.stdout) // fsize
                if n:
                    buf = np.frombuffer(res.stdout, dtype=np.uint8, count=n * fsize).reshape(n, h, w, 3)
                    frames = [Image.fromarray(buf[i].copy(), "RGB") for i in range(n)]
    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        print(f"items: could not load {path}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        frames = []
    _VIDEO_CACHE[key] = frames
    return frames


def _resolve(path, scene):
    if not path:
        return None
    return path if os.path.isabs(path) else os.path.join(getattr(scene, "base_dir", "."), path)


def screen_image(p, t, scene):
    """RGBA image (SCREEN_W x SCREEN_H) of the phone screen at scene time t.

    p keys: video, video_start (s), video_speed (1), loop (True), screen_color (shown with no video or before it starts).
    """
    W, H = SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H
    fill = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), parse_color(p.get("screen_color") or "#101820"))
    fps = scene.fps
    frames = load_video_frames(_resolve(p.get("video"), scene), W, H, fps)
    if not frames:
        return fill
    since = (t - float(p.get("video_start") or 0.0))
    if since < 0:
        return fill
    speed = float(p.get("video_speed") or 1.0)
    idx = int(since * speed * fps)
    n = len(frames)
    if p.get("loop", True):
        idx %= n
    else:
        idx = min(idx, n - 1)
    return frames[idx].convert("RGBA")


def _mix(c, target, u):
    return tuple(int(round(a + (b - a) * u)) for a, b in zip(c, target))


def _upright(k, w, h, g, color, screen, L):
    """phone standing upright on an L x L transparent layer, grip point at the layer centre.
    k = pixels per world unit. g = grip position along the length from the bottom (0..1)."""
    layer = Image.new("RGBA", (L, L), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(layer)
    c = L / 2.0
    W, H = w * k, h * k
    left, right = c - W / 2, c + W / 2
    top, bot = c - (1 - g) * H, c + g * H

    edge = _mix(color, (255, 255, 255), 0.35)
    d.rounded_rectangle([left, top, right, bot], radius=3.2 * k, fill=color + (255,),
                        outline=edge + (255,), width=max(1, int(round(0.6 * k))))

    bs, bt, bb = 1.2 * k, 2.4 * k, 2.4 * k           # side, top and bottom bezels
    sx0, sy0, sx1, sy1 = left + bs, top + bt, right - bs, bot - bb
    sw, sh = max(1, int(round(sx1 - sx0))), max(1, int(round(sy1 - sy0)))
    d.rounded_rectangle([sx0, sy0, sx1, sy1], radius=2.0 * k, fill=(8, 8, 10, 255))
    scr = ImageOps.fit(screen, (sw, sh), Image.BICUBIC)
    mask = Image.new("L", (sw, sh), 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(mask).rounded_rectangle([0, 0, sw - 1, sh - 1], radius=2.0 * k, fill=255)
    layer.paste(scr, (int(round(sx0)), int(round(sy0))), mask)

    nw, nh = 5.0 * k, 1.3 * k                          # front camera notch in the top bezel
    ncy = top + bt / 2
    d.rounded_rectangle([c - nw / 2, ncy - nh / 2, c + nw / 2, ncy + nh / 2], radius=nh / 2, fill=(4, 4, 6, 255))
    return layer


def _composite(img, layer, x0, y0):
    """alpha-composite layer onto img with its top-left at (x0, y0), clipping at the edges."""
    W, H = img.size
    lw, lh = layer.size
    sx, sy = max(0, -x0), max(0, -y0)
    dx, dy = max(0, x0), max(0, y0)
    cw, ch = min(lw - sx, W - dx), min(lh - sy, H - dy)
    if cw <= 0 or ch <= 0:
        return
    crop = layer.crop((sx, sy, sx + cw, sy + ch))
    if img.mode == "RGBA":
        img.alpha_composite(crop, (dx, dy))
    else:
        img.paste(crop, (dx, dy), crop)


def draw_phone(img, p, cam, t, scene):
    """draw a smartphone onto img. The grip point (x, y) in world units lands on cam.p(x, y).

    p keys: x, y, rot (deg; direction from grip to the top edge, 0 = right, -90 = up), w (26), h (48), scale (1),
    color ('#2a2a30'), grip_at (0.35, fraction up the length from the bottom), plus the screen_image keys.
    """
    x, y = float(p["x"]), float(p["y"])
    rot = float(p.get("rot", 0.0))
    w, h = float(p.get("w", 26)), float(p.get("h", 48))
    scale = float(p.get("scale", 1.0))
    g = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(p.get("grip_at", 0.35))))
    color = parse_color(p.get("color") or "#2a2a30")[:3]

    k = 4.0 * cam.z * E.S * scale                       # px per world unit
    # square layer centred on the grip that holds the phone at any angle: half-size must cover the far end
    # of the body (the longer side of the grip) and its corners
    far = max(g, 1 - g) * h * k
    L = 2 * int(math.ceil(max(w * k / 2, far, math.hypot(w * k / 2, far)))) + 8
    screen = screen_image(p, t, scene)
    layer = _upright(k, w, h, g, color, screen, L)
    layer = layer.rotate(-(rot + 90.0), resample=Image.BILINEAR)  # PIL rotates counter-clockwise; screen angles are clockwise

    gx, gy = cam.p(x, y)
    _composite(img, layer, int(round(gx - L / 2)), int(round(gy - L / 2)))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import time
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from .engine import Cam

    src = "/tmp/phone_test_src.mp4"
    subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc=size=240x480:rate=30:duration=4",
                    "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", src], check=True)
    scene = SimpleNamespace(base_dir=".", fps=30)
    E.S = 2.0

    assert load_video_frames(None, 120, 240, 30) == []
    assert load_video_frames("/nonexistent/clip.mp4", 120, 240, 30) == []
    frames = load_video_frames(src, SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, 30)
    assert len(frames) == 120, len(frames)
    assert load_video_frames(src, SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, 30) is frames  # cached
    scr = screen_image(dict(video=src, video_start=0.0, screen_color="#1e90ff"), 1.0, scene)
    assert scr.size == (SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H) and scr.mode == "RGBA"
    assert screen_image(dict(video=src, video_start=2.0, screen_color="#1e90ff"), 1.0, scene).getpixel((5, 5)) == (30, 144, 255, 255)

    # 300 x 300 reference canvas at S = 2
    canvas = Image.new("RGBA", (int(300 * E.S), int(300 * E.S)), (40, 44, 52, 255))
    cam = Cam(1.0, 150.0, 150.0, 150.0, 150.0)
    p = dict(video=src, video_start=0.0, video_speed=1, loop=True, screen_color="#1e90ff", x=150, y=150, rot=-90)
    draw_phone(canvas, p, cam, 1.0, scene)
    canvas.convert("RGB").save("/tmp/phone_test.png")

    # speed check on a full-size 1152 x 2048 canvas, a few runs after warm-up
    big = Image.new("RGBA", (1152, 2048), (40, 44, 52, 255))
    bigcam = Cam()
    draw_phone(big, dict(p, x=288, y=512, rot=20), bigcam, 1.0, scene)
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for i in range(5):
        draw_phone(big, dict(p, x=288 + i, y=512, rot=20 + i), bigcam, 1.0 + i / 30, scene)
    ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) / 5 * 1000
    print(f"self-test ok: 120 frames decoded, phone drawn to /tmp/phone_test.png, {ms:.1f} ms per draw at 1152x2048")
``````

``````python
"""Named poses and loops. A pose is just a dict of skeleton params; a loop is a repeating set of keys over phase 0..1.

Scenes can add their own under "poses" and "loops" (same format), so an agent never has to touch Python.
"""
import math

POSES = {
    "stand": {},
    "relax": dict(arm_r=[4, 8], arm_l=[-4, 8], leg_r=[3, 0], leg_l=[-3, 0]),
    "hands_up": dict(arm_r=[152, 6], arm_l=[-152, 6], lean=-2),
    "hands_up_wide": dict(arm_r=[140, 6], arm_l=[-140, 6], lean=-3),
    "cheer": dict(arm_r=[150, 14], arm_l=[-150, 14], lean=-4, head=-6),
    "wave": dict(arm_r=[150, 50], arm_l=[-6, 10]),
    "point": dict(arm_r=[92, 0], arm_l=[-8, 10], lean=4),
    "reach_forward": dict(arm_r=[100, 0], arm_l=[92, 0], lean=8),
    "crouch": dict(leg_r=[62, 118], leg_l=[40, 100], lean=22, arm_r=[40, 30], arm_l=[20, 30]),
    "sit": dict(leg_r=[90, 90], leg_l=[84, 90], lean=2, arm_r=[20, 40], arm_l=[10, 40]),
    "slump": dict(lean=14, head=18, arm_r=[10, 6], arm_l=[-2, 6]),
    "shrug": dict(arm_r=[40, 70], arm_l=[-40, 70], head=6),
    "hold_overhead": dict(arm_r=[160, 30], arm_l=[160, 30], lean=-4),
    "fall": dict(arm_r=[150, 8], arm_l=[-150, 8], leg_r=[28, 10], leg_l=[-28, 10], rot=-8),
    "tired": dict(lean=18, head=22, arm_r=[6, 4], arm_l=[-6, 4], leg_r=[6, 12], leg_l=[-6, 12]),
}


def _sin_keys(fn, n=8):
    return [dict(t=i / n, **fn(2 * math.pi * i / n)) for i in range(n)]


def _walk(amp, knee, arm, lean, lift=0.0):
    def k(th):
        c, s = math.cos(th), math.sin(th)
        c2, s2 = math.cos(th + math.pi), math.sin(th + math.pi)
        return dict(
            leg_r=[amp * c, knee * max(0.0, -s)], leg_l=[amp * c2, knee * max(0.0, -s2)],
            arm_r=[-arm * c, 6 + 8 * max(0.0, -c)], arm_l=[-arm * c2, 6 + 8 * max(0.0, -c2)], lean=lean, lift=lift * abs(s))
    return _sin_keys(k, 12)


# mode "add": values are added to whatever the keys set; "replace": loop values win.
# source "distance": one cycle = `stride` pixels travelled (feet do not slide); "time": one cycle = `period` seconds;
# "hits": phase follows the actor's `hits` list (see docs).
LOOPS = {
    "walk": dict(mode="add", source="distance", stride=150, keys=_walk(26, 50, 22, 3)),
    "run": dict(mode="add", source="distance", stride=250, keys=_walk(44, 95, 55, 14, lift=9)),
    "idle": dict(mode="add", source="time", period=2.4, keys=_sin_keys(lambda th: dict(
        lean=1.2 * math.sin(th), head=1.5 * math.sin(th - 0.8), arm_r=[1.5 * math.sin(th), 0], arm_l=[-1.5 * math.sin(th), 0]), 8)),
    "wave": dict(mode="add", source="time", period=0.7, keys=_sin_keys(lambda th: dict(arm_r=[0, 28 * math.sin(th)]), 8)),
    "bounce": dict(mode="add", source="time", period=0.5, keys=_sin_keys(lambda th: dict(lift=7 * (1 - math.cos(th)) / 2), 8)),
    "dance": dict(mode="add", source="time", period=0.8, keys=_sin_keys(lambda th: dict(
        arm_r=[60 * math.sin(th), 30], arm_l=[-60 * math.sin(th), 30], lean=6 * math.sin(th), lift=4 * abs(math.sin(th)),
        head=8 * math.sin(th)), 12)),
    # Hand-keyed two-handed overhead chop (both hands on ONE grip, rigid tool). phase 0 = tool back behind the head.
    # grip_rel is hip-relative; prop_angle 0 = tool pointing forward(right), -90 = up. Hit = contact (hit_phase).
    "chop": dict(mode="replace", source="hits", period=0.733, hit_phase=4 / 22, keys=[
        dict(t=0 / 22, grip_rel=[-45, -86], prop_angle=-148, lean=6, head=0, foot_r_rel=[16, 82], foot_l_rel=[-20, 81]),
        dict(t=2 / 22, grip_rel=[-40, -90], prop_angle=-132, lean=4, head=0, foot_r_rel=[16, 83], foot_l_rel=[-20, 82]),
        dict(t=3 / 22, grip_rel=[8, -80], prop_angle=-70, lean=16, head=-6, foot_r_rel=[20, 81], foot_l_rel=[-24, 80]),
        dict(t=4 / 22, grip_rel=[76, -38], prop_angle=8, lean=30, head=-13, foot_r_rel=[26, 78], foot_l_rel=[-30, 77]),
        dict(t=7 / 22, grip_rel=[80, -36], prop_angle=6, lean=31, head=-14, foot_r_rel=[26, 77], foot_l_rel=[-30, 76]),
        dict(t=9.5 / 22, grip_rel=[78, -38], prop_angle=0, lean=30, head=-14, foot_r_rel=[26, 78], foot_l_rel=[-30, 77]),
        dict(t=11.5 / 22, grip_rel=[68, -52], prop_angle=-14, lean=26, head=-17, foot_r_rel=[24, 79], foot_l_rel=[-27, 78]),
        dict(t=14 / 22, grip_rel=[46, -72], prop_angle=-30, lean=21, head=-17, foot_r_rel=[22, 81], foot_l_rel=[-22, 80]),
        dict(t=17 / 22, grip_rel=[44, -86], prop_angle=-56, lean=14, head=-11, foot_r_rel=[20, 83], foot_l_rel=[-18, 82]),
        dict(t=19.5 / 22, grip_rel=[12, -98], prop_angle=-92, lean=7, head=-3, foot_r_rel=[18, 84], foot_l_rel=[-17, 83]),
    ]),
}
for _k in LOOPS["chop"]["keys"]:
    _k.update(grip_w=1.0, foot_r_w=1.0, foot_l_w=1.0)
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/render.py -->
``````python
"""Frame rendering and video export."""
import math
import os
import random
import subprocess
import sys
from multiprocessing import Pool

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageChops

from . import engine as E
from .engine import Cam
from .figure import draw_pick
from .scene import Scene
from .track import is_color, parse_color

ART = {}


def art(name):
    """register static artwork: fn(img, cam, props, t)."""
    def deco(fn):
        ART[name] = fn
        return fn
    return deco


def col(v, alpha=1.0):
    if v is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)):
        c = tuple(int(x) for x in v) + (255,) * (4 - len(v))
    else:
        c = parse_color(v)
    return c[:3] + (int(c[3] * min(max(alpha, 0.0), 1.0)),)


def _layered(img, fn, alpha, blend):
    if alpha >= 0.999 and blend == "normal":
        fn(img)
        return
    layer = Image.new("RGBA", img.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    fn(layer)
    if alpha < 0.999:
        a = layer.getchannel("A").point(lambda v: int(v * max(alpha, 0.0)))
        layer.putalpha(a)
    if blend == "add":
        rgb = ImageChops.add(img.convert("RGB"), Image.composite(layer.convert("RGB"), Image.new("RGB", img.size), layer.getchannel("A")))
        img.paste(rgb.convert("RGBA"))
    else:
        img.alpha_composite(layer)


_SPRITES = {}


def _sprite(path):
    if path not in _SPRITES:
        _SPRITES[path] = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA")
    return _SPRITES[path]


def _rot_pts(pts, c, deg, scale=1.0):
    r = math.radians(deg)
    cs, sn = math.cos(r), math.sin(r)
    return [(c[0] + (x * cs - y * sn) * scale, c[1] + (x * sn + y * cs) * scale) for x, y in pts]


def draw_kind(img, kind, p, cam, t, scene, override=None):
    """draw one object of `kind` with evaluated props p."""
    if override:
        p = dict(p, **override)
    a = p["alpha"]
    ow = p.get("ow", 2)
    if kind == "rect":
        w, h = p["w"], p["h"]
        if p["anchor"] == "topleft":
            cx, cy = p["x"] + w / 2, p["y"] + h / 2
        else:
            cx, cy = p["x"], p["y"]
        pts = _rot_pts([(-w / 2, -h / 2), (w / 2, -h / 2), (w / 2, h / 2), (-w / 2, h / 2)], (cx, cy), p["rot"])
        E.poly(img, pts, cam, fill=col(p["fill"]), outline=col(p["outline"]), width=ow)
    elif kind == "circle":
        E.circle(img, (p["x"], p["y"]), p["r"], cam, fill=col(p["fill"]), outline=col(p["outline"]), width=ow)
    elif kind == "ellipse":
        E.ellipse(img, (p["x"], p["y"]), p["rx"], p["ry"], cam, fill=col(p["fill"]), outline=col(p["outline"]), width=ow, rot=math.radians(p["rot"]))
    elif kind == "line":
        E.line(img, (p["x"], p["y"]), (p["x2"], p["y2"]), cam, col(p["color"]), p["width"])
    elif kind == "poly":
        pts = _rot_pts(p["points"], (p["x"], p["y"]), p["rot"], p["scale"])
        E.poly(img, pts, cam, fill=col(p["fill"]), outline=col(p["outline"]), width=ow)
    elif kind == "text":
        g = p["grad"]
        s_ = str(p["text"])
        if p.get("reveal") is not None:
            s_ = s_[:max(0, int(p["reveal"]))]
        if not s_:
            return
        E.text(img, s_, (p["x"], p["y"]), p["size"], cam, fill=col(p["fill"]), outline=col(p["outline"]), ow=ow,
               track=p["track"], weight=p["weight"], align=p["align"], grad=tuple(g) if g else None, shadow=col(p["shadow"]), width=p["width"], alpha=a)
    elif kind == "glow":
        E.glow(img, (p["x"], p["y"]), p["r"], cam, col(p["color"])[:3], p["strength"] * a, p["power"])
    elif kind == "ring":
        x, y = cam.p(p["x"], p["y"])
        R = cam.k(p["r"])
        ImageDraw.Draw(img, "RGBA").ellipse([x - R, y - R, x + R, y + R], outline=col(p["color"]), width=max(1, int(round(cam.k(p["w"])))))
    elif kind == "rays":
        for i in range(int(p["n"])):
            ang = math.radians(p["rot"] + 360.0 * i / p["n"])
            d = (math.cos(ang), math.sin(ang))
            E.line(img, (p["x"] + d[0] * p["r0"], p["y"] + d[1] * p["r0"]), (p["x"] + d[0] * p["r1"], p["y"] + d[1] * p["r1"]),
                   cam, col(p["color"]), p["w"])
    elif kind == "star":
        E.star(img, (p["x"], p["y"]), p["r"], cam, col(p["color"]), p["thin"])
    elif kind == "sprite":
        path = p["file"] if os.path.isabs(p["file"]) else os.path.join(scene.base_dir, p["file"])
        im = _sprite(path)
        k = cam.z * E.S * p["scale"]
        im2 = im.resize((max(1, int(im.width * k)), max(1, int(im.height * k))), Image.LANCZOS)
        if p["rot"]:
            im2 = im2.rotate(-p["rot"], expand=True, resample=Image.BICUBIC)
        cx, cy = cam.p(p["x"], p["y"])
        img.paste(im2, (int(cx - im2.width / 2), int(cy - im2.height / 2)), im2)
    elif kind == "pick":
        draw_pick(img, (p["x"], p["y"]), p["angle"], p["len"], cam)
    elif kind == "phone":
        from .items import draw_phone
        draw_phone(img, p, cam, t, scene)
    elif kind == "burst":
        _burst(img, p, cam, t, scene)
    elif kind == "pulse":
        _pulse(img, p, cam, t, scene)
    elif kind == "art":
        fn = ART.get(p["name"])
        if fn is None:
            raise KeyError(f"unknown art '{p['name']}'. Registered: {', '.join(ART)}")
        fn(img, cam, p, t)


def _origin(at, te, scene):
    if isinstance(at, str):
        return scene.resolve(at, te)
    return at


def _burst(img, p, cam, t, scene):
    times = scene.times(p["times"])
    for ei, te in enumerate(times):
        age = t - te
        if age < 0 or age > p["life"]:
            continue
        ox, oy = _origin(p["at"], te, scene)
        rng = random.Random(p["seed"] * 7919 + ei)
        c = p["color"]
        for i in range(int(p["n"])):
            sp = rng.uniform(*p["speed"])
            an = math.radians(rng.uniform(*p["angle"]))
            sz = rng.uniform(*p["size"])
            life = p["life"] * rng.uniform(0.6, 1.0)
            if age > life:
                continue
            x = ox + math.cos(an) * sp * age
            y = oy + math.sin(an) * sp * age + 0.5 * p["gravity"] * age * age
            f = 1 - age / life
            E.circle(img, (x, y), sz * (0.6 + 0.4 * f), cam, fill=col(c if isinstance(c, str) else c[i % len(c)], f))


def _pulse(img, p, cam, t, scene):
    for te in scene.times(p["times"]):
        age = t - te
        if age < 0 or age > p["dur"]:
            continue
        f = 1 - age / p["dur"]
        ox, oy = _origin(p["at"], te, scene)
        for r, c, s in zip(p["r"], p["color"], p["strength"]):
            E.glow(img, (ox, oy), r, cam, col(c)[:3], s * f, 1.3)


def draw_actor(img, actor, cam, t, scene):
    pose, anch, p = actor.solve(t)
    hooks = {}
    for pname, ang_key, hand_key in (("prop", "prop_angle_world", p["prop_hand"]), ("prop_l", "prop_l_angle_world", "l")):
        obj = scene.objects.get(p[pname]) if p[pname] else None
        if obj is None:
            continue
        if hand_key == "auto":
            hand_key = "grip" if p["grip_w"] >= 0.5 else "r"
        g = anch["grip"] if hand_key == "grip" else anch["hand_" + hand_key]
        arm = "arm_r" if hand_key in ("r", "grip") else "arm_l"
        if hand_key == "grip":
            arm = "arm_r" if pose["depth"]["arm_r"] <= pose["depth"]["arm_l"] else "arm_l"
        hp = obj.props(t)
        ang = anch[ang_key]

        def draw_it(base, obj=obj, hp=hp, g=g, ang=ang):
            draw_kind(base, obj.kind, hp, cam, t, scene, override=dict(x=g[0], y=g[1], angle=ang, rot=ang))
        hooks[arm] = draw_it
    if p["shadow"]:
        sh = 1.0 - min(p["lift"] / 120.0, 0.8)
        E.ellipse(img, (p["x"], p["ground"]), 30 * sh, 5.5 * sh, cam, fill=(0, 0, 0, int(55 * sh)))
    from .figure3d import draw_figure
    pose["tint"] = _tint(p["tint"])
    from .faces import talk_amount
    talk = talk_amount(t, p["talk_seed"]) if p["talk"] else None
    draw_figure(img, pose, cam, face=p["expr"], talk_open=talk, faces=scene.faces, item_hooks=hooks, t=t, blink=p["blink"], depth_shade=p["depth_shade"])


def _tint(v):
    if v is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(v, str):
        c = parse_color(v)
        return tuple(x / 255.0 for x in c[:3])
    return tuple(v)


def _sharp(o, p):
    """objects drawn crisp on top of the motion-blurred picture: text and screen-space overlays, unless blur is set."""
    if p.get("blur") is not None:
        return not p["blur"]
    return o.kind == "text" or p["space"] == "screen"


def render_frame(scene, t, S=None, layer="all"):
    c = scene.canvas
    E.S = S or c["scale"]
    W, H = int(c["w"] * E.S), int(c["h"] * E.S)
    img = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), col(c["bg"]) if layer != "sharp" else (0, 0, 0, 0))
    cv = scene.cam_at(t)
    world = Cam(cv["zoom"], cv["cx"], cv["cy"], cv["tx"], cv["ty"])
    screen = Cam(1.0, c["w"] / 2, c["h"] / 2, c["w"] / 2, c["h"] / 2)
    held = {a.params(t)["prop"] for a in scene.actors.values()}
    items = []
    for i, o in enumerate(scene.objects.values()):
        if o.name in scene.held_names or not o.visible(t):
            continue
        items.append((o.track.get("z", t, 0), i, "obj", o))
    for j, a in enumerate(scene.actors.values()):
        items.append((a.params(t)["z"], 1000 + j, "actor", a))
    items.sort(key=lambda it: (it[0], it[1]))
    for _, _, typ, thing in items:
        if typ == "actor":
            if layer != "sharp":
                draw_actor(img, thing, world, t, scene)
            continue
        p = thing.props(t)
        if layer != "all" and _sharp(thing, p) != (layer == "sharp"):
            continue
        cam = screen if p["space"] == "screen" else world
        a = p["alpha"] if thing.kind in ("rect", "circle", "ellipse", "line", "poly", "ring", "rays", "star", "sprite", "pick", "art", "burst") else 1.0
        if a <= 0.001 and thing.kind not in ("burst", "pulse"):
            continue
        blend = "add" if p["blend"] == "add" else "normal"
        _layered(img, lambda im, th=thing, pp=p, cm=cam: draw_kind(im, th.kind, pp, cm, t, scene), a if thing.kind != "text" else 1.0, blend)
    return img


def frame_rgb(scene, n):
    """final frame n as an RGB PIL image at canvas size, with motion blur (canvas.blur sub-frames)."""
    c = scene.canvas
    blur = int(c["blur"])
    t0 = n / scene.fps
    if blur <= 1:
        im = render_frame(scene, t0)
    else:
        acc = None
        for i in range(blur):
            ti = t0 + ((i + 0.5) / blur - 0.5) / scene.fps
            arr = np.asarray(render_frame(scene, max(ti, 0.0), layer="blur").convert("RGB"), dtype=np.float32)
            acc = arr if acc is None else acc + arr
        im = Image.fromarray((acc / blur).astype(np.uint8)).convert("RGBA")
        im.alpha_composite(render_frame(scene, t0, layer="sharp"))
    ow, oh = c["out"] if c.get("out") else (c["w"], c["h"])
    im = im.convert("RGB").resize((ow, oh), Image.LANCZOS)
    post = c.get("post")
    if post:
        arr = np.asarray(im, dtype=np.float32)
        if post.get("vignette"):
            yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:oh, 0:ow]
            d = np.sqrt(((xx - ow / 2) / (ow / 2)) ** 2 + ((yy - oh / 2) / (oh / 2)) ** 2) / 1.41
            arr = arr * (1 - post["vignette"] * np.clip(d, 0, 1) ** 2.2)[..., None]
        if post.get("grain"):
            rng = np.random.default_rng(n)
            arr = arr + rng.normal(0, 255 * post["grain"], arr.shape[:2])[..., None]
        im = Image.fromarray(np.clip(arr, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8))
    return im


_G = {}


def _init(path):
    _G["scene"] = Scene.load(path)


def _work(n):
    return n, frame_rgb(_G["scene"], n).tobytes()


def render_video(path, out, start=None, end=None, workers=2, png_dir=None, crf=18, audio=True):
    scene = Scene.load(path)
    c = scene.canvas
    n0 = int(round((start or 0) * scene.fps))
    n1 = int(round((end if end is not None else scene.duration) * scene.fps))
    frames = list(range(n0, n1))
    ff = None
    if out:
        ff = subprocess.Popen(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgb24", "-s", f"{(c['out'] or [c['w'], c['h']])[0]}x{(c['out'] or [c['w'], c['h']])[1]}",
                               "-r", str(scene.fps), "-i", "-", "-vf", "scale=out_color_matrix=bt709:out_range=tv,format=yuv420p",
                               "-c:v", "libx264", "-crf", str(crf), "-colorspace", "bt709", "-color_primaries", "bt709",
                               "-color_trc", "bt709", "-movflags", "+faststart", out], stdin=subprocess.PIPE)
    if png_dir:
        os.makedirs(png_dir, exist_ok=True)
    with Pool(workers, initializer=_init, initargs=(path,)) as pool:
        for k, (n, raw) in enumerate(pool.imap(_work, frames, chunksize=2)):
            if ff:
                ff.stdin.write(raw)
            if png_dir:
                Image.frombytes("RGB", tuple(c["out"] or (c["w"], c["h"])), raw).save(os.path.join(png_dir, f"f{n:04d}.png"))
            if k % 15 == 0:
                print(f"frame {n}/{n1}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
    if ff:
        ff.stdin.close()
        ff.wait()
        if audio and scene.audio and out:
            from . import audio as A
            import tempfile
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
                wav = os.path.join(td, "mix.wav")
                A.write_wav(wav, A.mix(scene, n0 / scene.fps, n1 / scene.fps))
                tmp = os.path.join(td, "muxed.mp4")
                A.mux(out, wav, tmp)
                os.replace(tmp, out) if os.path.dirname(tmp) == os.path.dirname(out) else subprocess.run(["cp", tmp, out], check=True)
            print("audio mixed in", file=sys.stderr)
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/rotofit.py -->
``````python
"""Rotoscope fit: find stick-figure rig parameters whose silhouette matches the white figure in a reference frame.

Mask = near-white, not yellow pixels. Loss = 1 - IoU on a region around the figure (don't-care boxes are ignored).
Search = coordinate descent with shrinking steps, tracking from the previous frame; knee/elbow bend sides are
chosen from the 16 combinations. Pure numpy + OpenCV, about 0.3-1 s per figure per frame.
"""
import math
import itertools
import numpy as np
import cv2
from .figure import ik2, UPPER_ARM, FORE_ARM, THIGH, SHIN, LIMB_ARM, LIMB_LEG, LIMB_TORSO, HEAD_R

TL, HL = 58.0, 19.0


def white_mask(rgb):
    r = rgb[..., 0].astype(np.int16)
    g = rgb[..., 1].astype(np.int16)
    b = rgb[..., 2].astype(np.int16)
    mn = np.minimum(np.minimum(r, g), b)
    m = ((mn >= 172) & (b >= r - 4) & (b >= g - 2)).astype(np.uint8)
    return cv2.morphologyEx(m, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, np.ones((5, 5), np.uint8))


def build(p, bends):
    hip = (p[0], p[1])
    a = p[2]
    neck = (hip[0] + TL * math.sin(a), hip[1] - TL * math.cos(a))
    hd = a + p[3]
    head = (neck[0] + HL * math.sin(hd), neck[1] - HL * math.cos(hd))
    hands = [(p[4], p[5]), (p[6], p[7])]
    feet = [(p[8], p[9]), (p[10], p[11])]
    return dict(head=head, neck=neck, hip=hip, hands=hands, feet=feet, eb=list(bends[:2]), kb=list(bends[2:]))


def limbs(pose):
    hip, neck = pose["hip"], pose["neck"]
    sh = (neck[0], neck[1] + 8)
    out = []
    stretch = 0.0
    for i in (0, 1):
        f = pose["feet"][i]
        d = math.hypot(f[0] - hip[0], f[1] - hip[1])
        stretch += max(0.0, d / (THIGH + SHIN) - 1.08)
        out.append(([hip, ik2(hip, f, THIGH, SHIN, pose["kb"][i]), f], LIMB_LEG))
    out.append(([neck, hip], LIMB_TORSO))
    for i in (0, 1):
        h = pose["hands"][i]
        d = math.hypot(h[0] - sh[0], h[1] - sh[1])
        stretch += max(0.0, d / (UPPER_ARM + FORE_ARM) - 1.08)
        out.append(([sh, ik2(sh, h, UPPER_ARM, FORE_ARM, pose["eb"][i]), h], LIMB_ARM))
    return out, stretch


def draw_mask(pose, box, shape):
    """binary mask of the figure inside box = (x0, y0, x1, y1)."""
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = box
    m = np.zeros((y1 - y0, x1 - x0), np.uint8)
    ls, stretch = limbs(pose)
    for pts, w in ls:
        P = [(int(round(x - x0)), int(round(y - y0))) for x, y in pts]
        for a, b in zip(P[:-1], P[1:]):
            cv2.line(m, a, b, 1, int(round(w)))
        for q in P:
            cv2.circle(m, q, int(round(w / 2)), 1, -1)
    c = pose["head"]
    cv2.circle(m, (int(round(c[0] - x0)), int(round(c[1] - y0))), int(HEAD_R), 1, -1)
    return m, stretch


STEPS = np.array([4, 4, 0.07, 0.08, 5, 5, 5, 5, 5, 5, 5, 5], dtype=np.float64)


class Fitter:
    def __init__(self, dontcare=()):
        self.dontcare = dontcare

    def region(self, ref, p, pad=60):
        pose = build(p, (1, 1, 1, 1))
        xs = [pose["head"][0], pose["hip"][0]] + [h[0] for h in pose["hands"]] + [f[0] for f in pose["feet"]]
        ys = [pose["head"][1], pose["hip"][1]] + [h[1] for h in pose["hands"]] + [f[1] for f in pose["feet"]]
        H, W = ref.shape
        return (max(0, int(min(xs)) - pad), max(0, int(min(ys)) - pad), min(W, int(max(xs)) + pad), min(H, int(max(ys)) + pad))

    def prep(self, ref, box, other, care):
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = box
        r = ref[y0:y1, x0:x1].copy()
        c = care[y0:y1, x0:x1] if care is not None else np.ones_like(r)
        r = r * c
        o = other[y0:y1, x0:x1] if other is not None else None
        dref = cv2.distanceTransform((r == 0).astype(np.uint8), cv2.DIST_L2, 3)
        return r, c, o, dref

    def loss(self, ref, p, bends, box, other=None, care=None, prior=None, lam=0.0, cache=None):
        pose = build(p, bends)
        m, stretch = draw_mask(pose, box, ref.shape)
        r, c, o, dref = cache
        if o is not None:
            m = np.maximum(m, o)
        m = m * c
        inter = np.count_nonzero(m & r)
        union = np.count_nonzero(m | r)
        iou = inter / max(1, union)
        nm = max(1, np.count_nonzero(m))
        nr = max(1, np.count_nonzero(r))
        dmine = cv2.distanceTransform((m == 0).astype(np.uint8), cv2.DIST_L2, 3)
        A = float((m * dref).sum()) / nm
        B = float((r * dmine).sum()) / nr
        L = 1.0 - iou + 0.03 * min(A + B, 40.0) + 0.6 * stretch
        shp = (pose['neck'][0], pose['neck'][1] + 8)
        pen = 0.0
        for h in pose['hands']:
            pen += max(0.0, 40.0 - math.hypot(h[0] - shp[0], h[1] - shp[1]))
        for f in pose['feet']:
            pen += max(0.0, 46.0 - math.hypot(f[0] - pose['hip'][0], f[1] - pose['hip'][1]))
        L += 0.025 * pen
        ht = getattr(self, 'ht', None)
        if ht is not None:
            L += 0.012 * min(40.0, math.hypot(pose['head'][0] - ht[0], pose['head'][1] - ht[1]))
        if prior is not None and lam > 0:
            L += lam * float(np.sum(((p - prior) / STEPS) ** 2)) / len(p)
        return L

    def descend(self, ref, q, b, box, other, care, prior, lam, cache, rounds=10):
        Lq = self.loss(ref, q, b, box, other, care, prior, lam, cache)
        step = STEPS.copy()
        for _ in range(rounds):
            improved = False
            for i in range(len(q)):
                for sgn in (1, -1):
                    r = q.copy()
                    r[i] += sgn * step[i]
                    Lr = self.loss(ref, r, b, box, other, care, prior, lam, cache)
                    if Lr < Lq - 1e-5:
                        q, Lq, improved = r, Lr, True
                        break
            if not improved:
                step *= 0.55
            if step[0] < 0.4:
                break
        return q, Lq

    def fit(self, ref, p0, bends0, care=None, other=None, prior=None, lam=0.002, rounds=10, try_bends=True, restarts=5, seed=0, trigger=0.30):
        p = np.array(p0, dtype=np.float64)
        box = self.region(ref, p, 80)
        cache = self.prep(ref, box, other, care)
        cands = [tuple(bends0)]
        if try_bends:
            cands += [c for c in itertools.product((1, -1), repeat=4) if c != tuple(bends0)]
        scored = sorted(((self.loss(ref, p, b, box, other, care, prior, lam, cache) + (0.0 if b == tuple(bends0) else 0.035 * sum(x != y for x, y in zip(b, bends0))), b) for b in cands), key=lambda t: t[0])
        results = []
        for L0, b in scored[:3]:
            q, Lq = self.descend(ref, p.copy(), b, box, other, care, prior, lam, cache, rounds)
            results.append((Lq + (0.0 if b == tuple(bends0) else 0.035 * sum(x != y for x, y in zip(b, bends0))), b, q))
        results.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])
        L, b, q = results[0]
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        k = 0
        while L > trigger and k < restarts:
            k += 1
            q0 = q + rng.normal(0, 1, len(q)) * STEPS * 2.2
            for bb in (b,) + tuple(c for _, c, _ in results[1:2]):
                q2, L2 = self.descend(ref, q0.copy(), bb, box, other, care, prior, lam, cache, rounds)
                if L2 < L:
                    L, b, q = L2, bb, q2
        return q, b, L
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/scene.py -->
``````python
"""Scene file loading, validation and evaluation (time -> things to draw). See `python -m stickreel docs`."""
import difflib
import importlib
import json
import math
import os
import numpy as np

from .ease import periodic_pchip
from .poses import POSES, LOOPS
from .skeleton import DEFAULTS, LIMBS, pad_limb, solve
from .faces import FACES
from .track import Track, SceneError

CANVAS_DEFAULT = dict(w=576, h=1024, fps=30, duration=5.0, scale=2.0, bg="#000000", blur=1, out=None, post=None)
COMMON = dict(z=0, show=None, alpha=1.0, space="world", follow=None, offset=[0, 0], blend="normal", grow=None, blur=None)

# kind -> default props. Anything not listed here is rejected, with a did-you-mean hint.
KINDS = {
    "rect": dict(x=288, y=512, w=100, h=100, rot=0, fill="#ffffff", outline=None, ow=2, anchor="center"),
    "circle": dict(x=288, y=512, r=40, fill="#ffffff", outline=None, ow=2),
    "ellipse": dict(x=288, y=512, rx=50, ry=30, rot=0, fill="#ffffff", outline=None, ow=2),
    "line": dict(x=0, y=0, x2=100, y2=100, color="#ffffff", width=3),
    "poly": dict(points=[[0, 0], [50, 0], [25, 40]], x=0, y=0, rot=0, scale=1.0, fill="#ffffff", outline=None, ow=2),
    "text": dict(text="TEXT", x=288, y=512, size=24, fill="#ffffff", outline="#000000", ow=2, track=0.0, width=None,
                 weight=800, grad=None, shadow=None, align="center", reveal=None),
    "glow": dict(x=288, y=512, r=60, color="#ffd27a", strength=0.8, power=1.5),
    "ring": dict(x=288, y=512, r=50, w=3, color="#ffffff"),
    "rays": dict(x=288, y=512, r0=20, r1=200, n=16, w=3, color="#ffffff", rot=0),
    "star": dict(x=288, y=512, r=8, color="#ffffff", thin=0.18),
    "sprite": dict(file=None, x=288, y=512, scale=1.0, rot=0),
    "pick": dict(x=288, y=512, angle=-45, len=64),
    "phone": dict(x=288, y=512, rot=-90, w=26, h=48, scale=1.0, color="#2a2a30", grip_at=0.35, video=None, video_start=0.0,
                  video_speed=1.0, loop=True, screen_color="#101018", len=48),
    "burst": dict(times=[], at=[288, 512], n=10, speed=[40, 140], angle=[-150, -30], gravity=420, life=0.5,
                  size=[1.5, 3.5], color="#8a5a2a", seed=1),
    "pulse": dict(times=[], at=[288, 512], r=[46, 16], color=["#ffd278", "#fffadc"], strength=[0.8, 0.95], dur=0.18),
    "art": dict(name=None),
}
ACTOR_EXTRA = ("keys", "kind")


def _suggest(name, options):
    m = difflib.get_close_matches(name, list(options), n=3, cutoff=0.5)
    return f" Did you mean: {', '.join(m)}?" if m else ""


def _check(where, props, allowed):
    for p in props:
        if p not in allowed:
            raise SceneError(f"{where}: unknown property '{p}'.{_suggest(p, allowed)} Allowed: {', '.join(sorted(allowed))}")


EFFECTORS = ("hand_r", "hand_l", "foot_r", "foot_l")


def _norm_actor_key(k, poses, where):
    """expand `pose`, and make reach targets switch their own weight on/off."""
    k = dict(k)
    if "pose" in k:
        name = k.pop("pose")
        if name not in poses:
            raise SceneError(f"{where}: unknown pose '{name}'.{_suggest(name, poses)} Known: {', '.join(poses)}")
        base = {p: DEFAULTS[p] for p in ("arm_r", "arm_l", "leg_r", "leg_l", "lean", "head", "rot")}
        base.update(poses[name])
        for p, v in base.items():
            k.setdefault(p, v)
    if "face" in k:
        f = k.pop("face")
        if f not in ("left", "right"):
            raise SceneError(f"{where}: 'face' is 'left' or 'right' (or use numeric 'yaw' in degrees), got {f!r}")
        k.setdefault("yaw", 180.0 if f == "left" else 0.0)
    for limb in LIMBS:
        if limb in k and k[limb] is not None:
            k[limb] = pad_limb(limb, k[limb])
    for e in EFFECTORS:
        for suffix in ("_at", "_rel"):
            if e + suffix in k:
                if k[e + suffix] is None:
                    k.pop(e + suffix)
                    k.setdefault(e + "_w", 0.0)
                else:
                    k.setdefault(e + "_w", 1.0)
    if any(k.get(e + "_at") is not None for e in ("hand_r", "hand_l")) and "grip_rel" not in k and "grip_w" not in k:
        k["grip_w"] = 0.0                       # an explicit hand target releases any two-handed grip
    if "grip_rel" in k:
        if k["grip_rel"] is None:
            k.pop("grip_rel")
            k.setdefault("grip_w", 0.0)
        else:
            k.setdefault("grip_w", 1.0)
    if "grip" in k:
        raise SceneError(f"{where}: use 'grip_rel': [dx, dy] (hip-relative) for a two-handed grip, not 'grip'")
    return k


def _prop_lookup(d, key):
    return d[key] if key in d else None


class Actor:
    def __init__(self, name, spec, scene):
        self.name, self.scene = name, scene
        spec = dict(spec)
        keys = spec.pop("keys", [])
        spec.pop("kind", None)
        where = f"actor '{name}'"
        _check(where, list(spec), set(DEFAULTS) | set(ACTOR_EXTRA) | {"face"})
        self.segs = []                                  # anim segments
        nkeys = []
        for i, k in enumerate(keys):
            w = f"{where} key {i} (t={k.get('t')})"
            _check(w, [p for p in k if p not in ("pose", "hits", "ease", "eases", "t")], set(DEFAULTS) | {"face"})
            k = _norm_actor_key(k, scene.poses, w)
            hits = k.pop("hits", None)
            if "anim" in k:
                a = k["anim"]
                if a is not None and a not in scene.loops:
                    raise SceneError(f"{w}: unknown anim '{a}'.{_suggest(a, scene.loops)} Known: {', '.join(scene.loops)}")
                self.segs.append(dict(t=k["t"], name=a, hits=sorted(hits) if hits else None))
            elif hits is not None:
                raise SceneError(f"{w}: 'hits' only goes with an 'anim' in the same key")
            nkeys.append(k)
        self.segs.sort(key=lambda s: s["t"])
        static = _norm_actor_key(spec, scene.poses, where) if spec else {}
        self.track = Track(static, nkeys, where, DEFAULTS)
        self._dist = None
        self._cache = {}

    # ---- parameters over time
    def base(self, t):
        return {p: self.track.get(p, t, DEFAULTS[p]) for p in DEFAULTS}

    def dist(self, t):
        if self._dist is None:
            step = 1 / 120
            n = int(self.scene.duration / step) + 2
            xs = np.array([self.track.get("x", i * step, DEFAULTS["x"]) for i in range(n)])
            self._dist = (step, np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(np.abs(np.diff(xs)))]))
        step, cum = self._dist
        return float(np.interp(t / step, np.arange(len(cum)), cum))

    def phase(self, loop, seg, t, speed):
        if loop["source"] == "distance":
            return ((self.dist(t) - self.dist(seg["t"])) / loop["stride"]) % 1.0
        hits = seg.get("hits")
        if loop["source"] == "hits" and hits:
            ph = loop["hit_phase"]
            if len(hits) == 1:
                return (ph + (t - hits[0]) / loop["period"]) % 1.0
            i = 0
            while i < len(hits) - 2 and t >= hits[i + 1]:
                i += 1
            return (ph + (t - hits[i]) / (hits[i + 1] - hits[i])) % 1.0
        return ((t - seg["t"]) * speed / loop["period"]) % 1.0

    def loop_values(self, loop, phase):
        out = {}
        props = {}
        for k in loop["keys"]:
            for p, v in k.items():
                if p != "t":
                    props.setdefault(p, []).append((k["t"], v))
        for p, pts in props.items():
            xs = [a for a, _ in pts]
            vals = [b for _, b in pts]
            if isinstance(vals[0], (list, tuple)):
                out[p] = [periodic_pchip(xs, [v[i] for v in vals], 1.0, phase) for i in range(len(vals[0]))]
            elif isinstance(vals[0], (int, float)):
                out[p] = periodic_pchip(xs, vals, 1.0, phase)
        return out

    def params(self, t):
        key = round(t, 6)
        if key in self._cache:
            return self._cache[key]
        base = self.base(t)
        res = base
        idx = -1
        for i, s in enumerate(self.segs):
            if s["t"] <= t:
                idx = i
        if idx >= 0:
            cur = self.segs[idx]
            prev = self.segs[idx - 1] if idx > 0 else None
            bl = max(base["anim_blend"], 1e-3)
            w = 1.0 if (cur["t"] <= 0 and prev is None) else min(max((t - cur["t"]) / bl, 0.0), 1.0)
            w = w * w * (3 - 2 * w)

            def applied(seg):
                if seg is None or seg["name"] is None:
                    return base
                loop = self.scene.loops[seg["name"]]
                vals = self.loop_values(loop, self.phase(loop, seg, t, base["anim_speed"]))
                out = dict(base)
                for p, v in vals.items():
                    b = base[p]
                    if isinstance(v, list):
                        o = list(b)
                        for i, y in enumerate(v):
                            o[i] = (o[i] + y) if loop["mode"] == "add" else y
                        out[p] = o
                    else:
                        out[p] = b + v if loop["mode"] == "add" else v
                return out

            a, b = applied(prev), applied(cur)
            res = dict(base)
            for p in DEFAULTS:
                va, vb = a[p], b[p]
                if isinstance(va, list) and isinstance(vb, list):
                    res[p] = [x + (y - x) * w for x, y in zip(va, vb)]
                elif isinstance(va, (int, float)) and isinstance(vb, (int, float)) and not isinstance(va, bool):
                    res[p] = va + (vb - va) * w
                else:
                    res[p] = vb if w >= 0.5 else va
        self._cache[key] = res
        return res

    def hit_times(self):
        out = []
        for i, s in enumerate(self.segs):
            end = self.segs[i + 1]["t"] if i + 1 < len(self.segs) else 1e9
            out += [h for h in (s.get("hits") or []) if s["t"] <= h < end]
        return sorted(out)

    def step_times(self):
        """times a foot lands: where a distance-locked loop (walk/run) crosses phase 0 or 0.5 while moving."""
        out = []
        dt = 1 / 240
        for i, seg in enumerate(self.segs):
            loop = self.scene.loops.get(seg["name"]) if seg["name"] else None
            if not loop or loop["source"] != "distance":
                continue
            end = self.segs[i + 1]["t"] if i + 1 < len(self.segs) else self.scene.duration
            prev_cell = None
            t = seg["t"]
            while t < end:
                cell = int(self.phase_raw(loop, seg, t) * 2)
                if prev_cell is not None and cell > prev_cell:
                    out.append(round(t, 4))
                prev_cell = cell
                t += dt
        return out

    def phase_raw(self, loop, seg, t):
        return (self.dist(t) - self.dist(seg["t"])) / loop["stride"]

    def solve(self, t):
        p = self.params(t)
        pose, anch = solve(p, lambda ref: self.scene.resolve(ref, t, exclude=self.name))
        c = anch["cos_yaw"]

        def world_angle(a):                            # item angle in facing space -> screen angle
            r = math.radians(a)
            return math.degrees(math.atan2(math.sin(r), math.cos(r) * c))

        anch["prop_angle_world"] = world_angle(p["prop_angle"])
        anch["prop_l_angle_world"] = world_angle(p["prop_l_angle"])
        anch["tip"] = anch["grip"]
        obj = self.scene.objects.get(p["prop"]) if p["prop"] else None
        if obj is not None and obj.kind in ("pick", "phone"):
            pr = obj.props(t)
            ln = pr["len"] if obj.kind == "pick" else pr["h"] * pr["scale"] * (1 - pr.get("grip_at", 0.35))
            a = math.radians(anch["prop_angle_world"])
            base = anch["grip"] if p["prop_hand"] in ("auto", "grip") else anch["hand_" + p["prop_hand"]]
            anch["tip"] = (base[0] + math.cos(a) * ln, base[1] + math.sin(a) * ln)
        return pose, anch, p


class Obj:
    def __init__(self, name, spec, scene):
        self.name, self.scene = name, scene
        spec = dict(spec)
        self.kind = spec.pop("kind", None)
        if self.kind not in KINDS:
            raise SceneError(f"object '{name}': unknown kind '{self.kind}'.{_suggest(str(self.kind), KINDS)} Known: {', '.join(KINDS)}")
        keys = spec.pop("keys", [])
        self.defaults = dict(COMMON)
        self.defaults.update(KINDS[self.kind])
        if self.kind == "art":
            self.defaults.update({k: None for k in spec if k not in self.defaults})
        allowed = set(self.defaults)
        _check(f"object '{name}' ({self.kind})", list(spec), allowed)
        for i, k in enumerate(keys):
            _check(f"object '{name}' key {i} (t={k.get('t')})", [p for p in k if p not in ("t", "ease", "eases")],
                   allowed | ({p for p in k} if self.kind == "art" else set()))
        self.track = Track(spec, keys, f"object '{name}'", self.defaults)
        self.artname = spec.get("name")

    def props(self, t):
        out = {p: self.track.get(p, t, d) for p, d in self.defaults.items()}
        for p in self.track.props:
            if p not in out:
                out[p] = self.track.get(p, t)
        g = out.get("grow")
        if g:
            for gg in (g if isinstance(g, list) else [g]):
                times = self.scene.times(gg["times"])
                tot = 0.0
                for th in times:
                    u = min(max((t - th) / max(gg.get("dur", 0.1), 1e-3), 0.0), 1.0)
                    tot += gg["by"] * u * u * (3 - 2 * u)
                out[gg["prop"]] = out[gg["prop"]] + tot
        if out.get("follow"):
            a = self.scene.resolve(out["follow"], t, exclude=self.name)
            if a is not None:
                off = out["offset"]
                out["x"], out["y"] = a[0] + off[0], a[1] + off[1]
        return out

    def visible(self, t):
        s = self.track.get("show", t, None)
        return s is None or (s[0] <= t < s[1])


class Scene:
    def __init__(self, data, base_dir="."):
        data = dict(data)
        self.base_dir = base_dir
        _check("scene", list(data), {"canvas", "poses", "loops", "camera", "actors", "objects", "plugins", "faces", "audio", "note", "meta"})
        self.canvas = dict(CANVAS_DEFAULT)
        _check("canvas", list(data.get("canvas", {})), set(CANVAS_DEFAULT))
        self.canvas.update(data.get("canvas", {}))
        self.duration = float(self.canvas["duration"])
        self.fps = int(self.canvas["fps"])
        self.faces = dict(FACES)
        self.faces.update(data.get("faces", {}))
        self.poses = dict(POSES)
        self.poses.update(data.get("poses", {}))
        self.loops = {k: dict(v) for k, v in LOOPS.items()}
        for k, v in data.get("loops", {}).items():
            v = dict(v)
            v.setdefault("mode", "add")
            v.setdefault("source", "time")
            v.setdefault("period", 1.0)
            v.setdefault("stride", 150)
            v.setdefault("hit_phase", 0.0)
            if "keys" not in v:
                raise SceneError(f"loop '{k}' needs 'keys' (phase 0..1)")
            self.loops[k] = v
        for m in data.get("plugins", []):
            importlib.import_module(m)
        cam = dict(data.get("camera", {}))
        ckeys = cam.pop("keys", [])
        self.shakes = cam.pop("shake", [])
        if isinstance(self.shakes, dict):
            self.shakes = [self.shakes]
        cdef = dict(zoom=1.0, cx=288.0, cy=512.0, tx=288.0, ty=512.0)
        _check("camera", list(cam) + [p for k in ckeys for p in k if p not in ("t", "ease", "eases")], set(cdef))
        self.camera = Track(cam, ckeys, "camera", cdef)
        self.cdef = cdef
        self.objects = {}
        for n, spec in data.get("objects", {}).items():
            self.objects[n] = Obj(n, spec, self)
        self.actors = {}
        for n, spec in data.get("actors", {}).items():
            self.actors[n] = Actor(n, spec, self)
        self._solving = set()
        self.audio = data.get("audio")
        if self.audio:
            from .audio import validate as _va
            _va(self.audio, _suggest)
        # objects named by any actor's `prop` are tools: drawn only while held
        self.held_names = {v for a in self.actors.values() for pr in ("prop", "prop_l") for (_, v, _) in a.track.lines.get(pr, []) if v}
        for a in self.actors.values():
            for pr in ("prop", "prop_l"):
                if a.track.static.get(pr):
                    self.held_names.add(a.track.static[pr])
            names = [e[1] for e in a.track.lines.get("expr", [])]
            if a.track.static.get("expr"):
                names.append(a.track.static["expr"])
            for n in [x for x in names if x is not None]:
                if n not in self.faces:
                    raise SceneError(f"actor '{a.name}': unknown expr '{n}'.{_suggest(n, self.faces)} Known: {', '.join(self.faces)}")

    # ---- references and events
    def times(self, ref):
        if isinstance(ref, list):
            return [float(x) for x in ref]
        if isinstance(ref, dict):
            if not {"from", "to", "every"} <= set(ref):
                raise SceneError("times range needs {\"from\":s, \"to\":s, \"every\":s}")
            n = int((ref["to"] - ref["from"]) / ref["every"] + 1e-9) + 1
            return [ref["from"] + i * ref["every"] for i in range(n)]
        if isinstance(ref, str) and ref.endswith(".step"):
            a = self.actors.get(ref[:-5].lstrip("@"))
            if a is None:
                raise SceneError(f"times '{ref}': no actor named '{ref[:-5]}'. Actors: {', '.join(self.actors)}")
            return a.step_times()
        if isinstance(ref, str) and ref.endswith(".hit"):
            a = self.actors.get(ref[:-4].lstrip("@"))
            if a is None:
                raise SceneError(f"times '{ref}': no actor named '{ref[:-4]}'. Actors: {', '.join(self.actors)}")
            return a.hit_times()
        raise SceneError(f"times must be a list of seconds, 'actor.hit', 'actor.step' or a {{from,to,every}} range, got {ref!r}")

    def resolve(self, ref, t, exclude=None):
        name, _, anchor = ref.lstrip("@").partition(".")
        if name in self.actors:
            if name == exclude and name in self._solving:
                return None
            if name in self._solving:
                return None
            self._solving.add(name)
            try:
                pose, anch, _ = self.actors[name].solve(t)
            finally:
                self._solving.discard(name)
            a = anchor or "hip"
            if a not in anch:
                raise SceneError(f"reference '{ref}': actors have anchors {', '.join(k for k in anch if k not in ('face', 'prop_angle_world'))}")
            return anch[a]
        if name in self.objects:
            if name == exclude:
                return None
            pr = self.objects[name].props(t)
            return (pr["x"], pr["y"])
        raise SceneError(f"reference '{ref}': no actor or object '{name}'. Have: {', '.join(list(self.actors) + list(self.objects))}")

    def cam_at(self, t):
        c = {p: self.camera.get(p, t, d) for p, d in self.cdef.items()}
        for sh in self.shakes:                      # {"times": "you.hit", "amp": 2.5, "dur": 0.14}
            for th in self.times(sh["times"]):
                u = (t - th) / max(sh.get("dur", 0.15), 1e-3)
                if 0 <= u < 1:
                    k = sh.get("amp", 2.0) * (1 - u) ** 2 / max(c["zoom"], 1e-3)
                    c["cx"] += k * math.sin(th * 37.0 + t * 90.0)
                    c["cy"] += k * math.cos(th * 53.0 + t * 110.0)
        return c

    @staticmethod
    def load(path):
        with open(path) as fh:
            return Scene(json.load(fh), os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path)))
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/sfx.py -->
``````python
"""Procedural sound-effects library for short cartoon/promo videos.

Pure numpy + stdlib. Every sound is synthesised from scratch (no sample files,
no network).

    render_sfx(name, pitch=1.0, seed=0) -> float32 mono array at SR, peak 0.9
    write_wav(path, samples)            -> 16-bit mono PCM WAV
    describe()                          -> {name: one-line description}
    SFX                                 -> {name: fn(rng, pitch)}

pitch is a varispeed multiplier: 2.0 is an octave up and twice as fast.

Self-test: python3 -m stickreel.sfx
"""

import difflib
import wave

import numpy as np

SR = 44100

SFX = {}
_DESC = {}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers

def _n(sec):
    """Number of samples for a duration in seconds (at pitch 1)."""
    return max(1, int(round(sec * SR)))


def _t(n):
    return np.arange(n) / SR


def _sine(freq, n, phase=0.0):
    """Sine whose frequency may vary per sample. Sample 0 has phase 0."""
    f = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(freq, dtype=float), (n,))
    ph = np.cumsum(f) - f  # exclusive running sum of frequency
    return np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * ph / SR + phase)


def _env_exp(n, tau):
    """Exponential decay with time constant tau (seconds)."""
    return np.exp(-_t(n) / tau)


def _attack(n, ms):
    """Linear fast attack over ms milliseconds."""
    return np.minimum(1.0, _t(n) / (ms / 1000.0))


def _noise(rng, n):
    return rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0, n)


def _lp(x, fc):
    """One-pole low-pass. fc may be a scalar or a per-sample array (Hz)."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = x.size
    fc = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(fc, dtype=float), (n,))
    a = 1.0 - np.exp(-2.0 * np.pi * np.clip(fc, 5.0, 0.45 * SR) / SR)
    out = np.empty(n)
    acc = 0.0
    for i, (xi, ai) in enumerate(zip(x.tolist(), a.tolist())):
        acc += ai * (xi - acc)
        out[i] = acc
    return out


def _hp(x, fc):
    """One-pole high-pass (input minus its low-pass)."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    return x - _lp(x, fc)


def _glide(f0, f1, n, tau):
    """Frequency falling/rising exponentially from f0 to f1 (tau in seconds)."""
    return f1 + (f0 - f1) * np.exp(-_t(n) / tau)


def _place(out, sig, start):
    """Add sig into out starting at sample start (truncated at the end)."""
    s = int(start)
    if s >= out.size:
        return
    m = min(sig.size, out.size - s)
    out[s:s + m] += sig[:m]
``````
