``````python
def _varispeed(x, pitch):
    """Resample so the sound plays pitch times faster (and pitch times higher)."""
    if pitch == 1.0:
        return x
    n_out = max(1, int(len(x) / pitch))
    src = np.arange(n_out) * pitch
    return np.interp(src, np.arange(len(x)), x)


def _finish(x, fi_ms=3.0, fo_ms=6.0):
    """Short linear fades at both ends (no clicks), then clip to [-1, 1]."""
    n = x.size
    fi = min(max(1, int(fi_ms * SR / 1000.0)), max(1, n // 4))
    fo = min(max(1, int(fo_ms * SR / 1000.0)), max(1, n // 4))
    env = np.ones(n)
    env[:fi] *= np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, fi, endpoint=False)
    env[n - fo:] *= np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, fo)
    return np.clip(x * env, -1.0, 1.0)


def _sfx(name, desc):
    """Register a native-pitch generator fn(rng) as SFX[name] = fn(rng, pitch)."""
    def deco(gen):
        def run(rng, pitch=1.0):
            x = _varispeed(gen(rng), float(pitch))
            return _finish(x).astype(np.float32)
        run.__doc__ = desc
        run.__name__ = name
        SFX[name] = run
        _DESC[name] = desc
        return gen
    return deco


def _clink_raw(n, ratio=1.0, amp=1.0):
    t = _t(n)
    y = 0.6 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 2300.0 * ratio * t)
    y += 0.4 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 3100.0 * ratio * t)
    tr = np.random.default_rng(7).standard_normal(n)         # metal transient: a few ms of band-passed noise
    tr = _lp(_hp(tr, 3000.0), 9000.0) * np.exp(-t / 0.003) * 0.9
    return amp * (y * np.exp(-t / 0.07) + tr) * _attack(n, 0.5)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- sounds

@_sfx("thunk", "pick hitting rock: low sine drop 120->50 Hz plus noise crack, 0.25 s")
def _thunk(rng):
    n = _n(0.25)
    t = _t(n)
    body = _sine(_glide(120.0, 50.0, n, 0.05), n) * np.exp(-t / 0.09) * 0.9
    crack = _lp(_hp(_noise(rng, n), 1200.0), 6000.0) * np.exp(-t / 0.008) * 1.3
    knock = _sine(_glide(260.0, 130.0, n, 0.03), n) * np.exp(-t / 0.045) * 0.9      # mid layer: phone speakers drop the sub
    return 0.7 * body + crack + knock


@_sfx("whoosh", "swing: band-limited noise, swelling then falling, filter sweeps up then down, 0.35 s")
def _whoosh(rng):
    n = _n(0.35)
    t = _t(n)
    fc = 300.0 + 2800.0 * np.sin(np.pi * t / 0.35) ** 2
    y = _lp(_lp(_noise(rng, n), fc), fc)
    y = _hp(y, 150.0)
    env = np.where(t < 0.16, (t / 0.16) ** 2, np.exp(-(t - 0.16) / 0.1))
    return y * env


@_sfx("dirt", "crumbling dirt: 5-9 tiny noise ticks scattered over 0.4 s, low-passed")
def _dirt(rng):
    n = _n(0.4)
    out = np.zeros(n)
    for _ in range(int(rng.integers(5, 10))):
        start = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.37) * SR
        ln = _n(rng.uniform(0.004, 0.009))
        burst = _noise(rng, ln) * np.exp(-_t(ln) / 0.0025) * rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0)
        _place(out, burst, start)
    return _lp(out, 2200.0)


@_sfx("clink", "single coin clink: two inharmonic sines near 2.3k and 3.1k Hz, fast decay, 0.3 s")
def _clink(rng):
    return _clink_raw(_n(0.3))


@_sfx("coins", "coin pour: 14-20 random clinks of varied pitch over 0.9 s")
def _coins(rng):
    n = _n(0.9)
    out = np.zeros(n)
    for _ in range(int(rng.integers(14, 21))):
        start = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.85) * SR
        ratio = rng.uniform(0.8, 1.3)
        amp = rng.uniform(0.4, 0.9)
        _place(out, _clink_raw(_n(0.3), ratio, amp), start)
    return out


@_sfx("ding", "clean bell: 880 Hz with 2.7x and 4.1x partials, 1.2 s decay")
def _ding(rng):
    n = _n(1.2)
    t = _t(n)
    y = np.sin(2 * np.pi * 880.0 * t) * np.exp(-t / 0.45)
    y += 0.35 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 880.0 * 2.7 * t) * np.exp(-t / 0.25)
    y += 0.15 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 880.0 * 4.1 * t) * np.exp(-t / 0.12)
    return y * _attack(n, 2.0)


@_sfx("sparkle", "magic sparkle: 3-5 fast rising bell glints over 0.6 s")
def _sparkle(rng):
    n = _n(0.6)
    out = np.zeros(n)
    k = int(rng.integers(3, 6))
    starts = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.38, k))
    ratios = np.sort(rng.uniform(1.0, 1.6, k))
    for s, r in zip(starts, ratios):
        m = _n(0.22)
        t = _t(m)
        f = 1100.0 * r
        tone = _sine(_glide(0.8 * f, f, m, 0.02), m) * np.exp(-t / 0.07)
        tone += 0.25 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 2 * f * t) * np.exp(-t / 0.03)
        _place(out, 0.6 * tone, s * SR)
    return out


@_sfx("riser", "rising sweep: noise and sine 200->1200 Hz, level rising, 1.2 s")
def _riser(rng):
    n = _n(1.2)
    u = _t(n) / 1.2
    tone = 0.5 * u ** 2 * _sine(200.0 * 6.0 ** u, n)
    noise = _lp(_noise(rng, n), 300.0 * 20.0 ** u) * u ** 1.5
    return tone + noise


@_sfx("boom", "flash impact: sine drop 90->35 Hz plus noise burst, long tail, 1.0 s")
def _boom(rng):
    n = _n(1.0)
    t = _t(n)
    body = _sine(35.0 + 55.0 * np.exp(-t / 0.08), n) * np.exp(-t / 0.35)
    body = 0.5 * body + 0.7 * np.tanh(3.0 * body)           # saturation adds harmonics a phone speaker can reproduce
    burst = _lp(_noise(rng, n), 1200.0) * np.exp(-t / 0.12) * 0.8
    mid = _lp(_noise(rng, n), 700.0) * np.exp(-t / 0.22) * 1.2 + _sine(_glide(180.0, 90.0, n, 0.12), n) * np.exp(-t / 0.25) * 0.8
    return 0.5 * body + burst + mid


@_sfx("shimmer", "soft ring expansion: detuned high sines, slow swell and decay, 1.0 s")
def _shimmer(rng):
    n = _n(1.0)
    t = _t(n)
    base = 1500.0 * (1.0 + 0.12 * (1.0 - np.exp(-t / 0.5)))
    y = np.zeros(n)
    for ratio, detune, amp in ((1.0, 0.0, 0.5), (1.5, 4.0, 0.25), (2.01, -5.0, 0.15)):
        y += amp * _sine(base * ratio + detune, n)
    env = (1.0 - np.exp(-t / 0.15)) * np.exp(-t / 0.4)
    return y * env


@_sfx("tick", "tiny typewriter click: high noise transient plus a short 3.2 kHz blip, 0.04 s")
def _tick(rng):
    n = _n(0.04)
    t = _t(n)
    click = _lp(_hp(_noise(rng, n), 1200.0), 6500.0) * np.exp(-t / 0.004)
    blip = 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 1900.0 * t) * np.exp(-t / 0.003)
    return click + blip


@_sfx("pop", "bubble pop: short sine pitch up 300->900 Hz, 0.1 s")
def _pop(rng):
    n = _n(0.1)
    t = _t(n)
    f = 900.0 - 600.0 * np.exp(-t / 0.025)
    return _sine(f, n) * np.exp(-t / 0.03)


@_sfx("step", "footstep: low thud with a soft noise puff, 0.12 s")
def _step(rng):
    n = _n(0.12)
    t = _t(n)
    thud = 0.9 * _sine(70.0 + 50.0 * np.exp(-t / 0.015), n) * np.exp(-t / 0.03)
    soft = 2.0 * _lp(_noise(rng, n), 700.0) * np.exp(-t / 0.04)
    return thud + soft


@_sfx("thump", "body hits floor: lower and softer than thunk, 0.2 s")
def _thump(rng):
    n = _n(0.2)
    t = _t(n)
    body = 0.8 * _sine(40.0 + 30.0 * np.exp(-t / 0.03), n) * np.exp(-t / 0.06)
    body = 0.5 * body + 0.7 * np.tanh(3.0 * body)
    soft = 1.5 * _lp(_noise(rng, n), 380.0) * np.exp(-t / 0.05)
    mid = _sine(_glide(190.0, 110.0, n, 0.03), n) * np.exp(-t / 0.05) * 0.9
    return 0.6 * body + soft + mid


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- API

def render_sfx(name, pitch=1.0, seed=0):
    """Render a named sound as float32 mono at SR, peak-normalised to 0.9."""
    if name not in SFX:
        close = difflib.get_close_matches(str(name), list(SFX), n=3, cutoff=0.4)
        hint = f" Did you mean {', '.join(close)}?" if close else ""
        raise KeyError(f"unknown sound {name!r}.{hint}")
    pitch = float(pitch)
    if not pitch > 0:
        raise ValueError(f"pitch must be > 0, got {pitch}")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = SFX[name](rng, pitch).astype(np.float64)
    peak = float(np.max(np.abs(x))) if x.size else 0.0
    if peak > 0:
        x *= 0.9 / peak
    return x.astype(np.float32)


def write_wav(path, samples):
    """Write float samples in [-1, 1] as 16-bit mono PCM WAV at SR."""
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(samples, dtype=float), -1.0, 1.0)
    pcm = np.round(x * 32767.0).astype("<i2")
    with wave.open(str(path), "wb") as w:
        w.setnchannels(1)
        w.setsampwidth(2)
        w.setframerate(SR)
        w.writeframes(pcm.tobytes())


def describe():
    """Return {name: one-line description}."""
    return dict(_DESC)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- self-test

def _dominant(x):
    spec = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(x * np.hanning(x.size)))
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(x.size, 1.0 / SR)
    spec[0] = 0.0
    return float(freqs[int(np.argmax(spec))])


def _selftest():
    import os

    outdir = "/tmp/sfx_test"
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
    problems = []
    print(f"{'name':<9}{'pitch':>6}{'sec':>7}{'peak':>7}{'rms':>7}{'dom_Hz':>9}")
    for name in SFX:
        for pitch in (1.0, 1.3):
            where = f"{name} @ {pitch}"
            x = render_sfx(name, pitch, seed=0).astype(np.float64)
            sec = x.size / SR
            peak = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(x ** 2)))
            dom = _dominant(x)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                problems.append(f"{where}: NaN/inf")
            if peak > 1.0:
                problems.append(f"{where}: peak {peak:.3f} > 1")
            if not (0.03 <= sec <= 1.6):
                problems.append(f"{where}: duration {sec:.3f} s out of range")
            if abs(x[0]) >= 0.02 or abs(x[-1]) >= 0.02:
                problems.append(f"{where}: edge not at zero ({x[0]:.4f}, {x[-1]:.4f})")
            if not rms > 0.01:
                problems.append(f"{where}: rms {rms:.4f} too quiet / silent")
            print(f"{name:<9}{pitch:>6.1f}{sec:>7.3f}{peak:>7.3f}{rms:>7.3f}{dom:>9.0f}")
            fname = f"{name}.wav" if pitch == 1.0 else f"{name}_p1.3.wav"
            write_wav(os.path.join(outdir, fname), x)
    if problems:
        print("\nFAILED:")
        for p in problems:
            print("  " + p)
        return 1
    print(f"\nall {len(SFX)} sounds passed at pitch 1.0 and 1.3; WAVs in {outdir}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(_selftest())
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/skeleton.py -->
``````python
"""The template stick figure: a small 3D rig described by named numeric parameters, solved to screen joints.

AXES: the figure has forward (f), down (y) and sideways (z) axes. `yaw` turns it about its vertical axis and the
result is projected flat onto the screen, so one figure can face right, turn to the camera, show its back, walk away.
  yaw   0 = profile facing right, 90 = facing the camera, 180 = profile facing left, 270 (or -90) = back to camera.
        yaw tweens like any number, so 0 -> 180 passes through the front, 0 -> -180 passes through the back.
        ("face":"left"/"right" in a key is shorthand for yaw 180/0.)
ANGLES in degrees. A limb angle of 0 = hanging down, 90 = pointing forward, 180 = straight up, -90 = backward.
  arm_r / arm_l = [swing, elbow_bend, out]  swing forward/back, bend folds the forearm forward, out lifts it sideways
  leg_r / leg_l = [swing, knee_bend, out]   bend folds the shin backward           (r = the figure's right side)
  lean = torso tilt (+ forward), head = extra head tilt, rot = whole body pitch about the hip (+ forward)
  x = hip x, ground = floor y (the hip height is solved so the lowest foot touches it), lift = height of a jump
Reach (IK, in screen space, so it works from any yaw), blended by a weight so it can be keyframed on and off:
  hand_r_at / hand_l_at / foot_r_at / foot_l_at = [x, y] world point, or "@name" / "@actor.anchor"
  hand_r_rel / ... = [dx, dy] from the hip, forward being the way the figure faces on screen
  weights ..._w = 0..1, set automatically to 1 when you give a target and to 0 when you write null
  grip_rel = [dx, dy] both hands on one point (two-handed tool grip), grip_w its weight.
"""
import math

UPPER_ARM, FORE_ARM = 33.0, 37.0
THIGH, SHIN = 43.0, 43.0
TORSO, HEAD_OFF, SHOULDER_DROP = 58.0, 19.0, 8.0
SHOULDER_W, HIP_W = 11.0, 7.0

DEFAULTS = dict(
    x=288.0, ground=900.0, lift=0.0, yaw=0.0, rot=0.0, lean=0.0, head=0.0, tint=None,
    arm_r=[16.0, 14.0, 10.0], arm_l=[-12.0, 14.0, 10.0], leg_r=[7.0, 0.0, 4.0], leg_l=[-7.0, 0.0, 4.0],
    hand_r_at=None, hand_l_at=None, foot_r_at=None, foot_l_at=None,
    hand_r_rel=[30.0, -40.0], hand_l_rel=[30.0, -40.0], foot_r_rel=[10.0, 85.0], foot_l_rel=[-10.0, 85.0],
    hand_r_w=0.0, hand_l_w=0.0, foot_r_w=0.0, foot_l_w=0.0,
    grip_rel=[60.0, -40.0], grip_w=0.0,
    expr="calm", talk=False, talk_seed=0, blink=True, depth_shade=True,
    prop=None, prop_angle=0.0, prop_hand="auto", prop_l=None, prop_l_angle=0.0,
    anim=None, anim_speed=1.0, anim_blend=0.15, z=10, shadow=True,
)
LIMB_DEFAULT_OUT = dict(arm_r=10.0, arm_l=10.0, leg_r=4.0, leg_l=4.0)
LIMBS = tuple(LIMB_DEFAULT_OUT)


def pad_limb(name, v):
    v = list(v)
    while len(v) < 3:
        v.append(LIMB_DEFAULT_OUT[name] if len(v) == 2 else 0.0)
    return v


def _dir(swing, out, side, flip=False):
    a = math.radians(swing)
    o = math.radians(out)
    return (math.sin(a) * math.cos(o), math.cos(a) * math.cos(o), side * math.sin(o))


def ik2(root, target, l1, l2, score):
    """two-bone IK in screen space. `score(point)` picks between the two bend directions (higher wins)."""
    rx, ry = root
    dx, dy = target[0] - rx, target[1] - ry
    d = max(math.hypot(dx, dy), 1e-3)
    reach = (l1 + l2) * 0.999
    if d > reach:                       # cartoon stretch
        k = d / reach
        l1, l2 = l1 * k, l2 * k
    a = (l1 * l1 - l2 * l2 + d * d) / (2 * d)
    h = math.sqrt(max(l1 * l1 - a * a, 0.0))
    ux, uy = dx / d, dy / d
    px, py = rx + ux * a, ry + uy * a
    c1 = (px - uy * h, py + ux * h)
    c2 = (px + uy * h, py - ux * h)
    return c1 if score(c1) >= score(c2) else c2


def _lerp2(a, b, w):
    return (a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * w, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * w)


def solve(p, resolve):
    """params -> (pose dict for figure3d.draw_figure, anchors). resolve(ref) -> (x, y) | None."""
    th = math.radians(p["yaw"])
    cth, sth = math.cos(th), math.sin(th)
    fs = 1.0 if cth >= 0 else -1.0                       # which way "forward" points on screen
    rot = math.radians(p["rot"])
    cr, sr = math.cos(rot), math.sin(rot)

    def pitch(pt):                                       # body pitch about the hip (f, y plane)
        return (pt[0] * cr - pt[1] * sr, pt[0] * sr + pt[1] * cr, pt[2])

    la = math.radians(p["lean"])
    neck = (TORSO * math.sin(la), -TORSO * math.cos(la), 0.0)
    ha = math.radians(p["lean"] + p["head"])
    head = (neck[0] + HEAD_OFF * math.sin(ha), neck[1] - HEAD_OFF * math.cos(ha), 0.0)
    sh = {"r": (neck[0], neck[1] + SHOULDER_DROP, -SHOULDER_W), "l": (neck[0], neck[1] + SHOULDER_DROP, SHOULDER_W)}
    hp = {"r": (0.0, 0.0, -HIP_W), "l": (0.0, 0.0, HIP_W)}

    def chain(root, ang, l1, l2, side, flip):
        sw, bend, out = ang
        d1 = _dir(sw, out, side)
        k = tuple(root[i] + l1 * d1[i] for i in range(3))
        d2 = _dir(sw - bend if flip else sw + bend, out, side)
        e = tuple(k[i] + l2 * d2[i] for i in range(3))
        return k, e

    arms = {s: chain(sh[s], p["arm_" + s], UPPER_ARM, FORE_ARM, -1.0 if s == "r" else 1.0, False) for s in "rl"}
    legs = {s: chain(hp[s], p["leg_" + s], THIGH, SHIN, -1.0 if s == "r" else 1.0, True) for s in "rl"}
    neck, head = pitch(neck), pitch(head)
    sh = {s: pitch(v) for s, v in sh.items()}
    arms = {s: (pitch(k), pitch(e)) for s, (k, e) in arms.items()}
    legs = {s: (pitch(k), pitch(e)) for s, (k, e) in legs.items()}

    def sx(pt):                                          # local (f, y, z) -> screen offset from hip
        return (pt[0] * cth + pt[2] * sth, pt[1])

    def depth(pt):                                       # smaller = nearer the camera
        return -pt[0] * sth + pt[2] * cth

    lows = []
    for s in "rl":
        dy = legs[s][1][1]
        w = p["foot_" + s + "_w"]
        if w > 0 and p["foot_" + s + "_at"] is None:
            dy = dy + (p["foot_" + s + "_rel"][1] - dy) * w
        lows.append(dy)
    hip = (p["x"], p["ground"] - p["lift"] - max(lows))

    def W(pt):
        o = sx(pt)
        return (hip[0] + o[0], hip[1] + o[1])

    n_, h_ = W(neck), W(head)
    sh_w = {s: W(sh[s]) for s in "rl"}
    elbows = {s: W(arms[s][0]) for s in "rl"}
    hands = {s: W(arms[s][1]) for s in "rl"}
    knees = {s: W(legs[s][0]) for s in "rl"}
    feet = {s: W(legs[s][1]) for s in "rl"}
    # depth per part (for draw order)
    dep = {}
    for s in "rl":
        dep["arm_" + s] = (depth(sh[s]) + depth(arms[s][0]) + depth(arms[s][1])) / 3.0
        dep["leg_" + s] = (depth(hp[s]) + depth(legs[s][0]) + depth(legs[s][1])) / 3.0
    dep["torso"] = depth(neck) * 0.5
    dep["head"] = depth(head)

    def target(at, rel):
        if at is not None:
            if isinstance(at, str):
                return resolve(at)
            return (float(at[0]), float(at[1]))
        return (hip[0] + rel[0] * cth, hip[1] + rel[1])

    gw = p.get("grip_w", 0.0)
    for s in "rl":
        w = p["hand_" + s + "_w"]
        tgt = target(p["hand_" + s + "_at"], p["hand_" + s + "_rel"]) if w > 0 else None
        if gw > 0:
            g = (hip[0] + p["grip_rel"][0] * cth, hip[1] + p["grip_rel"][1])
            tgt, w = (g if tgt is None or gw >= w else _lerp2(tgt, g, gw / max(gw + w, 1e-9))), max(w, gw)
        if tgt is not None and w > 0:
            side = -1.0 if s == "r" else 1.0
            pdx = -0.5 * cth + side * sth
            shp = sh_w[s]
            el = ik2(shp, tgt, UPPER_ARM, FORE_ARM, lambda c: c[1] + pdx * (c[0] - shp[0]))
            elbows[s] = _lerp2(elbows[s], el, w)
            hands[s] = _lerp2(hands[s], tgt, w)
    for s in "rl":
        w = p["foot_" + s + "_w"]
        if w > 0:
            tgt = target(p["foot_" + s + "_at"], p["foot_" + s + "_rel"])
            if tgt is not None:
                kn = ik2(hip, tgt, THIGH, SHIN, lambda c: cth * (c[0] - hip[0]))
                knees[s] = _lerp2(knees[s], kn, w)
                feet[s] = _lerp2(feet[s], tgt, w)

    grip = _lerp2(hands["r"], hands["l"], 0.5) if gw >= 0.5 else hands["r"]
    pose = dict(head=h_, neck=n_, hip=hip, shoulders=[sh_w["r"], sh_w["l"]], hands=[hands["r"], hands["l"]],
                feet=[feet["r"], feet["l"]], elbows=[elbows["r"], elbows["l"]], knees=[knees["r"], knees["l"]],
                depth=dep, yaw=p["yaw"], head_tilt=p["lean"] + p["head"], tint=p.get("tint"))
    anchors = dict(head=h_, neck=n_, hip=hip, hand_r=hands["r"], hand_l=hands["l"], grip=grip, foot_r=feet["r"],
                   foot_l=feet["l"], elbow_r=elbows["r"], elbow_l=elbows["l"], knee_r=knees["r"], knee_l=knees["l"],
                   shoulder_r=sh_w["r"], shoulder_l=sh_w["l"], cos_yaw=cth)
    return pose, anchors
``````


<!-- FILE stick/stickreel/track.py -->
``````python
"""Keyframe tracks. A thing (actor, object, camera) has static props plus `keys`:

    {"t": 1.5, "x": 300, "ease": "out"}      # values arrive at t; unspecified props keep their last value

Values can be numbers, lists of numbers, "#rrggbb" colours (blended), or anything else (strings, null, true: they
switch when the key time is reached). `ease` is the curve of the segment that ENDS at this key; `eases` can set a
different curve per property: {"eases": {"x": "linear"}}.
"""
from .ease import ease


class SceneError(ValueError):
    pass


def is_color(v):
    return isinstance(v, str) and len(v) in (4, 7, 9) and v.startswith("#")


def parse_color(v):
    h = v[1:]
    if len(h) == 3:
        h = "".join(c * 2 for c in h)
    a = 255 if len(h) == 6 else int(h[6:8], 16)
    return (int(h[0:2], 16), int(h[2:4], 16), int(h[4:6], 16), a)


def to_hex(c):
    return "#%02x%02x%02x%02x" % tuple(max(0, min(255, int(round(x)))) for x in c)


def blend(a, b, u):
    """value between a and b at 0..1, or None if they do not blend (caller then steps)."""
    if isinstance(a, bool) or isinstance(b, bool):
        return None
    if isinstance(a, (int, float)) and isinstance(b, (int, float)):
        return a + (b - a) * u
    if isinstance(a, (list, tuple)) and isinstance(b, (list, tuple)) and len(a) == len(b):
        out = []
        for x, y in zip(a, b):
            v = blend(x, y, u)
            if v is None:
                return None
            out.append(v)
        return out
    if is_color(a) and is_color(b):
        ca, cb = parse_color(a), parse_color(b)
        return to_hex([x + (y - x) * u for x, y in zip(ca, cb)])
    return None


class Track:
    """all props of one thing over time."""

    def __init__(self, static, keys, where="?", defaults=None):
        self.static = dict(static)
        self.keys = sorted(keys, key=lambda k: k["t"])
        self.where = where
        for k in self.keys:
            if "t" not in k or not isinstance(k["t"], (int, float)):
                raise SceneError(f"{where}: every key needs a numeric 't' (seconds): {k}")
        self.props = set(self.static)
        for k in self.keys:
            self.props |= {p for p in k if p not in ("t", "ease", "eases")}
        # per-prop timeline: [(t, value, ease)]
        self.lines = {p: [] for p in self.props}
        for k in self.keys:
            for p, v in k.items():
                if p in ("t", "ease", "eases"):
                    continue
                e = (k.get("eases") or {}).get(p, k.get("ease", "inout"))
                if e not in __import__("stickreel.ease", fromlist=["EASES"]).EASES:
                    raise SceneError(f"{where}: unknown ease '{e}' at t={k['t']}. Known: "
                                     + ", ".join(__import__("stickreel.ease", fromlist=["EASES"]).EASES))
                self.lines[p].append((k["t"], v, e))
        # A property first mentioned in a later key starts from its default/static value at the key BEFORE it, so
        # it neither holds its final value backwards in time nor creeps from t=0.
        defaults = defaults or {}
        for p, ln in self.lines.items():
            if not ln:
                continue
            first_t = ln[0][0]
            earlier = [k["t"] for k in self.keys if k["t"] < first_t]
            have = p in self.static or p in defaults
            base = self.static.get(p, defaults.get(p))
            if earlier and have:
                ln.insert(0, (earlier[-1], base, "linear"))

    def get(self, prop, t, default=None):
        ln = self.lines.get(prop)
        if not ln:
            return self.static.get(prop, default)
        if t <= ln[0][0]:
            return ln[0][1] if not (self.static.get(prop) is not None and False) else self.static[prop]
        if t >= ln[-1][0]:
            return ln[-1][1]
        # find segment
        lo, hi = 0, len(ln) - 1
        while hi - lo > 1:
            mid = (lo + hi) // 2
            if ln[mid][0] <= t:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        (t0, v0, _), (t1, v1, e1) = ln[lo], ln[hi]
        u = (t - t0) / max(t1 - t0, 1e-9)
        b = blend(v0, v1, ease(e1, u))
        if b is None:
            return v0 if ease("step", u) < 1 else v1
        return b

    def times(self, prop):
        return [x[0] for x in self.lines.get(prop, [])]
``````


<!-- FILE stick/tests/test_audio.py -->
``````python
import unittest

import numpy as np

from stickreel import audio as A
from stickreel.render import frame_rgb
from stickreel.scene import Scene
from stickreel.track import SceneError

BASE = {"canvas": {"w": 120, "h": 200, "scale": 1, "duration": 3, "fps": 30},
        "actors": {"a": {"x": 20, "ground": 150, "keys": [{"t": 0, "anim": "walk"}, {"t": 2, "x": 420, "ease": "linear"}]}}}


def scene(audio, **extra):
    d = dict(BASE, audio=audio)
    d.update(extra)
    return Scene(d)


class AudioTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_unknown_sfx_suggests(self):
        with self.assertRaises(SceneError) as c:
            scene({"cues": [{"sfx": "thnk", "t": 0.5}]})
        self.assertIn("thunk", str(c.exception))

    def test_cue_needs_t_or_times(self):
        with self.assertRaises(SceneError):
            scene({"cues": [{"sfx": "pop"}]})

    def test_mix_places_sound_and_limits_peak(self):
        s = scene({"cues": [{"sfx": "boom", "t": 1.0, "gain": 3.0}]})
        m = A.mix(s)
        self.assertEqual(m.shape, (int(3 * 44100), 2))
        self.assertLessEqual(float(np.abs(m).max()), 0.98 + 1e-6)
        self.assertLess(float(np.abs(m[:int(0.95 * 44100)]).max()), 1e-6)       # silent before the cue
        self.assertGreater(float(np.abs(m[int(1.0 * 44100):int(1.2 * 44100)]).max()), 0.1)

    def test_range_times_and_footsteps(self):
        s = scene({"cues": [{"sfx": "tick", "times": {"from": 0.5, "to": 0.6, "every": 0.05}}, {"sfx": "step", "times": "a.step"}]})
        self.assertEqual(len(s.times({"from": 0.5, "to": 0.6, "every": 0.05})), 3)
        steps = s.times("a.step")
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(steps), 3)
        self.assertTrue(all(b > a for a, b in zip(steps, steps[1:])))
        A.mix(s)

    def test_partial_mix_window(self):
        s = scene({"cues": [{"sfx": "ding", "t": 2.0}]})
        m = A.mix(s, 1.5, 2.5)
        self.assertEqual(len(m), 44100)
        self.assertGreater(float(np.abs(m[int(0.5 * 44100):]).max()), 0.1)


class PolishTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_out_size_and_post(self):
        s = Scene(dict(BASE, canvas=dict(BASE["canvas"], out=[240, 400], post={"vignette": 0.2, "grain": 0.01})))
        self.assertEqual(frame_rgb(s, 3).size, (240, 400))

    def test_null_expr_is_allowed_and_blink_runs(self):
        Scene(dict(BASE, actors={"a": {"keys": [{"t": 0, "expr": None}]}}))
        s = Scene(dict(BASE, actors={"a": {"x": 60, "ground": 150, "keys": [{"t": 0, "expr": "happy", "yaw": 90}]}}))
        frame_rgb(s, 0)

    def test_camera_shake_moves_camera_only_near_events(self):
        s = Scene(dict(BASE, camera={"shake": {"times": [1.0], "amp": 4, "dur": 0.2}}))
        self.assertEqual(s.cam_at(0.5)["cx"], 288.0)
        self.assertNotEqual(s.cam_at(1.05)["cx"], 288.0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
``````


<!-- FILE stick/tests/test_engine.py -->
``````python
"""Engine tests for stickreel: tracks, scene validation, hit events, grounding, turnaround, walk distance,
phone video, and one full frame render. Run from /home/user/LegoReel/stick:

    python3 -m unittest discover tests
"""
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest

import numpy as np

from stickreel.scene import Scene
from stickreel.track import Track, SceneError, parse_color
from stickreel.poses import LOOPS
from stickreel import items
from stickreel.render import render_frame

CANVAS = dict(w=200, h=300, fps=30, duration=3.0, scale=1.0, blur=1)


def make_scene(**parts):
    data = dict(canvas=dict(CANVAS))
    data.update(parts)
    return Scene(data, base_dir=tempfile.gettempdir())


def actor_scene(keys, **static):
    spec = dict(static)
    spec["keys"] = keys
    return make_scene(actors=dict(a=spec))


def img_bytes(im):
    return np.asarray(im.convert("RGBA"), dtype=np.int16)


class TrackTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_values_hold_from_previous_key(self):
        tr = Track({}, [dict(t=0, x=1.0, y=5.0, ease="linear"), dict(t=1, x=2.0, ease="linear")])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(tr.get("x", 0.5), 1.5)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(tr.get("y", 0.5), 5.0)   # y not set again: holds
        self.assertAlmostEqual(tr.get("y", 3.0), 5.0)

    def test_ease_applies_to_segment_ending_at_key(self):
        # 'in' on the key at t=1 shapes the 0 -> 1 segment: 100 * 0.5**3
        tr = Track({}, [dict(t=0, x=0.0), dict(t=1, x=100.0, ease="in")])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(tr.get("x", 0.5), 12.5, places=6)
        # 'in' on the key at t=0 has no segment ending there, so the 0 -> 1 segment keeps its own ease ('inout')
        tr2 = Track({}, [dict(t=0, x=0.0, ease="in"), dict(t=1, x=100.0)])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(tr2.get("x", 0.5), 50.0, places=6)

    def test_colours_blend(self):
        tr = Track({}, [dict(t=0, fill="#000000"), dict(t=1, fill="#ffffff", ease="linear")])
        c = parse_color(tr.get("fill", 0.5))
        for ch in c[:3]:
            self.assertLessEqual(abs(ch - 127.5), 1.0)
        self.assertEqual(parse_color(tr.get("fill", 0.0))[:3], (0, 0, 0))
        self.assertEqual(parse_color(tr.get("fill", 1.0))[:3], (255, 255, 255))

    def test_strings_step_at_key_time(self):
        tr = Track({}, [dict(t=0, text="a"), dict(t=1, text="b")])
        self.assertEqual(tr.get("text", 0.5), "a")
        self.assertEqual(tr.get("text", 0.999), "a")
        self.assertEqual(tr.get("text", 1.0), "b")
        self.assertEqual(tr.get("text", 2.0), "b")


class SceneErrorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def assertSceneError(self, fn, *needles):
        with self.assertRaises(SceneError) as cm:
            fn()
        for n in needles:
            self.assertIn(n, str(cm.exception))
        return str(cm.exception)

    def test_misspelled_actor_property_suggests_fix(self):
        msg = self.assertSceneError(lambda: make_scene(actors=dict(a=dict(leen=1.0))), "Did you mean")
        self.assertIn("lean", msg)

    def test_misspelled_key_property_suggests_fix(self):
        self.assertSceneError(lambda: actor_scene([dict(t=0, leen=1.0)]), "Did you mean")

    def test_unknown_pose(self):
        self.assertSceneError(lambda: actor_scene([dict(t=0, pose="stnad")]), "unknown pose")

    def test_unknown_anim(self):
        self.assertSceneError(lambda: actor_scene([dict(t=0, anim="chpo")]), "unknown anim")

    def test_unknown_object_kind(self):
        self.assertSceneError(lambda: make_scene(objects=dict(o=dict(kind="blob"))), "unknown kind")

    def test_unknown_expr_on_keyed_expression(self):
        self.assertSceneError(lambda: actor_scene([dict(t=0, expr="happyy")]), "unknown expr")


class HitTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.scene = actor_scene([dict(t=0, anim="chop", hits=[0.5, 1.2, 1.9])])
        self.actor = self.scene.actors["a"]
        self.loop = self.scene.loops["chop"]
        self.seg = self.actor.segs[0]

    def test_hit_times(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.actor.hit_times(), [0.5, 1.2, 1.9])

    def test_hit_phase_at_hits(self):
        want = 4 / 22
        for t in (0.5, 1.2):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(self.actor.phase(self.loop, self.seg, t, 1.0), want, delta=1e-6)


class GroundingTests(unittest.TestCase):
    GROUND = 250.0

    def test_lowest_foot_touches_ground(self):
        for pose in ("stand", "crouch", "sit", "hands_up"):
            for yaw in (0, 90, 180, 270):
                with self.subTest(pose=pose, yaw=yaw):
                    sc = actor_scene([dict(t=0, pose=pose, yaw=float(yaw))], x=100.0, ground=self.GROUND, lift=0.0)
                    pose_d, _, _ = sc.actors["a"].solve(0.0)
                    lowest = max(f[1] for f in pose_d["feet"])
                    self.assertAlmostEqual(lowest, self.GROUND, delta=0.01)


class TurnaroundTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def render_at_yaw(self, yaw):
        sc = actor_scene([dict(t=0, pose="stand", yaw=float(yaw))], x=100.0, ground=250.0)
        return render_frame(sc, 0.0)

    @staticmethod
    def bbox(im):
        a = np.asarray(im.convert("RGBA"))[..., :3].sum(axis=2)
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(a)
        return xs.min(), ys.min(), xs.max(), ys.max()

    def test_front_and_back_differ(self):
        a = img_bytes(self.render_at_yaw(0))
        b = img_bytes(self.render_at_yaw(270))
        self.assertFalse(np.array_equal(a, b))

    def test_side_views_differ_and_silhouette_close(self):
        a = self.render_at_yaw(90)
        b = self.render_at_yaw(270)
        self.assertFalse(np.array_equal(img_bytes(a), img_bytes(b)))
        ba, bb = self.bbox(a), self.bbox(b)
        for u, v in zip(ba, bb):
            self.assertLessEqual(abs(int(u) - int(v)), 3)


class WalkDistanceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_phase_advances_by_distance_over_stride(self):
        # linear x from 100 to 400 over 1 s: 300 px per second, stride 150 px -> phase 2 per second
        sc = actor_scene([dict(t=0, anim="walk", x=100.0, ease="linear"), dict(t=1, x=400.0, ease="linear")])
        a = sc.actors["a"]
        loop, seg = LOOPS["walk"], a.segs[0]
        t1, t2 = 0.2, 0.6
        dx = a.track.get("x", t2, 0) - a.track.get("x", t1, 0)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(dx, 120.0, places=6)
        p1 = a.phase(loop, seg, t1, 1.0)
        p2 = a.phase(loop, seg, t2, 1.0)
        advance = (p2 - p1) % 1.0
        self.assertAlmostEqual(advance, (dx / loop["stride"]) % 1.0, delta=1e-3)


class PhoneVideoTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="stick_phone_")
        cls.video = os.path.join(cls.tmp, "clip.mp4")
        subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i",
                        "testsrc=size=240x480:rate=30:duration=2", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", cls.video],
                       check=True, timeout=60)
        cls.scene = Scene(dict(canvas=dict(CANVAS)), base_dir=cls.tmp)

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        shutil.rmtree(cls.tmp, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_video_frames_change_over_time(self):
        p = dict(video="clip.mp4", video_start=0.0, loop=False, screen_color="#102030")
        a = np.asarray(items.screen_image(p, 0.1, self.scene), dtype=np.int16)
        b = np.asarray(items.screen_image(p, 1.5, self.scene), dtype=np.int16)
        self.assertEqual(a.shape, (items.SCREEN_H, items.SCREEN_W, 4))
        self.assertFalse(np.array_equal(a, b))

    def test_missing_video_falls_back_to_screen_colour(self):
        p = dict(video=os.path.join(self.tmp, "nope.mp4"), screen_color="#102030")
        im = items.screen_image(p, 0.5, self.scene)
        self.assertEqual(im.size, (items.SCREEN_W, items.SCREEN_H))
        self.assertEqual(im.getpixel((3, 3)), (16, 32, 48, 255))


class FullFrameTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_render_frame_with_pick_burst_and_pulse(self):
        sc = make_scene(
            objects=dict(
                pk=dict(kind="pick", x=0, y=0, angle=-45, len=40),
                burst=dict(kind="burst", times="a.hit", at=[100, 150], n=8, seed=3, life=0.5),
                pulse=dict(kind="pulse", times="a.hit", at=[100, 150], r=[30, 12], dur=0.2),
            ),
            actors=dict(a=dict(
                x=100.0, ground=250.0, prop="pk", prop_hand="r", expr="happy",
                keys=[dict(t=0, anim="chop", hits=[0.5, 1.2, 1.9])],
            )),
        )
        im = render_frame(sc, 0.6)
        self.assertEqual(im.size, (CANVAS["w"], CANVAS["h"]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
``````
